"Quiet! You shouldn’t speak carelessly, making it sound like Eldest Senior Sister is over two thousand years old!"

Occasionally descending the mountain, Lu Yang heard the discussions of the junior brothers and sisters and sighed at how young they were. He remembered the times when he was just as ignorant of danger until Eldest Senior Sister threw him to the border, where he quickly learned to behave.

"Speaking of which, there was never an official statement regarding the relationship between Eldest Senior Sister and the position of Sect Leader."

Lu Yang recalled his experiences since joining Seek Dao Sect. The matter of Eldest Senior Sister acting as the Sect Leader was always passed down by word of mouth, so it was no wonder the new disciples were unaware of the facts.

Daoist Buyu looked at the congratulatory letters fluttering in like snowflakes, feeling quite angry.

"Did you all have Zijin write these congratulatory letters? They all sound so sarcastic!"

What consoled Daoist Buyu was that there were also a few letters that were sincerely meant.

Such as those from Demon City, from Western Paradise Temple, from the East Sea Dragon Clan.

The only problem was that they began their messages differently from others, all uniformly starting with: Lu Yang, his master, and so on...

"This must be what people mean by ’the disciple’s honor reflects upon the master.’"

Outside Seek Dao Sect, several Transcendance Tribulation Stage cultivators gathered to discuss heatedly, their indignation causing them to slap their thighs in frustration.

"Didn’t Old Buyu just tell us he was going to visit Lu Yang? How has he become the Sect Leader now?"

"Damn it, we’ve been tricked by that old sly fox!"

"Always so treacherous and cunning!"

"I knew we couldn’t believe a word that old thief said!"

After much discussion, they reluctantly came to a conclusion that there was nothing they could do, they couldn’t very well squat at the gates of Seek Dao Sect waiting for Old Buyu, who might not come out for a whole year and a half.

At the top floor of Scripture Pavilion, the minimum requirement to access this level was to be in the Body Integration Realm.

Now that Lu Yang had surpassed this requirement, he easily entered the highest floor.

As Lu Yang walked on the top floor, any scripture he casually picked from the shelves was at least of the Body Integration Realm, even the Transcendance Tribulation Stage techniques were not in short supply.

In order to compose the "Mind Seeing Method", Tribulation Crossing Chapter, these references were indispensable.

"But why do these techniques look so ancient?"

Lu Yang noticed that some techniques were noticeably different in color from the rest, extremely old, it was unclear how long they had been there.

"Ah, I see, these techniques were provided by the prisoners of Captive Peak."

Lu Yang realized, with each capture of a prisoner from Captive Peak, a treasure location was obtained, where these prisoners had collected these techniques.

Considering there were more than a hundred prisoners from the Body Integration Realm, it was clear that these old techniques had been acquired from them.

Presumably, Elder Brother Dai had led people to scour these treasure locations, keeping the valuable Heaven and Earth Treasures in the treasury of Sky Gate Mountain and placing the techniques on the top floor of Scripture Pavilion.

Previously, Lu Yang did not have the privilege to access the top floor, unaware of these matters; now seeing them, he figured it out quite simply.

"Mountain and Sea Immortal Technique", "Sealing God Technique"?

Two techniques were particularly conspicuous, their names indicating they were anything but ordinary.

With Lu Yang’s level of insight, he wasn’t fully capable of comprehending the mysteries of Immortal Level Techniques, but he could grasp the general idea.

"Are these Guan Shanhai and The Controller’s techniques?"

"The two of them sure gave a thorough account," Lu Yang chuckled. These two’s techniques, if released into the world, would cause untold chaos, for they directly pointed towards becoming an Immortal, and with careful study, one could find the opportunity to form an outline of Dao Fruit.

Of course, whether one could condense an outline of Dao Fruit depended on one’s comprehension.

"One embodies the universe within the heart, evolving the world and the other uses the Power of Faith to seal the gods and become the source of belief, both quite imaginative." Fairy Eternity marveled, finding the approaches of these two techniques unique, never having appeared even in Ancient Times.

Although Guan Shanhai and The Controller were prisoners, they were also Immortals, protagonists who had dominated their respective eras for a hundred thousand years.

Those who cultivated to Immortal status were without exception geniuses, each with their own unique skills.

Their luck was just poor, having encountered Yun Zhi.

Hearing Little Yang mutter as he scrutinized the techniques, Fairy Eternity was quite astonished, "Isn’t it because their fates weren’t as strong as yours, and you imprisoned them in Captive Peak?"

Lu Yang could only roughly understand Immortal Level techniques, but he could leisurely read Tribulation-Passing Level techniques and comprehend them with ease.

Something other Transcendance Stage cultivators could not accomplish, not even Daoist Buyu.

Lu Yang’s level of insight had already surpassed that of the Transcendance Stage.

If insight had a level, Lu Yang’s would be of the Half-Immortal level.

"These techniques can only serve as reference, yet they’re still not enough," Lu Yang put down a technique left by Blood Demon Venerable, shaking his head slightly, feeling something was missing.

"That’s right, these techniques only discuss the methods of cultivation at the Transcendance Stage, but there’s nothing about the outline of Dao Fruit."

Half-Immortal techniques were also available; if Lu Yang wished, Ao Ling, Jiang Lianyi, and others would also be willing to share their respective techniques with him.

But intuition told Lu Yang that none of these were what he needed.

He had a vague sense that he had grasped a shred of inspiration regarding the outline of Dao Fruit, yet couldn’t quite articulate what it was.

"Does it have something to do with my cultivation technique?"

"What’s this... ’Sword Opens Heaven’s Gate’?"

"My master’s ultimate move? Perfect for a change of pace and some relaxation."

Lu Yang set aside his concerns about cultivation techniques and eagerly began to learn.

Five days later, Lu Yang returned Sword Opens Heaven’s Gate to its original place.

"I’ve learned it."

Chapter 1211: 1210 Qilin’er

After relaxing from mastering the Sword Opens Heaven’s Gate, Lu Yang continued to study cultivation techniques.

The matter of cultivation techniques was related to his future path in cultivating, so, even though Eldest Senior Sister helped him revise the techniques to guarantee a base level, Lu Yang dared not slack off.

Months passed by, during which Lu Yang had stayed in the Scripture Pavilion and had never left the top floor.

He had memorized all the techniques on the top floor and had analyzed the strengths and weaknesses of others’ techniques.

"Guan Shanhai’s ’Mountain and Sea Immortal Technique’ can evolve the heaven and earth, The Controller’s ’Sealing God Technique’ can become the source of faith; whether it’s an immortal level technique or a tribulation crossing level technique, each inherently possesses a significant effect."

"The sole effect of the ’Mind Seeing Method’ is to persist on one’s true self, to see one’s nature clearly... Fairy, could the condition to condense the Eternity Dao Fruit be to persist in true self and to see one’s nature clearly?"

Recalling Sister Mengmeng and the Fairy’s behavior, Lu Yang could easily identify a common trait between them.

"Have you always said I could condense the Eternity Dao Fruit because of this reason?"

Floating around Lu Yang, Immortal Fairy chuckled upon hearing his words: "Indeed, it’s one of the reasons."

"The most fundamental condition for the Eternity Dao Fruit is that the heart must remain immortal, unaffected by the passage of years, without major personality changes or deviating from one’s original intention."

"Are there many such people?" Lu Yang could not recall seeing anyone like that.

Yawning, Immortal Fairy continued: "There are many, it’s just that you have cultivated too quickly and never had the chance to meet such people. In ancient times, Immortal here had seen countless such people, living longer and pursuing power, becoming less and less human."

"You don’t have to worry about this when cultivating the Clear Mind Technique."

Lu Yang suddenly understood; Eldest Senior Sister initially made him cultivate the Clear Mind Technique likely for this purpose.

Understanding the true meaning of the Clear Mind Technique, Lu Yang had new ideas for continuing the Tribulation Crossing Chapter.

...

"Eldest Senior Sister, this is the new technique I’ve written."

Lu Yang took out the recently written technique, his months of accumulation and enlightenment all focused within this text.

Remembering the times when he had shown his techniques to Eldest Senior Sister, Lu Yang felt nervous, neither able to sit nor stand, fearing Eldest Senior Sister might say it was insufficient.

It was not until he noticed Eldest Senior Sister’s slight smile that his heart trembled.

"Well written."

"Eldest Senior Sister, has my technique passed?"

Yun Zhi did not directly answer his question but returned the technique to Lu Yang: "Since you reached the Transcendance Tribulation Stage, each thing you do may become an opportunity to form an outline of your Dao Fruit; and your technique is particularly significant for the Dao Fruit Outline."

"If I intervene too much, it might actually harm you; after the Transcendance Tribulation Stage, you must ultimately rely on yourself"

Hearing Eldest Senior Sister say this, Lu Yang did not feel overjoyed but felt a melancholic sense of loss.

The path Eldest Senior Sister had laid out for him ended here; he could grow without her protection, which also meant that she would no longer provide help in his future cultivation.

Boom—

A sudden clap of thunder interrupted Lu Yang’s sadness.

"Is someone going to Pass Tribulation?"

Following the sound of the thunder, Lu Yang saw it originated from Old Meng’s location.

"That’s right, it’s time for Old Meng to break through."

With the lifting of the Sun seal and the restoration of the Sun and Lunar Power to the standards of the Ancient Times, Meng Jingzhou’s opportunities came one after another, surpassing the hardships of living.

Before Lu Yang had entered the Scripture Pavilion, he heard Old Meng had gone somewhere to experience the mortal trials.

Since it drew the thunder, it seemed the trials were highly successful.

Thunder, manifesting the might of heaven, repeatedly struck, and Meng Jingzhou’s figure appeared as tiny as an insect before the thunder, his body lacerated yet resiliently persevering.

"It’s strange, why does Old Meng have only eighty-one thunders?"

Lu Yang thought about himself facing one hundred sixty-two thunders; Old Meng should also have drawn at least one hundred sixty thunders.

"Could it really be that Senior Yingtian Immortal is targeting me?"

Like Meng Jingzhou’s tribulation, the Seek Dao Sect’s disciples also kept their eyes on Meng Jingzhou on the mountaintop.

Thunder Tribulation and Heart Tribulation had ended, and heavenly nectar descended to nourish Meng Jingzhou’s body.

Meng Jingzhou clenched his fist slightly, feeling the explosive power within his body, and he let out an excited long howl.

He had reached the Transcendance Tribulation Stage as well.

Once he condensed the Dao Fruit Outline and then the complete Dao Fruit to become an immortal, he would be liberated!

Becoming an immortal was within reach, how could he not be excited!

"Hahaha, excellent, excellent, the Meng Family has a successor!" Before anyone could celebrate, Meng Junzi unexpectedly showed up at Seek Dao Sect.

As the most outstanding talent of the Meng Family, Meng Jingzhou’s crucial event of Transcendance could not be missed by Meng Junzi.

Lu Yang noticed that Eldest Senior Sister’s expression was calm, not surprised by Meng Junzi’s arrival; he thought that Meng Junzi’s visit to Seek Dao Sect must have been informed to Eldest Senior Sister in advance.

"Great Ancestor." Meng Jingzhou got dressed before paying his respects. Eighty-one bolts of Thunder Tribulation would reduce any material to ashes.

"Truly worthy of being our Meng Family’s Qilin’er!" Meng Junzi had never been as happy as he was today.

No, defeating Jiang Pingan before the founding of the nation was equally thrilling.

"Great Ancestor, calling me Qilin’er, isn’t it a bit..." Meng Jingzhou carefully said, although Qilin’er sounded complimentary, it seemed that in the heavens and earth there was only one Qilin, the Qilin Immortal.

Wouldn’t that make him the son of the Qilin Immortal?

"That’s also true, never mind, it’s not important. Let me take you to meet a few old friends."

Meng Junzi, without further ado, dragged Meng Jingzhou along and headed straight for the Imperial City.

Recently, the Great Xia Royal Family had received invigorating news; the reclusive great ancestor Jiang Pingan had once again emerged to teach the princes and princesses cultivation.

Emperor Xia decisively "sold" his children—having the great ancestor personally coach them in cultivation was a privilege to be enjoyed by his descendants.

The princes and princesses toiled and sweated profusely, enduring bitter hardships.

Jiang Pingan, carrying a hoe, pointed out their improper movements strictly.

Meng Junzi barged into the Imperial Palace with Meng Jingzhou: "Oh my, Old Jiang, you can’t teach like this."

"Look at Jingzhou, this boy. I barely intervened, and he accidentally reached the Transcendance Tribulation Stage."

Meng Junzi patted Meng Jingzhou’s back: "Come on, call him Grandpa."

"Hello, Grandpa Jiang." Meng Jingzhou obediently greeted, clearly a well-mannered child from another family.

Jiang Pingan’s eye twitched, wishing he could swing his hoe at them.

"Old Jiang, the kid is calling you Grandpa, you have to give a meeting gift, right? I won’t ask for much, just any Immortal Artifact will do."

"I think that Taishang Sword of yours looks pretty good."

"Get lost!"

Meng Junzi left the Imperial Palace, satisfied, and continued to take Meng Jingzhou to meet old friends.

The grandfather and grandson duo toured three of the Five Great Immortal Sects. Only then did Meng Jingzhou realize that the original founders of the Five Great Immortal Sects, the five Semi-Immortals, were still alive.

Meng Junzi, thinking of his old friends’ envious and jealous looks, felt as refreshed as if he had drunk a bucket of iced sour plum juice during the height of summer.

"Jingzhou, if you have any wishes, just mention them. Your great ancestor will fulfill them!"

Meng Jingzhou joyfully said, "Great Ancestor, my good brother Lu Yang is backed by Eldest Senior Sister and feels more confident when he goes out. Can you become stronger than Eldest Senior Sister? That way, I’d feel confident too."

"What did you just say?"

"Can you become stronger than Eldest Senior Sister?"

"The sentence before that."

"My good brother Lu Yang is backed by Eldest Senior Sister."

"And before that."

"Great Ancestor."

"I am not your Great Ancestor, from today onwards you are Qilin’er."

Chapter 1212: 1211: Daoist, Dare You Battle With Me?

"No no no, great ancestor, I want to change my wish," Meng Jingzhou quickly admitted his fault.

Meng Junzi quickly let Meng Jingzhou recognize his lineage, "Change to what wish?"

"I wish to defeat Lu Yang!"

Meng Junzi shook his head, "To defeat an opponent of the same level, how can you rely on someone else’s power? You must truly have the ability to defeat him by yourself."

Meng Jingzhou said with a bitter face, "Great ancestor, I naturally wouldn’t fear Old Lu in a same-level fight, but the problem is that he has a secret technique that can temporarily boost him to the peak of Pass Tribulation. When he fights me without using this kind of technique, it would mean he didn’t give his all, and my victory wouldn’t be honorable. However, if he does use this technique, I won’t be able to beat him!"

"Oh, there’s such a secret technique?"

Meng Junzi understood the concerns of his descendant, truly worthy of being the Junior Brother trained by Yun Zhi, indeed remarkable.

"It’s alright, your ancestor can lend you a portion of my strength with the Borrowing Dao Fruit, making you too reach the peak of Pass Tribulation."

Meng Jingzhou was overjoyed beyond expectation, never realizing that the Borrowing Dao Fruit had such a wonderful use.

Although Lu Yang has Eldest Senior Sister and Yun Zhi supporting him from behind, and others like Qinghe, Yun Mengmeng, and Sha Meless, a bunch of Semi-Immortals, Meng Jingzhou, thinking of having an Immortal ancestor as his support, felt a little more confident than before.

The grandparent and grandchild duo arrived at Sky Gate Mountain, "Daoist Yun Zhi, we’ve come to disturb you."

"What brings the elder here?"

"Just a small matter. It’s just that this boy Jingzhou has just made a breakthrough to the Transcendance Tribulation Stage, and it’s the right time for him to practice. It so happens that Little Lu also made a breakthrough not long ago, and it would be good for the two of them to have a contest."

"That sounds good." Yun Zhi always encouraged Lu Yang to fight more, to enhance his combat experience and promote growth.

Yun Zhi called over Lu Yang, who was still confused upon seeing Meng Junzi and Meng Jingzhou.

Previously, watching Meng Junzi’s momentum, it seemed he was set to show off Old Meng far and wide.

How did this showing off reach their Seek Dao Sect?

After listening to Eldest Senior Sister’s words, Lu Yang finally understood—it turns out Old Meng felt confident again.

"Let’s fight then, am I afraid of him?"

It wasn’t Lu Yang bragging. At the Pass Tribulation Stage, he feared no one now.

The group once again headed deep into the Seek Dao Sect. With the vastness of the sect and sparse population, they walked deeper, finding any place would likely be an uninhabited area, and a small chance a Secret Realm.

Seek Dao Sect’s history of a hundred and twenty thousand years had accumulated not a few Secret Realms, all of which could be used to train disciples, some even used as a test to join the sect.

To others, passing these Secret Realms wouldn’t be easy, but for Lu Yang and Meng Jingzhou, it was a breeze. Since they could not provide the intended training effect, Yun Zhi had never let either of them enter these realms.

Lu Yang and the others randomly selected a deserted area to be the battleground.

Lu Yang took off his wide coat and warmed up his wrists and ankles. He needed to hold back when fighting other Pass Tribulation fighters, but there was no need to be so considerate with his own people.

Meng Jingzhou was even more confident than Lu Yang, "Old Lu, go all out against me, use whatever moves you’ve got!"

"Oh, any move works. Then how about I use the Imitation Fist?"

"That works too!"

This time it was Lu Yang who was surprised—it seemed Old Meng felt he had a way to deal with him.

"This is your own words; don’t regret it later."

"No regrets, come on!"

Since Old Meng was so relaxed, Lu Yang didn’t hold back either. As the fight started, he soared up in realm, reaching the peak of Pass Tribulation with the help of Imitation Fist.

To Lu Yang’s surprise, Old Meng used the Supreme Pure Yang Dharma Image. The Dharma Image was grand and imposing; with a single stomp it could shatter a mountain, and the Green Peak Sword could only leave a shallow mark on it.

The recently advanced Meng Jingzhou definitely couldn’t have done this!

"It seems you really do have a way to boost your realm."

Lu Yang noticed Meng Junzi, who was watching the fight, and recalling Meng Junzi’s Borrowing Dao Fruit, he guessed the answer.

Meng Jingzhou grinned, "Old Lu, didn’t you have your meal? You can’t even break through my Dharma Image!"

Originally, Lu Yang was worried about not using too much force to avoid giving Old Meng a psychological shadow, but seeing that Old Meng truly had the ability, there was no need to hold back.

"Law of Heaven and Earth!"

"Devour the Sky and Swallow the Earth!"

"Universe within the Sleeve!"

Lu Yang, in one breath, cast three Great Divine Skills, transforming himself into a giant reaching the heavens and standing upon the earth.

The giant, clutching the Green Peak Sword, struck at the Dharma Aspect. The Dharma Aspect, with three heads and six arms, locked the Green Peak Sword tightly with all six palms.

Lu Yang, like a rabbit, aimed a kick at the belly of the Dharma Aspect.

The two battled fiercely, their fight so intense that, had it taken place in ancient times, the mere shockwaves could have flattened countless stars.

Seeing his junior’s outstanding performance, Meng Junzi couldn’t help but smile.

"Daoist Yun Zhi, I’m lending Meng Jingzhou a bit of power, that doesn’t count as cheating, right?" Meng Junzi was overjoyed inside; although he couldn’t defeat Yun Zhi, watching his junior defeat Yun Zhi’s cultivated Junior Brother would count as a victory for him.

"Naturally, it doesn’t count." Yun Zhi remained expressionless.

"Old Lu, that’s all you’ve got? Not impressive at all. Haven’t I told you not to be polite and to use all your strength?" Meng Jingzhou laughed triumphantly, the sensation of being steadily at the peak of the Transcendance Tribulation Stage was just too exhilarating.

Hearing this, Lu Yang revealed a meaningful smile, putting Meng Jingzhou on alert.

Is he about to use the Immortal Sword Technique? No matter, I have the ’I Am the Only One Fist’ to counter it.

The giant Lu Yang radiated rays of white light, and the leakage of power caused Meng Jingzhou’s expression to drastically change, making him curse out loud.

"Old Lu, you bastard..."

Boom—

Lu Yang self-destructed, directly blasting the Dharma Aspect to pieces and sending Meng Jingzhou flying quite a distance.

Lu Yang reconstituted his body and rode the shockwave of the self-detonation toward Meng Jingzhou, followed by yet another deafening self-explosion.

The sound surpassed any Thunder Tribulation.

Witnessing this, Meng Junzi coughed continuously, what in the world kind of move was that!

Meng Jingzhou lay on the ground, eyes devoid of life.

Lu Yang landed and crouched by the edge of the crater, concerned for the good brother lying at the center of the crater: "Old Meng, are you alright? I was holding back."

"Old Lu, you bastard, you should’ve said earlier your Tofu Venerable Celestial Form could borrow the power of the Initial Form of Immortal Dao Fruit."

"Why didn’t I see you use this trick when you fought with Yang Kongdong in the Extreme Northern Lands!"

Lu Yang scratched the back of his head: "Directly using the Initial Form of the Immortal Dao Fruit makes winning too easy. I wanted to practice a bit during that fight, so I didn’t use it."

"And why didn’t you practice when fighting me!" Meng Jingzhou roared angrily.

"Stop barking; it was you who told me to go all out."

Rushing over, Meng Junzi looked at Lu Yang with incredulity: "Semi-Immortal?"

Those two self-detonations had surpassed the realm of magic skills and Divine Abilities; they could only be the methods of the Dao Fruit Outline.

"Hehe, how about that, Little Yang is impressive, huh?" Seeing Little Yang finally willing to use the Initial Form of Immortal Dao Fruit made Fairy Eternity extremely happy.

"Immortal?" Meng Junzi realized he couldn’t see through Fairy Eternity; she must certainly be an Immortal.

"I presume Daoist friend is Little Lu’s Protector, and since I’ve promised Jingzhou to help him defeat Little Lu."

"Now that it’s come to this and Jingzhou can’t continue to fight, I will have to take his place!"

"Do you dare to fight me?"

Fairy Eternity quickly coughed twice, pretending to be very weak: "That’s not possible, as an Immortal, I was heavily wounded in the fight to defeat Ashy Bean, and I can’t move my hands."

At that moment, Yun Zhi arrived, explaining on behalf of Fairy Eternity: "That’s the person who beat you half-dead in Buddha Country."

Seeing Yun Zhi, Fairy Eternity suddenly had an idea: "Oh, that’s right, I currently lack a physical body, but I can borrow Miss Yun’s body to fight you."

Meng Junzi narrowed his eyes upon hearing this, revealing a highly dangerous look, and demanded in a deep voice, "Daoist friend, would you dare to repeat what I’ve just said?"

Of course, that couldn’t scare Fairy Eternity; she recalled Meng Junzi’s recent words: "Do you dare to fight me?"

"I do not dare!"

Chapter 1213: 1212: Are You the Immortal or Am I the Immortal?

"Just kidding, Meng Junzi isn’t stupid. The opponent was obviously ridiculously strong; how could he be headstrong enough to fight them?"

"Who cares if they’re an Immortal? Immortals are still human, and humans would recognize when they’re outmatched. It’s only natural."

"As for Jingzhou, this child, I, the old ancestor, am powerless. I apologize, you might as well become Qilin’er."

Lu Yang, with a playful grin, dug Meng Jingzhou out of a pit, and seeing that Old Meng kept giving him resentful looks, Lu Yang felt a bit embarrassed.

"Come on, if you can’t win, just admit it. Do you know what I was doing before you found me?"

"What could you possibly have been doing?" Meng Jingzhou asked irritably.

Actually, just a moment ago, Meng Junzi could have lent the Borrowing Dao Fruit Embryo to Meng Jingzhou, but the problem was that Meng Jingzhou wouldn’t know how to use it.

"I was about to add a special edition of the Cultivation Monthly, mainly to report on you reaching the Transcendance Tribulation Stage."

Excited by the news, Meng Jingzhou gripped Lu Yang’s wrist like a tiger’s claw, not even feeling the pain from the earlier blast anymore: "Good brother!"

Lu Yang grimaced in pain, having already released his Imitation Fist, the sudden grip from Old Meng was terribly painful.

"It’s perfect timing. I noticed that the citizens quite like the newspaper, and I was considering whether to change the Cultivation Monthly to the Cultivation Weekly."

Previously, the Cultivation Monthly reported major events in the Cultivation World, but Lu Yang noticed that people also enjoyed the smaller bits of information, such as the establishment of a new Third Grade small sect, alliances or feuds between two sects, and results of major cultivation contests held by different states.

Such fresh news happened every day, enough to fill a weekly.

Moreover, frequently small sects would contact Lu Yang, hoping to advertise their recruitment of disciples in the newspaper, but due to space issues, it was often impossible to fulfill their requests.

Guan Shanhai and The Controller, both full of fighting spirit, secretly competed with each other, but there was no lack of effort on their part.

"This idea is pretty good. During my recent mortal world experience, I also noticed this issue; a monthly newspaper isn’t quite sufficient for the people."

To achieve his recent breakthrough, Meng Jingzhou underwent another Red Dust Mortality, which eventually allowed him to successfully enter the Void Refinement Stage.

Interrupting their conversation, Meng Junzi said seriously, "Jingzhou, now that you’ve successfully stepped into the Transcendance Tribulation Stage, it’s time for me, your old ancestor, to tell you about the knowledge of condensing the Dao Fruit Outline."

"Condensing the Dao Fruit Outline and becoming a Semi-Immortal should not be difficult with your talent, even an Immortal has a great probability."

Meng Jingzhou cautiously raised his hand, "Old ancestor, about the knowledge of condensing the Dao Fruit Outline, may I listen to Immortal Fairy Eternity explain it?"

"Immortal Fairy Eternity?" Meng Junzi raised his eyebrows; apparently, the Immortal within Lu Yang was called Immortal Fairy Eternity.

"Although I am unaware of her identity, given that she hasn’t been seen for three hundred thousand years, she must be an Immortal from the Ancient Times."

"But don’t assume that the older the Immortal, the stronger they are."

"Whatever her identity, when it comes to the Dao Fruit, she won’t know as much as I do."

Meng Junzi knew that his combat power wasn’t the strongest among the Immortals, with Daoist Yun Zhi and Ashy Bean definitely ranking ahead of him.

But he dared to say his understanding of the Dao Fruit was unprecedented.

Meng Jingzhou felt it would still be better to listen to Immortal Fairy Eternity teach, quietly advocating his point: "But she was the one who created the concept of the Dao Fruit."

Meng Junzi: "..."

The identity of the opponent was a bit scary.

But no matter, why shouldn’t the modern be as competent as the ancient?

"Your old ancestor has an extraordinary understanding of the Dao Fruit; others’ Dao Fruits possess the Forgetfulness Attribute, but your old ancestor took a different path, condensing a Dao Fruit that does not have the Forgetfulness Attribute, as far as I know, no one else has ever condensed such a Dao Fruit."

"But her Dao Fruit doesn’t have the Forgetfulness Attribute," Meng Jingzhou continued in a low voice.

Meng Junzi silently turned around to face Fairy Eternity.

"Would you trouble Daoist to explain to this junior how to condense Dao Fruit?"

At this point, Meng Junzi paused and added, "And if possible, could I listen in as well?"

Such a painstaking task of surpassing the ancients should be left to Daoist Yun Zhi; he wouldn’t bother.

"Fairy, could you explain it to Old Meng?" Lu Yang also asked.

"Of course." Fairy Eternity had never hidden the method of condensing Dao Fruits; otherwise, there wouldn’t have been so many Semi-Immortals in Ancient Times.

Seeing this, Yun Zhi thoughtfully brought out the Emperor’s Chair, one for each, to listen to Fairy Eternity’s lecture.

Fairy Eternity was discussing content that Lu Yang had heard more than once, now merely accompanying Old Meng to listen again.

Meng Jingzhou didn’t find the theory of Dao Fruits surprising since Old Lu occasionally shared his knowledge of Dao Fruits, pretending to be learned.

However, Meng Junzi was different; it was his first time hearing that Dao Fruits were deduced this way, the first time learning that Dao Fruits could be categorized into "Causality-Tainting" and "Causality-Severing" types, and that beyond Dao Fruits lay Omniscience and Transcendence...

Meng Junzi couldn’t help but feel amazed; this trip to the Seek Dao Sect hadn’t been in vain. Jingzhou was worth taking a twice political hit for, as this was an immense opportunity, clearly pointing out his future path.

He glanced out of the corner of his eye, noticing Meng Jingzhou’s lack of focus and felt a bit annoyed, transmitting a message: "Aren’t you going to pay attention? This knowledge is greatly beneficial to you!"

"Ah? Don’t you already know this knowledge, ancestor?"

Meng Junzi: "..."

Seriously, are you the Immortal or am I the Immortal?

Although it was a brief lesson, for Meng Junzi, it held profound significance, making him wonder if this was the vision of the founder of Dao Fruits, truly extraordinary; he struggled for a hundred thousand years and couldn’t grasp what was directly pointed out today.

"Daoist Immortal, how exactly does one walk the path of Transcendence?" Meng Junzi couldn’t help asking and regretted it immediately.

Such a question touched upon the fundamental essence of Dao Fruits; he had just met Fairy Eternity, how could he ask such a foundational question.

"Of course, it’s, uh, how does it go again?"

Though he did not receive an answer, Meng Junzi wasn’t disappointed; today’s gains far exceeded his expectations, and he dared not wish for more.

"Daoist Immortal, Daoist Yun Zhi, I’ll take my leave now." Meng Junzi saluted with his hands and once he left the Seek Dao Sect, he couldn’t hold back his speed and immediately flew back to Imperial City. He would go into seclusion to cultivate, possibly deepening his understanding of Dao Fruits.

In the following days, Meng Jingzhou peacefully went into closed-door cultivation, and Lu Yang began to report about Old Meng, printing a newspaper and setting up a Cultivation Weekly Plan.

As for the specific implementation staff for the Cultivation Weekly Plan, Lu Yang posted it as a task in the Task Hall, and naturally, Junior Brothers and Sisters would take it on.

"It’s good to have a high realm; think of how I used to struggle to complete tasks, never having posted tasks before."

Lu Yang felt something was off about his words after saying them.

It seemed like he had only completed tasks twice, the first time capturing a parrot, the second time fighting a Tiger Demon.

Ten days later, Lu Yang received a letter.

"A letter sent by Man Gu?"

Lu Yang casually opened the letter; since Man Gu had become the Top Scorer and the County Magistrate, the two had corresponded frequently.

Probably discussing some minor affairs of the town, Lu Yang thought.

"Ghosts in the city?"

Chapter 1214: 1123 County Magistrate Man Gu

Man Gu’s letter was lengthy, filled with all sorts of references that should not have been referenced, and ended with superfluous classical embellishments.

Stripping away the frivolous content, Lu Yang condensed the message. It roughly meant — the town’s haunted troubles can’t be resolved, Senior Brother, save me.

Lu Yang found it quite preposterous. Man Gu was a formidable Void Refinement cultivator, how could he be unable to deal with a mere haunting... Oh, right, Void Refinement. Indeed, he couldn’t handle it.

"Old Meng, Junior Brother Man sent me a letter saying their town’s troubled by ghosts," said Lu Yang as he sought the specter-banishing expert.

Ten days had passed since Meng Jingzhou’s explosive encounter, and he had just consolidated his realm after emerging from seclusion.

"Did you get one too?"

Meng Jingzhou also had a copy of the letter from Man Gu.

Upon comparing the dates on their letters, they figured out what had happened: Man Gu first sent a letter to Meng Jingzhou, but it arrived while Meng Jingzhou was in seclusion, so he sent another to Lu Yang.

However, considering Man Gu still took the time to craft verbose phrases in his letter, it probably wasn’t an urgent matter.

"What do you think?" Meng Jingzhou asked.

Lu Yang displayed a profound smile, a habit he had acquired since advancing to the Tribulation Passing Stage, "After a stillness comes movement; it’s time to get out and about."

"That makes sense."

"Senior Ma..."

Lu Yang initially intended to call for Old Ma but scanned around and didn’t see him.

"Senior Ma has gone back to his hometown in the East Sea to break through in seclusion," Meng Jingzhou reported, "Looks like he’s poised to ascend to the Transcendence Tribulation Stage."

"That’s truly a cause for congratulations," Lu Yang laughed. Senior Ma, as Old Meng’s Protector, had worked hard despite not having to intervene much.

They would have to send a congratulatory gift to the Dragon Horse clan to celebrate Senior Ma’s breakthrough.

Old Ma’s breakthrough pleased Meng Jingzhou as well: "Uncle Ma has even moved onto the Transcendence Tribulation Stage, and here old dad is still stuck at the Body Integration Realm. If dad had run away from home with me back then, he’d be at the Transcendence Tribulation Stage by now!"

"... Do you even listen to yourself?"

Sometimes Old Meng’s thoughts were so lively that Lu Yang felt his own were too rigid.

"It’s fine without Senior Ma; after all, you are at the Transcendence Tribulation Stage now."

"Right, with your stage of cultivation, what’s there to fear?"

They generously complemented each other.

After a hearty laugh, the two decided to set out immediately.

Considering their notoriety with full-page spreads in newspapers, they had to disguise themselves before stepping out.

Man Gu was in charge of Quyi County in Da Xia Huangzhou Zhongshan County.

The two raced there at lightning speed, arriving directly at Quyi County.

On the way, they accidentally encountered the opening of an ancient battlefield relic. This site was said to be a relic from the Great Qian’s last period when the world vied for supremacy. Although the souls of its warriors were long gone, their indomitable wills remained. The ancient battlefield was scattered with countless opportunities, accessible only to those of virtuous conduct and exceptional talent who could earn approval from the lingering wills and become the masters of the relics.

They ventured into the ancient battleground and used the Recording Orb to broadcast the Captive Peak footage, telling the unyielding wills that it didn’t matter whether they were Great Qian Cultivators or Great Yu Cultivators, they should behave themselves and stop the constant conflict. Both The Controller and Guan Shanhai were imprisoned in Captive Peak.

The unyielding wills were quickly subdued by the pair, who then took control of the ancient battlefield relic, donated it to the Great Xia Court, and proposed that Gold Core Period Cultivators under the age of twenty-two should be allowed to train at the relic site.

They also discovered an Immortal Artifact Fragment within the relic. It was of unknown origin according to the wills but contained the essence of Yin and Yang, which could aid those cultivating the Yin Yang Path. This fragment had once incited a fierce battle among Semi-Immortals over its possession.

According to Immortal Fairy Eternity, it was confirmed to be a recording device of Immortal Jiuchong observing the mating of the Golden Crow and the Jade Rabbit.

Both were keen to view Immortal Jiuchong’s recordings, but during their dispute, they accidently fell into a ravine below and got lost, so they had to give up.

A local Divine Archer, a young hunter renowned in the mountains, shot an arrow through a rabbit.

Following the direction of the fletching, he found a delicately crafted fragment with unknown inscriptions next to the rabbit.

"What is this?"

He picked up both the rabbit and the fragment, and as the rabbit’s blood accidentally stained the fragment, it shone brightly, projecting a beam of light into the boy’s forehead.

The child recited the information contained within the fragment, "Extreme Yin Cultivation Method first layer?"

"And there’s the Extreme Yang Cultivation Method, too. Why only the names without content?"

"Could it be because this is a fragment, and we need to find the other pieces to put together the complete cultivation technique?"

"And this Golden Crow and Jade Rabbit Visualization is covered by a layer of thin mist. Is it because I’m not a cultivator that I can’t see it?"

Although the origin of the fragment was unknown, from the names of the cultivation technique and the visualization, it was clear that it was no ordinary item.

"Look, told you not to grab it, you lost it, didn’t you?"

"Like you didn’t grab it."

Both of them were still going on about the incident on the road even after they arrived at Quyi County, although, in truth, they were just looking for an opportunity to bicker.

After all, what was lost wasn’t something very important. The Extreme Yin Cultivation Method wasn’t much use to the two of them, only the Golden Crow and Jade Rabbit Visualization held some interest.

"This place seems to be quite well managed by Man Gu," the two commented as they walked along the main street towards the government office. It was daytime, the perfect moment when the County Magistrate would be presiding over the office.

The roads were clean and tidy, children were playing around, and the sounds of reading aloud could be heard along with officers patrolling the streets.

As they reached the government office, they happened to catch Man Gu holding a court session.

Man Gu was tall and broad; his official attire seemed somewhat out of place, especially since he had rolled up his sleeves, revealing his muscular arms.

Man Gu sat up straight and imposingly slapped his palm on the table, yelling out for the session to begin.

After he finished, he remembered he had forgotten to use the justice wood to bang on the table, but then decided it didn’t make much difference.

Two women were holding a child, weeping and wailing.

Lu Yang stood among the crowd at the entrance to the county government office and roughly understood what had transpired.

One of the women claimed her child had been lost since infancy, and just yesterday, she found another family’s child who greatly resembled her own. She insisted that the child was hers, but the other family refused to agree, declaring the child to be their biological offspring.

The dispute continued until it reached Man Gu, who was begged to make a ruling.

The Chief Clerk whispered to Man Gu, "My lord, you could order someone to beat the child, and the harsher the better. Whichever woman feels the most distressed for the child should be the true mother."

Man Gu looked at the Chief Clerk with a strange expression, "Does this method really work? Not to mention the child will have to suffer a beating, but even if the child isn’t biologically related to that family, having raised him for several years, they would still feel distressed for him."

"Then what does my lord suggest?"

"Of course, bloodline verification."

"Ah? I heard bloodline verification isn’t very accurate."

"It’s quite accurate, I believe."

Man Gu had each woman and the child drop a drop of blood into a bowl, then activated his mana to cause a drop of blood to explode, presenting the information contained within the blood before everyone while concurrently providing an explanation.

"There are three hundred million unit components within the blood; the arrangement of these components varies, but only among blood relatives is the sequence identical."

"Three hundred million components?" the Chief Clerk stuttered, "How can we compare these?"

"Of course, you look with your eyes." Man Gu said matter-of-factly, seriously observing the arrangement of the tiny units, and quickly reached a conclusion.

"Oh, I see now; you three are from the same family."

Man Gu pointed to one of the families, "You’re actually over fifty years old, aren’t you? You appear younger because you’re a cultivator. Have you lost a daughter before?"

The family nodded repeatedly.

Then Man Gu pointed to the other woman, "Were you picked up as a child?"

The woman also nodded.

Man Gu banged his gavel and pointed towards the family, "Then that settles it, she is your mother."

"In other words, you’re the child’s mother, and she’s the child’s grandmother."

Chapter 1215: 1214: There Are So Many Rules in the Officialdom

It was evident that under Man Gu’s governance, Quyi County was harmonious and well-run; within moments, he had helped two people find their long-lost mother, a very touching scene.

As Man Gu decisively handled the case and returned to the back hall to rest, a voice called out to him.

Man Gu turned around and saw two immaculate, peerless experts standing in the back hall, overjoyed, "Brother Lu! Brother Meng!"

Although both had disguised their appearances, their unchanged voices made it easy for Man Gu to recognize them.

County Magistrate Man personally attended to the two distinguished guests, serving tea and water and a platter of fruits and snacks.

"Both elder brothers have ascended to the Transcendance Tribulation Stage, and I have been so busy with government affairs that I could only write letters to congratulate you, let me take this opportunity to congratulate you here now."

After sending a letter to Meng Jingzhou asking for help, Man Gu saw on the newspaper that Meng Jingzhou had advanced to the Transcendance Tribulation Stage and then sent a second letter to Lu Yang.

Lu Yang was taken aback to find that in the few years since they’d last met, Man Gu’s way of speaking had become pedantic, a complete change from before.

"It seems like Junior Brother Man is doing quite well as the County Magistrate?"

"That goes without saying—one moment, no, that’s not how I should say it. How should it go again? Just a second."

Saying this, Man Gu pulled out a small notebook from his bosom, its edges curled, showing signs of long-term use.

He flipped to the end and read earnestly from the contents of the notebook, "Thanks to the concern of both my elder brothers, ever since I’ve become the County Magistrate, I have been working hard and contemplated intensely about the direction of our county’s development..."

"Hold on, what are you reading there?" Lu Yang couldn’t help but ask.

Lu Yang also noticed that the notebook contained the congratulatory words verbatim, identical without a single letter changed.

Were you reading out scripted lines?

"You see, previously my manner of speaking didn’t quite fit that of a Confucian Cultivator. Confucian Cultivators always like to quote the words of the sages, which essentially is reciting lines. So, I thought of this method—plan the dialogue scenarios in advance, decide what to say, then jot it down and memorize it."

"My secretary even advises me to do this, and he often writes my speeches for me."

Both Lu Yang and Meng felt that Man Gu had some misunderstandings about Confucian Cultivators, but upon reflection, it seemed he might be on the right track.

Meng Jingzhou sighed, "Just say what you want to say, don’t memorize it."

"Well, being a County Magistrate is actually quite tough—the rules are too many."

"What do you mean?"

"When I first became the County Magistrate, I went to the county office for a meeting. It was almost over when the County Magistrate said we could openly express any thoughts about Zhongshan County or suggestions for him, and I was planning to speak after my colleagues, but after waiting for a long while, none of them spoke, so I stood up and mentioned a few points."

"The County Magistrate was quite pleased with me and asked if I wanted to become the County Magistrate right away. I kindly reminded him that I had just become the County Magistrate and couldn’t be promoted to County Magistrate immediately, as it wouldn’t be in line with the regulations."

"After returning, I mentioned this to the secretary, and he wrote some guidelines on my notebook, telling me I can’t do this."

Man Gu showed Lu Yang the notebook, flipping to the first few pages, which were densely packed with all sorts of prohibitions such as, "Don’t speak when your superior says you can speak freely," "Don’t report your colleagues for drinking after work," "When your superior invites you to stay for a meal, don’t actually eat it, and even if you do, don’t say you’re not full," and so on.

"Why are there so many guidelines?" Both Lu Yang were surprised; the secretary seemed to be a real talent, capable of noticing so many details.

"Oh, it’s because every time I break one, he writes one down for me."

"..."

"That’s why I say the officialdom has too many rules; it’s not as comfortable as being in a Sect."

"Actually, I’ve been having quite a good time in officialdom," Lu Yang said.

"Ah?" Man Gu was surprised, since when did Elder Brother Lu Yang join the officialdom?

"You wouldn’t guess, but my current identity is that of a High-Ranking Minister."

"High-Ranking Minister? Does our Great Xia even have such an official position?"

"Well, Emperor Xia recognizes it."

"Brother Lu is really amazing."

No wonder Elder Brother Lu Yang is incredible; even being a minor county magistrate is so difficult for me, yet he has become a High-Ranking Minister without anyone knowing. This is the difference between people.

I clearly have much more to learn.

"Enough of that, what’s this about a haunting?" Meng Jingzhou asked. Now that solar energy lamps have completely replaced candles, every household has solar energy lamps, and with the seal of the Sun fully lifted, the Power of the Supreme Yang has exploded in growth. How could ghosts still pose a threat?

"It was one night three months ago."

"At the time, I was cultivating, when suddenly a constable patrolling the streets reported to me, saying that there were several ghosts wandering the main street, and that they had tried various methods to no avail; the ghosts were completely unaffected."

"When I went to have a look, it was indeed the case. Several ghosts were shone on by solar energy lamps, stabbed with Peach Wood Swords, all to no effect. The Peach Wood Swords even passed straight through them as if they felt nothing. They did not respond to our attacks, nor did they seem to intend to attack us."

"When I arrived, the ghosts had already begun to vanish."

"Later, ghosts appeared again, but at that time my cultivation realm was fluctuating to a very low degree, so I couldn’t take action and had to ask for cultivators from the county to come, but to no effect."

"There was no other way, so I thought of asking the two Senior Brothers for help."

"It does sound a bit strange. How many times have the ghosts appeared?"

"Three times. The timing of their appearances isn’t fixed, but it’s always at night."

Meng Jingzhou stroked his chin. "The timing isn’t fixed, that’s troublesome. Has anything like this happened in nearby counties?"

Man Gu shook his head. "I asked around, no."

"It seems we’ll just have to stay here for a while. Oh, and Junior Brother Man, the matter of our arrival must be kept secret," Lu Yang said.

If the ghosts found out that Old Meng and I, renowned for upholding justice and righteousness, were here, they’d probably be too scared to show up.

"Of course," Man Gu nodded. Both Senior Brothers have always kept a low profile, not seeking the limelight.

"Shall I arrange accommodation for the two Senior Brothers?"

"Do you need people at the government office? Wherever you’re short-staffed, we can fill in temporarily."

"It so happens that two constables were dismissed for taking bribes, leaving two positions vacant. But, the two Senior Brothers are here to help as my guests..."

Meng Jingzhou hooked an arm around Man Gu’s neck. "No problem, it’s not good to be idle all the time. It’ll be fine to find something to do."

Man Gu was deeply moved. The two Senior Brothers had come a long way to catch ghosts and were even proactively helping him solve problems. What noble and righteous deeds.

Soon, Man Gu found constable uniforms for the two men. Once dressed, with their proud smiles, they suddenly looked like the typical bullying evil constables.

To avoid unnecessary trouble, Man Gu led the two to meet people in the government office, starting with the chief secretary.

Upon seeing the County Magistrate bringing in two unfamiliar constables, the secretary was astonished.

Normally, recruiting constables would require a series of trials; how had the County Magistrate found two so quickly?

"My lord, who are these two constables?"

Man Gu remembered Lu Yang’s reminder to keep their identities secret, so he couldn’t introduce the two as his Senior Brothers. A brief introduction would suffice.

"These two got in through the back door."

Chapter 1216: 1215: Poor Public Safety

Soon, everyone in the Government Office knew that two individuals had entered through the back door to become constables.

The entire office was speculating about their identities.

"The Lord County Magistrate is still from the Seek Dao Sect, with an iron face and impartiality, who doesn’t give face to anyone. To have the Lord County Magistrate pull strings for them, their connections must be really strong," one person said.

"Do you think it’s possible that they are from the Seek Dao Sect?" asked another.

"Impossible. Those from the Seek Dao Sect are all Heavenly Chosen Sons. Why would they stoop to becoming the hardworking constables?"

To the common people, constables appeared imposing and frightening, but only when one actually became one did they realize the job involved uninterrupted day and night shifts. Even on supposed days off, you could be called back for work, and with a major case, rest was out of the question. Moreover, physical skills were a requirement to avoid being stabbed to death by criminals.

The secretary spoke confidently, "In my opinion, these two are very likely from a Barbarian tribe."

"From a Barbarian tribe?"

"Yes, think about it. Our Quyi County is in Wild State, and so are the Barbarian tribes. These two are probably relatives of the magistrate."

"But their surnames aren’t Barbarian."

"Distant relatives, with different surnames from the Barbarian tribe."

"That makes sense."

This viewpoint quickly gained acceptance throughout the Government Office. Since they had used the backdoor to enter, the office’s rules didn’t apply to these two. As long as they didn’t do anything too outrageous, everyone turned a blind eye.

...

Wild State was rich in minerals. Cultivators adapted to local conditions to refine these minerals, and over time, Wild State became a gathering place for blacksmiths. There were over a hundred large and small blacksmith shops in Quyi County.

Amongst the many blacksmith shops, the Wang Family Blacksmith Shop was the most unimpressive one. Their main customers were neighbors needing repairs for their shovels, hoes, kitchen knives, and iron pots.

Old Wang, with a missing front tooth, was highly skilled and always repaired these items flawlessly.

But that was all there was to it.

The Wang Family Blacksmith Shop had only Old Wang and Little Wang, the blacksmith, who depended on each other for survival. As Old Wang grew older, Little Wang took over his work, repairing iron pots for the neighbors.

"Grandpa, it’s time to eat," called Little Wang to Old Wang.

Usually, Old Wang would come out of the house with a laugh, but today, Little Wang called several times without a response from Old Wang.

Worried about his grandfather, Little Wang went inside and saw Old Wang holding a piece of red cloth, staring into space, dumbfounded.

"Child, come here, your grandpa has something to tell you." Old Wang’s voice was hoarse, and his eyes were no longer as bright as before; they had become exceptionally dim.

"Grandpa, what’s wrong?"

"Actually, your grandpa has never told you this, but I am not an ordinary person, I am a cultivator."

"My true identity is that of an heir to the Divine Craftsman Wang Family, which has been a hermitage with a legacy lasting ten thousand years."

"Your grandpa broke the ancestral rules and forged an Evil Sword. The Evil Sword has no grade; it craves killing and blood. The more it kills, the more blood it drinks, the higher its grade becomes."

Old Wang uncovered the red cloth he was holding, revealing a fierce Blood Sword with a Vermilion Gem embedded in the hilt, like the eye of a Demon God.

"As soon as this sword came into existence, it was marked by the people of the Hundred Sword Alliance. The Alliance wanted to take my sword, and I knew that if this Bloodthirsty Evil Sword ended up in their hands, it would lead to untold slaughter."

"In order to avoid the pursuit of the Hundred Sword Alliance, I fled all the way here."

"I wanted to destroy this Evil Sword, but unfortunately, it is a product of my extraordinary performance; it’s too durable to be destroyed by me."

"Because forging a Demon Sword was banned by Great Xia, I couldn’t report this to the government office, so I had to settle here under a false name and then found you."

"Sadly, after so many years of hiding, I have finally been found by the people of the Hundred Sword Alliance."

Old Wang took out a token: "This is the Hundred Sword Pursuit Order, which I received today. The Hundred Sword Alliance loves toying with their prey, and if I’m not mistaken, they will make their move tonight."

"Members of the Hundred Sword Alliance each possess a Heavenly Weapon Divine Sword, commanding respect wherever the sword points."

Old Wang handed Little Wang a package: "Grandpa is helpless and cannot protect you. Later, I will fight a desperate battle with the people from the Hundred Sword Alliance. During the chaos, you must run!"

"The refining techniques that grandpa taught you are actually the Ninety-nine Stages of Refining Technique, passed down orally in the Divine Craftsman Wang Family. From now on, you are the sole heir of the Divine Craftsman Wang Family!"

"Grandfather!" Little Wang, the blacksmith, cried as he held Old Wang in his arms.

Little Wang wasn’t being a child, but Old Wang’s eyes also started to redden.

...

"It’s really deserted out here at night," Constable Lu Yang said to Constable Meng Jingzhou as they patrolled the streets at night.

It was already past midnight, the streets were chillingly silent with just the two of them.

"By the way, have you decided what kind of Dao Fruit you want to condense?" Lu Yang brought up a casual topic.

Meng Jingzhou shook his head, with his saber swinging to and fro, "Haven’t figured it out yet. With the Immortal Jiuchong around, I can’t condense anything like the Supreme Yang Dao Fruit."

"What about you?"

"I haven’t decided either."

As they were discussing, they saw two mysterious shadows approach, blending into the darkness with something on their backs wrapped in black cloth, bulging out.

"Halt, who are you, and why are you wandering the streets at night!"

Lu Yang demanded, "Show us your identity tokens or some other proof of identity!"

The shoulders of the mysterious figures shook as they let out a chilling laugh, "Ignorance truly is bliss. You’re just unlucky to have encountered us."

"Our swords are our identity!"

They drew the strange-shaped swords they were carrying on their backs!

In an instant, the two mysterious shadows appeared behind the two junior constables, neatly sheathing their swords.

"I’ve displayed eight swords," one of the mysterious figures declared.

"I’ve displayed nine swords," the other boasted, ostensibly stronger.

Sharing a cold laugh, the mysterious figures continued towards Wang Family Blacksmith Shop.

"Don’t hurry off, come back here."

Lu Yang grabbed the collars of the two mysterious figures in one swoop.

The figures looked incredulously at Lu Yang—as they were sure he should have been sliced into pieces by now.

They tried to draw their swords again, only to find that all that remained were the hilts!

Meng Jingzhou looked helplessly at the broken pieces of iron scattered at their feet—what lousy swords that shattered upon contact with them.

These two really were incompetent, not even noticing their swords were reduced to mere hilts and still wildly flailing around like lunatics.

"Speak, what are you doing?"

...

Wang Family Blacksmith Shop.

The grandfather and grandson hugged and cried for quite a while when Old Wang started feeling uneasy—members of the Hundred Sword Alliance should have shown up by now.

Thud, thud, thud.

A knocking sound came from the door, and Old Wang’s heart sank—it’s them!

"Is anyone home? We are constables from the County Government here to inquire about a matter."

Trying to impersonate constables to lure us to open the door? Does the Hundred Sword Alliance take me for a fool!

Old Wang’s palm slid over his sword, the Evil Sword absorbing the blood from his palm and becoming even more ferocious.

Little Wang, the blacksmith, cracked the door open stealthily, and Old Wang, gathering his strength, thrust his sword at the two figures at the door.

"Run, child! I’ll fight them!"

Lu Yang extended his index and middle finger, gently pinching the Evil Sword, being very careful not to break it with too much force.

Meng Jingzhou clicked his tongue, "Security here really is lacking, huh? First night on the job as a constable, and we’ve been attacked by two different groups?"

Chapter 1217: 1216: Both Our Alliance Hierarch and Vice Alliance Hierarch Have Participated in the Sword Inquiry Ceremony!

Meng Jingzhou felt that some things couldn’t be judged by their appearance.

In the daytime, when he arrived at Quyi County, everything seemed peaceful and harmonious, praising Man Gu for his good governance, but at night the truth came out.

The two of them, dressed as constables, patrolled the streets, and within less than the time it takes to finish a cup of tea, they had been spotted by two groups. Luckily, the brothers had some skills, and so nothing happened.

Forget it, as the Senior Brother, he should learn to be accommodating to his Junior Brother. While he and Old Lu were here, they might as well help Man Gu maintain the peace in Quyi County.

"What, what!?"

As Meng Jingzhou pondered, Old Wang was plunged into immense shock. He had planned this strike for a long time, even allowing the Bloodthirsty Evil Sword to drink blood to break through its restraints. How could it possibly be clamped by just two fingers?

Even the people from the Myriad Sword Alliance couldn’t achieve this feat!

Old Wang’s palm slashed across, blood flowing freely, all of which was absorbed by the Bloodthirsty Evil Sword. The sword itself possessed spirituality, but because it had not been nourished with fresh blood for a long time, it had fallen into a deep slumber.

Now that Old Wang was recklessly feeding it his blood, the Bloodthirsty Evil Sword finally awoke from its deep sleep!

This was the full glory of the Bloodthirsty Evil Sword!

Old Wang urged the Evil Sword forward, but the sword hummed as if it had encountered some terrifyingly formidable presence. It desperately retreated, trembling as if terrified to confront the person before it.

"Who are you!" Who exactly was the opponent that could instill such fear in the Bloodthirsty Evil Sword, completely surpassing Old Wang’s understanding?

Lu Yang, clutching the collar of the constable’s uniform with resignation, said, "Haven’t we already told you? We’re constables from the County Government. My surname is Lu, and this gentleman beside me is surnamed Meng."

The people of Quyi County are truly fierce, hacking at themselves and bleeding.

Then Lu Yang pulled out two unconscious mysterious figures from behind him.

"While patrolling the streets, we encountered these two scoundrels. After subduing them, they revealed their purpose, and we came to understand the situation."

Seeing the emblems on the chests of the mysterious figures, Old Wang’s pupils shrank: "People from the Myriad Sword Alliance!"

Lu Yang uttered an "Oh": "It seems they weren’t lying; they were indeed looking for you."

"I was a bit heavy-handed when interrogating these two just now, and they were knocked out before we could finish asking questions. You, as the victim, should know something. Tell us why they were looking for you?"

Old Wang clenched his teeth in turmoil for a moment before finally sighing internally. These two constables were definitely not simple; it would not be wise to hide anything.

"A hundred years ago, I violated our ancestral teachings and forged a heinous Evil Sword..."

Old Wang did not hide the truth, and he repeated the experiences he had once told young Wang the blacksmith.

While speaking, Old Wang covertly observed Lu Yang and his companion’s reactions, but found them unfathomable. Their expressions did not change, making it impossible to guess what they were thinking.

"I see." After hearing the story, Lu Yang and his companion casually nodded. It was a rather common tale—nothing surprising.

"However, we’ve never heard of a power called the Myriad Sword Alliance." From the Five Great Immortal Sects to Third Grade powers, they had heard of many names, but couldn’t recall one called the Myriad Sword Alliance.

Old Wang shook his head. He didn’t know much about the Myriad Sword Alliance either, only that it was filled with formidable experts, certainly no shortage of those on par with his own strength.

Old Wang lowered his voice as if sharing a great secret: "Also, I’ve heard that the Myriad Sword Alliance might have the backing of a government office!"

"Wow, now that’s interesting," Lu Yang chuckled, giving Meng Jingzhou a look. Meng Jingzhou proceeded to splash cold water on the two mysterious figures and pinch their acupuncture points, waking up the two Myriad Sword Alliance members.

"You two insignificant constables dare to meddle with people from the Myriad Sword Alliance; looks like you’re tired of living..."

Meng Jingzhou, running out of patience, slapped them: "Be quiet and show some manners. Answer what you’re asked!"

"What exactly is the origin of your Myriad Sword Alliance, and why have we never heard of it?"

"Heh." A mysterious figure let out a disdainful sneer, "The world is far from as simple as you imagine. Where there’s light, there’s darkness. The so-called ’Grades’ of Sects are just a public division of power, that’s all. We, the Myriad Sword Alliance, are the power that walks in the darkness!"

Lu Yang nodded, translating to himself, "So, it’s an unregistered, illegal organization."

"Boy, watch your language!" Another mysterious figure angrily said, feeling that Lu Yang was insulting the Myriad Sword Alliance.

"We, the Myriad Sword Alliance, aim to collect divine swords from all over the world, with each member possessing a Heavenly Weapon Divine Sword. Each Divine Sword has unparalleled power!"

"My ’Ghost King Soul-breaking Sword’ can injure the Three Souls and Seven Spirits! His ’Thunder Dragon Bone Sword’ can summon lightning!"

"The Alliance Hierarch and Vice Alliance Hierarch of our Myriad Sword Alliance are unfathomably powerful. They are Divinity Transformation Sword Cultivators who have participated in the Sword Inquiry Ceremony at the Sword Tower!"

"Sword Inquiry Ceremony?" After they had talked for so long, Lu Yang finally heard a familiar term, a bit surprised.

Was being qualified to attend the Sword Inquiry Ceremony something to boast about?

He had thought that as long as one was a Sword Cultivator, one would be eligible to attend the Sword Inquiry Ceremony. It turns out there was a threshold.

Seeing Lu Yang’s shocked expression, the two mysterious figures scoffed incessantly, "Now you know what kind of power you’ve provoked!"

Meng Jingzhou kicked over the two who were sneering, cursing, "I thought you were from some major power. Turns out you’re just a small Third Grade Sect puffing yourselves up!"

Small Third Grade power?

Both the mysterious figures and Old Wang started to doubt their ears. When had the term "small power" ever been linked to a Third Grade Sect?

"Let’s get things sorted out quickly while we’re on duty tonight. We’ll ask about the Myriad Sword Alliance’s information on the road." Lu Yang prepared to leave the Wang Family Blacksmith Shop with the two mysterious figures.

Old Wang called out to Lu Yang and his companion as he saw them about to leave.

"Wait a moment, aren’t you going to arrest me?"

Lu Yang and his companion were perplexed by Old Wang’s question, "Arrest you for what, attacking us? Isn’t everything clear now? You mistook us for people from the Myriad Sword Alliance. It’s understandable."

"I forged an Evil Sword!"

"Grandpa, please don’t leave!" Little Wang the blacksmith cried, clinging to Old Wang.

Old Wang affectionately stroked Little Wang’s head, reminiscing about the time when he had just found the child, "Time flies, you’ve grown up so. It’s a pity that Grandpa can’t stay by your side anymore."

"These years, Grandpa has been tormented day and night. If I hadn’t gone against the family traditions and forged an Evil Sword, none of this would have happened. Maybe if I spend my remaining years in prison, I might feel a bit better."

As Lu Yang and his companion listened to Old Wang, the more he spoke, the stranger it seemed, "Forging an Evil Sword isn’t illegal."

"That’s impossible!" Old Wang became anxious, even pulling out an ancient booklet from his chest — the ancestral teachings of the Divine Craftsman Wang Family, arguing vehemently with Lu Yang and his companion.

"One of our Divine Craftsman Wang Family’s ancestral teachings is not to forge Evil Swords, because forging them is illegal!"

Lu Yang was silent for a while before asking, "When was this ancestral teaching left behind?"

Speaking of the history of the Divine Craftsman Wang Family, Old Wang was extremely proud, "Our Divine Craftsman Wang Family is a reclusive family that dates back ten thousand years, so naturally, it was from ten thousand years ago!"

Lu Yang nodded, "That explains it."

"What explains what?"

"When the criminal law was revised eight thousand years ago, they had already removed the prohibition against forging Evil Swords."

"After all, whether an Evil Sword is wicked depends on how it’s used. This Bloodthirsty Evil Sword is quite suitable for nourishing with one’s own body. Who knows, if nurtured long enough, it might even develop a Sword Spirit."

Before leaving, Lu Yang took out a book and handed it to Old Wang, "Oh, and we constables also have the duty to enlighten the ignorant. This is the latest edition of the criminal law. Study it when you have the time, and don’t scare yourself for no reason."

Chapter 1218: 1217: Junior Brother Man, good news, there’s a big case!

Considering that the Hundred Sword Alliance is equivalent to a Third Grade Sect and both the Alliance Hierarch and the Vice Alliance Hierarch have participated in the Sword Inquiry Ceremony as Divinity Transformation Stage Sword Cultivators, who are well-known for their offensive power, it’s rare for them to meet their match among their peers.

The existence of the Alliance Hierarch and Vice Alliance Hierarch means that in the ranks of the Third Grade Sects, the Hundred Sword Alliance can be considered upper-middle level. Moreover, since the Hundred Sword Alliance is spread across the Wild State, they are somewhat difficult to apprehend, and even for two Tribulation Crossing Great Powers, resolving the issue of the Hundred Sword Alliance in one night is tough.

Lu Yang told them in an even tone on the way, though the content of his words sounded terrifying.

"Old Lu, don’t get carried away and Soul Search them both; just one is enough," Constable Meng reminded him. "We need to bring one back to explain how we came upon the trail of the Hundred Sword Alliance."

Soul Searching one of them and burying him in a hole meant that no one would ever know he existed. Simple.

Lu Yang nodded. "You’re right. We can’t exactly say that Old Wang knew the location of the Hundred Sword Alliance’s headquarters and took us there. Getting our stories straight with Old Wang would be a pain. So, I’ll just Soul Search one of them."

"No, no, no, we’ll talk! We’ll talk!"

The two members of the Hundred Sword Alliance trembled like two frightened quails. What kind of background did these two constables have? Not only was their cultivation outstanding, but they were also ruthless.

After the two confessed, Lu Yang got a rough idea of the situation within the Hundred Sword Alliance.

The Hundred Sword Alliance was an alliance formed by Sword Cultivators, committed to collecting renowned swords from around the world by any means. With members spread across the entire Wild State, they had considerable fame there. The Hundred Sword Alliance continued unscathed thanks to the support from an official who also had a fondness for collecting swords.

But as for which official was supporting them, that remained unknown—only the Alliance Hierarch and Vice Alliance Hierarch were privy to that crucial secret.

The Hundred Sword Alliance’s headquarters was located in the black market of Zhongshan County.

"Besides being a renowned power, the rest should be true," said Lu Yang.

In the past, he and Old Meng had been to the Wild State. It was their first encounter with Mu Baiyi, and they also took care of Emperor Wu Yao of Yu, having spent quite some time in the Wild State without hearing about any Hundred Sword Alliance.

"Let’s deal with the headquarters first. It’s quite close," Meng Jingzhou suggested. Conveniently, the headquarters were in Zhongshan County, and Quyi County was under its jurisdiction.

"Let’s go."

The night was dark and windy, perfect for a raid. Four figures flew swiftly and silently over the walls, entering Zhongshan County without a sound.

The two junior constables were new to the job and had been handed such a significant case. It would be a lie to say they weren’t nervous.

But even if they weren’t nervous, they couldn’t let the enemy escape.

Thus, as soon as they arrived at the black market, they wrapped the entire place with their Spiritual Sense to ensure not a single person from the Hundred Sword Alliance escaped.

The entrance to the Hundred Sword Alliance was deep underground, and there was a gate forged from a strange stone that could only be opened by uttering the correct passphrase.

As the gate sensed the presence of the two men, a ferocious human face appeared in its center, "State the passphrase."

Lu Yang ignored the face and simply pushed open the gate, declaring loudly, "Nobody move, hands on your heads and squat down! We are constables from Quyi County, and we have received a tip-off. This establishment is suspected of illegal operations, theft, robbery, tax evasion, intentional injury, murder, and various other charges. Resisting will only worsen your crimes!"

"Who are these little constables, daring to meddle with your superior?" The Hundred Sword Alliance had many formidable members and had been entrenched in the black market for centuries, all thanks to the swords they wielded.

"Xuan Hei, Overlord, it’s you two who betrayed the organization!"

"Two lowly trash dare to betray us? Wipe them out together!"

Xuan Hei and Overlord—who were they? Lu Yang wondered, then he quickly realized these were the two men they had captured that evening.

It turned out they hadn’t even asked their names during the interrogation.

No matter, it’s not important. Xuan Hei and Overlord didn’t sound like real names anyway. They would find out their actual names when making the report later.

Each member of the Hundred Sword Alliance held a Heavenly Weapon Divine Sword. These Divine Swords varied in shape and had very intimidating names. Some could tear through space, some could change the weather, some could control the earth, some could absorb spiritual essence...

But Lu Yang didn’t care.

Confronted with the Heavenly Weapon Divine Swords that exuded various eerie glows, Lu Yang simply uttered two words.

"Sword Return."

Without exception, the Divine Swords slipped from their masters’ control and rushed towards Lu Yang as if subjects meeting an emperor from ancient times!

The members of the Hundred Sword Alliance watched this scene in disbelief. They were the masters of the Heavenly Weapon Divine Swords. Once recognized by them, the swords wouldn’t become unowned unless they died and could only then be controlled by others.

Who exactly was this junior constable! How did he do it!

"Ha ha ha, who would have thought there would be such a master hiding among the constables!"

Two hearty voices echoed from the end of the hallway, accompanied by calm, unhurried footsteps.

"Alliance Hierarch! Vice Alliance Hierarch!"

The members of the Hundred Sword Alliance were ecstatic.

"All of you back down. This man is an extremely rare master of Swordsmanship, and he is not someone you can confront."

"Yes!"

The two Alliance Hierarchs stood side by side, looking at the seemingly insignificant constables with interest, and spoke slowly,

"Though Wild State has many blacksmiths forging countless divine weapons, there are very few sword cultivators. For this reason, we brothers defeated many swordsmanship masters of Wild State and earned the qualification to attend the Sword Inquiry Ceremony, merely seeking defeat."

"We never imagined that Wild State still had a swordsmanship master like you. Our trip to the Sword Inquiry Ceremony was indeed casting aside what is near in pursuit of what is distant!"

"Formless and shapeless, originally infinite, the chaotic void breeds the Taixu. Come, fight; my Infinite Yin Yang Sword has not met an opponent in a long time!" The Alliance Hierarch drew his Infinite Yin Yang Sword.

"The world fixes its position and the stars shift; the Eight Diagrams evolve and reveal the mysterious void. Let’s see how many moves of my Taixu Divine Sword you can withstand!" The Vice Alliance Hierarch drew his Taixu Divine Sword.

"It’s the Infinite Yin Yang Sword! How long has it been since the Alliance Hierarch has drawn that sword!"

"And the Taixu Divine Sword, it’s said that there are nine moves in total, but the people who have seen up to the sixth move are few and far between; no one has ever seen the ninth!"

The Alliance Hierarch and the Vice Alliance Hierarch mobilized their proud swords, which appeared mysterious and unfathomable, their abilities unknown.

Bang, bang.

Meng Jingzhou threw two punches, knocking one down with each punch: "Blah, blah, blah, what nonsensical flamboyance."

"Right, Old Lu, do you remember these two?"

Lu Yang tried hard to recall the Sword Inquiry Ceremony process, "I don’t remember the Vice Alliance Hierarch, but the Alliance Hierarch seems to be Xue Shilou’s opponent, eliminated by Xue Shilou in the first round."

"Also, next time, use a little less force; you’ve knocked them both out."

Meng Jingzhou looked and saw that this was indeed the case, and quickly pinched their pressure points to wake them up.

"Speak, who’s protecting the Hundred Sword Alliance!"

Meng Jingzhou exuded a trace of formidable pressure, like a primeval fierce beast or a resplendent sun, supreme and ultimate, making the Alliance Hierarch and the Vice Alliance Hierarch cringe on the ground like ants, not daring to hide anything.

"It’s... it’s the County Magistrate."

"How should we handle this?" Meng Jingzhou turned to ask, "Should we arrest these people first before finding the County Magistrate, or should we go directly to him?"

"To prevent him from hearing any rumors and running away, let’s go directly."

Lu Yang left a Wooden Avatar to watch over the members of the Hundred Sword Alliance while he and Old Meng hurriedly rushed to the Governor Mansion.

County Magistrate Lu of Zhongshan County was at that moment on a luxurious pleasure boat, blindfolded and frolicking with some young women.

Suddenly, the laughing voices of the women disappeared; County Magistrate Lu, puzzled, lifted the cloth, slightly annoyed: "Why has it gone quiet?"

The two constables appeared in the room at some point, smiling and watching County Magistrate Lu.

"County Magistrate Lu, I presume? We’re constables from Quyi County. We’ve received a report that you are colluding with the Hundred Sword Alliance. Please come with us."

Upon hearing this, County Magistrate Lu’s face changed, and then he angrily pushed Meng Jingzhou, "Get lost! Two little constables also dare to arrest me, ruining my mood for the evening!"

Meng Jingzhou remained immovable.

Lu Yang kicked County Magistrate Lu to the ground and swiftly tied him up.

County Magistrate Lu was at the Divinity Transformation Stage, but facing Lu Yang was like facing a great mountain, utterly suppressed and without a shred of resistance.

It was only then that he realized these two constables were no ordinary men.

"Who exactly are you, and who sent you! Remember, I am an official appointed by the Dynasty, you lack the grade to arrest me!"

Lu Yang was taken aback and turned to ask Meng Jingzhou, "Is there such a rule?"
