Meng Jingzhou thought for a moment: "The County Magistrate is a fourth-grade official, and usually to arrest an official of this rank, one would need an official of at least the fourth grade or higher. However, that’s an unspoken rule; there’s no explicit regulation."

Lu Yang shrugged, "So it’s just an unspoken rule, then we’ll ignore it."

Lu Yang always abided by the law, acting in accordance with explicit regulations. Since they had jurisdiction over the Hundred Sword Alliance case, they could arrest anyone related to it.

"Besides, I am a Pillar State Minister myself, of higher grade than him. What’s wrong with arresting him?"

Dawn arrived, and Man Gu woke up from a deep sleep, or rather, was woken up by Lu Yang and Meng Jingzhou.

"Junior Brother Man, good news, we have a big case."

"What big case?"

"We’ve arrested your superior, County Magistrate Lu."

"Ah?"

Am I still asleep?

Chapter 1219: 1218: Honest People Are Reasonable

Man Gu sat up bewildered, pondering seriously for a moment, and confirmed that this was not a dream.

The reason was simple, he couldn’t have such a dream.

"Senior Brothers, initially, I didn’t understand the norms of officialdom and offended County Magistrate Lu, which led to him always disliking me, but that’s not a reason to arrest him, is it?"

Meng Jingzhou gave a thumbs up, signaling Man Gu to be at ease, "We are all acting according to the rules and regulations, the County Magistrate has problems of his own."

Man Gu breathed a sigh of relief; indeed, both Senior Brothers were sticklers for rules and would not arrest someone for personal gain.

He remembered asking the two Senior Brothers for help, which was to capture a ghost, and spending a night to end up capturing a superior, the answer was quite obvious.

"So, was it County Magistrate Lu who was causing the trouble?"

No doubt it was the two Senior Brothers, who solved the problem that had troubled him for three months in a single day.

"That’s not quite it, we two were on the night shift when we stumbled upon an illegal organization and followed the clues to discover that County Magistrate Lu had been protecting this group all along," said Lu Yang.

"I see," Man Gu nodded.

He had confronted the County Magistrate before and didn’t have any psychological burden about it; he and Lu Yang had killed the Yanjiang County Magistrate.

Moreover, it was not a big deal now, as they were only arresting and not killing the County Magistrate.

The arrest of County Magistrate Lu was a big deal and couldn’t be kept secret, and since Lu Yang and his companion acted uprightly, there was no need to hide anything. The news of County Magistrate Lu’s arrest quickly spread throughout the entire Government Office.

"What the hell, County Magistrate Lu was arrested by the newcomers?"

Everyone in the Government Office who heard the news would freeze for a couple of seconds, their brains needing time to digest the information.

"They just started yesterday, didn’t they? And they arrested the County Magistrate the very same night?"

Having worked in the government system for so many years, they hadn’t seen or even heard of such outrageous events.

"Secretary, what do you think... Oh, you’re taking your medicine, I won’t disturb you then."

The officials had wanted to ask the secretary for his opinion, but seeing that he was shivering while taking his herbal medicine, they discreetly left.

How could the secretary not tremble? For years he had been warning Man Gu about what not to do, knowing full well that County Magistrate Lu harbored dislike for Man Gu. If it weren’t for Man Gu’s status as the Top Scorer, Lu would have caused him trouble long ago.

Man Gu’s annual performance evaluation depended on the County Magistrate’s assessment, so he had tried his best to maintain a good relationship between Man Gu and the County Magistrate.

Now, there was no need to maintain that relationship.

...

"Open court!" Man Gu, dressed in his official robes, rolled up his sleeves and smacked the table with a palm louder than the warning wood.

"Bring up the prisoner!"

"Authority—Justice—"

Two meritorious constables escorted County Magistrate Lu to the stand.

Hearing that there was another session, the citizens came to watch the excitement, and only after arriving did they believe that this time the new officials had made a real mark by capturing a high-ranking official.

Old Wang and his son, the blacksmith Wang, were shocked to see the two constables from the previous night escorting County Magistrate Lu; they were speechless with surprise.

Wasn’t it said that they were going to capture the Hundred Sword Alliance? How did they even arrest the County Magistrate?

"Man Gu, how dare you let them arrest me!" bellowed County Magistrate Lu, unable to contain his anger. These two constables were clearly directed by Man Gu!

"Why wouldn’t I dare to arrest... oh, at this time I should say, you are corrupt and have abused your power for personal gain; it is my duty to arrest you!"

"Is it true that the Hundred Sword Alliance has been regularly providing you with Spirit Stones?"

"No."

"Was it you who ordered Hundred Sword Alliance to use all means to seize swords from all over Wild State?"

"No."

"One hundred and eight swords, grades ranging from Spirit Transformation Level to Golden Core Level, were found in your room. Are these swords yours?"

"No."

"Did you violate the Dynasty’s regulations by going to the brothel after work hours? Did that happen?"

"No."

Man Gu turned his head to the secretary, who was writing feverishly, and said, "Write this down, the evidence is conclusive, but he refuses to admit guilt. He does not qualify for a lighter sentence due to confession."

Before the court session, Lu Yang and his companion had efficiently returned to gather all the evidence, including the ninety-nine people from Hundred Sword Alliance.

With so many people from Hundred Sword Alliance, the testimonies were not yet complete, so they had only questioned the main and deputy heads and the four individuals from Xuan Hei Overlord.

It wasn’t a big problem, they would complete the testimonies later and hold another court session.

"I am an official appointed by the Dynasty; no one below a fourth-grade official has the authority to deal with me!" County Magistrate Lu grew increasingly angry, regarding this as a tremendous disgrace.

"Just wait a moment."

Man Gu left these words behind as he leafed through various regulations on the spot, first checking the criminal law to confirm the charges and then the criminal procedures to determine the jurisdiction. Only then did he close the books with satisfaction.

"See, the law clearly states that I have authority over anyone in my jurisdiction. It doesn’t say lower-grade officials can’t arrest higher-grade ones."

Man Gu displayed his Identity Jade. "Besides, I am a disciple of the Seek Dao Sect. We, disciples of the Seek Dao Sect, have the right to enforce the law."

Man Gu had always seen Lu Yang and his companion as role models to learn from. If they were honest people, so was he. And honest people always act rationally.

"Alright, take him away."

Since County Magistrate Lu said nothing, Man Gu saw that there was no need for further questioning.

...

The news of Man Gu arresting his superior quickly reached the ears of the upper echelons in Wild State. Their reaction was not much better than those in the Government Office; they all hesitated for a moment, questioning if they had heard wrong.

History is often said to reflect reality, but in the combined thirty thousand years of Great Yu, Great Qian, and Great Xia, such an incident had never occurred.

"Look on the bright side, at least future generations will have a reference when studying history," joked one of the high-ranking officials, who had encountered Man Gu a few times and knew this seemed like something Man Gu would do.

"In fact, there are precedents. At the end of every dynasty, this is what the rebels did."

"Mr. Song, that is going too far. Are you implying that Man Gu is rebelling?"

"You said that, not me."

"That’s what you are implying!"

Seeing the conversation heading in an undesirable direction, Governor Lv tapped on the table: "Enough with the jokes. Although this incident is unprecedented, Man Gu’s actions are not against the rules. Based on the evidence submitted, County Magistrate Lu has indeed violated the law and should be removed from his position."

"Who should be appointed as the County Magistrate then? Man Gu? Since he became a County Magistrate, he has managed Quyi County efficiently, and there has been significant economic development."

"Of course, the economy has developed significantly. He took advantage of his fluctuation to the Body Integration Realm to go down the mines himself. He nearly excavated an entire vein. How is that different from cheating? He cannot be allowed to become a County Magistrate."

"As long as he can develop the economy, why demand so much? Why shouldn’t he use his cultivation level?"

"If not Man Gu, then who? Zhongshan County scarcely has anyone impressive; who could take on such a responsibility?"

Governor Lv shook his head and made his decision: "Indeed, Man Gu is not suitable. Although he is the Top Scorer, his experience is still insufficient. A promotion of such a nature would require an order from the Dynasty. In my opinion, the County Magistrate of Guyuan County in Zhongshan County is prudent and experienced and commands respect. He could be appointed as the County Magistrate."

Chapter 1220: 1219: The Drought Demon Appears, Catastrophe of World Annihilation!

Quyi County outskirts cemetery.

A sneaky figure danced a bizarre dance, and a great door appeared beneath his feet.

He followed the door down into an underground tomb.

The figure excitedly rubbed his hands together, the method indeed worked, and it opened the tomb.

However, the tomb chamber was empty, lacking the imagined rare treasures and valuable antiques, holding only a pitch-black coffin with its four corners nailed down by blood-stained nails, and atop the coffin lay a palm-sized seal.

He stealthily took the seal, with the words "Mountain Seal" inscribed at its base.

After taking the seal, the figure hurriedly left, not noticing that the coffin was shaking.

...

Corpse Chasing Sect.

"Typically, there are two methods of cultivation for those at the Void Refinement Stage, Red Dust Mortality or War Feeds War..."

As Zhao Po was promoted to the Void Refinement Stage, Lu Baqian personally instructed him, imparting various techniques of the stage.

Zhao Po was listening intently when suddenly a crisp sound came from within Lu Baqian’s embrace, as if something had shattered!

Lu Baqian pulled out an object from within, a broken piece of Jade.

"Damn, the seal has been broken!" exclaimed Lu Baqian, his expression drastically changing.

"Sect Leader, what happened?" Zhao Po asked hurriedly.

"The seal is gone!"

"Seal? What seal?" Zhao Po still didn’t understand.

"Six thousand years ago, one of our forebears in the Corpse Chasing Sect, for some unknown reason, let a zombie he had created slip beyond his control. That zombie had already cultivated to ’Unformable Bone,’ just half a step away from becoming a Drought Demon!"

"The forebear and his zombie waged a fierce battle, and he desperately sealed the zombie but perished in the process, only fragmented messages returned to the Sect."

"But the most critical location of the seal wasn’t communicated back, leaving our sect unable to deal with that zombie."

"Now, six thousand years have passed, and although that zombie was sealed, now that it has broken free, it must have reached the ’Drought Demon’ Realm!"

Upon hearing this, Zhao Po’s expression also severely changed, a Drought Demon appearing meant scorched lands for thousands of miles, a catastrophe capable of ending the world!

"Our Sect’s Elders have all vested their cultivation in their zombies, and the Drought Demon has absolute control over other zombies; they can’t join the battle, so I’m the only one who can act!"

"We could ask the Dynasty for help!"

"It’s too late, once a Drought Demon appears, it will undoubtedly flee. Once it gains strength, even those at the Tribulation Crossing Stage will be unable to contend with it; we must go quickly!"

"It seems that the Drought Demon is in the east!"

Oddly, Zhao Po’s heart panicked; he always felt that something bad was about to happen.

The Sect Leader facing this might be more dangerous than lucky.

"Sect Leader, isn’t there another way to deal with that Drought Demon?"

"There is one possibility; our forebear possessed a Mountain Suppressing Seal, which is highly effective against Drought Demons, but this seal was crafted by the hermit family, the Divine Craftsman Wang Family!"

Lu Baqian sighed, "The Divine Craftsman Wang Family has been lost for a hundred years, nowhere to be found."

"Battling the Drought Demon is perhaps my destined fate!"

...

"Am I finally rid of this damned seal?" the coffin rattled as the female Drought Demon murmured to herself, how long had she waited for this day, three thousand or five thousand years?

Zombies, as long as their Spiritual Wisdom is not destroyed, and their bodies are not demolished, will grow stronger over time; a seal can only slow this process.

Over these six thousand years, she had slowly grown stronger, finally advancing from Unformable Bone to Drought Demon.

But becoming a true Drought Demon wasn’t so simple; rejected by heaven and earth, only by undergoing the Heavenly Tribulation, Earth Tribulation, and Human Tribulation could one truly become a Drought Demon.

Five hundred years ago, tribulation clouds appeared in the tomb chamber, striking her to near death; fortunately, she survived, this was the Heavenly Tribulation.

The Earth Tribulation was the Mountain Suppressing Seal pressing on the coffin; only by removing it could she pass through the Earth Tribulation.

With the Mountain Suppressing Seal stolen by a grave robber, she was left with only the Human Tribulation.

"My current Realm is at the Half-step Tribulation Crossing Stage, once I emerge, I should encounter a Cultivator of similar strength; only by defeating this Cultivator can I transcend the Human Tribulation!"

The female Drought Demon chuckled, this was her domain, no one below the Tribulation Crossing Stage could defeat her unless they used the Mountain Suppressing Seal!

...

"If you ask me, our approach was all wrong; if looking for ghosts, then of course we should search in the cemetery," Constable Lu analyzed earnestly.

"Your words are sensible," Constable Meng nodded, agreeing with his colleague’s opinion.

Wandering around the town in the dead of night and not even encountering a ghost’s shadow, it was better to take proactive action rather than passively wait.

Two new constables, eager to prove themselves, resolved to handle the unexplained haunting incident no matter what.

"I heard there’s a deserted area on the outskirts of Quyi County that’s often used for burials; perhaps there are some aggrieved spirits there."

The two immediately agreed and headed to the cemetery on the outskirts.

Normally, a cemetery was a place of intense Yin Energy, and any mortal who was physically weak could be possessed upon visiting.

Meng Jingzhou arrived and yawned, his breath slightly escaping him, instantly dispersing the Yin Energy.

"Hey, focus on the job," Lu Yang reminded, "We’re in uniform, representing the Dynasty; we don’t want any ghosts thinking we are slacking off."

Meng Jingzhou snorted, "Mind your own business."

As the two bickered, the ground suddenly shifted and a burst of Qi soared into the sky, terrifyingly intense!

A stunningly beautiful woman appeared before them; although her complexion was pale, her beauty remained undeniable.

Bathed in the moonlight, the Drought Demon delightfully discovered that the Yin Energy was stronger than before the seal, which could help her grow even stronger.

She immediately set her sights on the two on-duty constables.

"Two little constables, huh? It’s been a while since I’ve fed, I’ll start with you!"

The Drought Demon released a terrifying aura, making the two constables tremble in fear.

A Drought Demon appears, wreaking havoc over thousands of miles!

Death Qi spread from her feet, leaving nothing alive in its wake.

This Death Qi extended a full two fathoms before stopping near Lu Yang’s feet.

However, the Drought Demon was so focused on Meng Jingzhou that she didn’t notice this anomaly.

Opening her mouth, she bit down on Meng Jingzhou’s neck, only to scream in agony and roll on the ground in pain.

"Who are you, how can you possess such terrifying Yang Qi!"

The Drought Demon’s pain was heart-wrenching; normally, Yang Qi would nourish her, but this individual’s Yang Qi was too abundant and intense.

"It seems that I’ve heard the Grand Elder mention that this creature is a Drought Demon," Meng Jingzhou jabbed at the Drought Demon, causing sizzling smoke wherever the touch, the Pure Yang Qi corroding her.

Such intense Yang Qi, truly her nemesis; indeed, crossing the Human Tribulation wasn’t easy, was it?

Determined, the Drought Demon knew that becoming a true Drought Demon wouldn’t be easy.

If that was the case, she’d go all out!

Unreservedly, the Drought Demon released the aura of a Half-step Tribulation Crossing Stage, an aura so terrifying that even zombies must bow before her, making her the true queen of zombies!

She was the embodiment of disaster!

"Come then, let’s see what capabilities you have!"

"Stop shouting, behave yourself," Meng Jingzhou impatiently pinned her down, thinking they had found the source of the ghosts only to discover it was a Drought Demon.

"Transcendence Tribulation Stage?" The Drought Demon panicked, easily subdued by someone of only a Great Power level!

"What do you think, shall we kill her?" Lu Yang drew the Green Peak Sword, his first time facing a real Drought Demon; it seemed respectful to use the sword.

"Another Transcendence Tribulation period? And a Sword Cultivator?"

This must be a joke, one with Yang Qi enough to suppress her and another whose attacking methods are unmatched among Sword Cultivators. She thought the Human Tribulation was difficult, but she didn’t expect it to be this challenging.

How long had she slept? Were Transcendence Stage beings now working as constables?

"Don’t kill me, I surrender!" The Drought Demon hurriedly kowtowed and admitted her faults, realizing she couldn’t win against these two.

"Do you see that? Isn’t she wearing clothes from the Corpse Chasing Sect? She must be a lost zombie from their flock," Meng Jingzhou queried, noting the tattered clothes that barely revealed their origin.

"Really? Should we send her back to the Corpse Chasing Sect and let them deal with her?"

Just as the Drought Demon felt relieved about surviving, she heard Lu Yang throw a damper.

"Not so simple, her Cultivation Level is such that the Corpse Chasing Sect might not be able to hold her, and if she escapes from their custody, that’d be problematic."

"I won’t run, I promise I won’t run!" The Drought Demon quickly said, seeing Lu Yang hadn’t sheathed the Green Peak Sword yet and revealed her vulnerability outright.

"There’s a magic artifact called the Mountain Suppressing Seal; with it, I can’t escape!"

"Is that true? Where is the Mountain Suppressing Seal then?" Lu Yang was skeptical.

"I can sense that the Mountain Suppressing Seal that originally sealed me has been taken away; it went in that direction!" the Drought Demon pointed.

Following her direction, Lu Yang and his companion checked the surroundings and recalled the map, "That direction, isn’t that Guyuan County?"

Chapter 1221: 1220: Valued by Ineffable Officials

Lu Baqian held a Peach Wood Sword, surrounded by numerous magic artifacts enshrouded in a thick fog, all designed to combat zombies.

Magic artifacts floated nearby for easy activation, and the thick fog helped conceal their forms from the Drought Demon.

During his flight here, Lu Baqian thought through many strategies, determined to restrain the Drought Demon here and prevent it from slaughtering the innocent!

Using his compass, he arrived at the cemetery on the outskirts of Quyi County, only to find empty coffins before him.

"Damn, I’m too late, it has escaped!" Lu Baqian’s face looked particularly unpleasant; with the Drought Demon free, endless troubles would follow.

After a careful observation, Lu Baqian was puzzled, "But the emergence of a Drought Demon scorches the earth for thousands of miles, so why does this place still have vitality?"

Logically, after a Drought Demon emerges, it would unconsciously release its domain, strip vitality, and turn the area into a dead land.

"And isn’t this Chaotic Tomb Ridge, why is there no Yin Energy at all? Was it absorbed by the Drought Demon?"

...

"Control the domain a bit, release just a hint of Death Qi and see," Constable Meng said unapologetically.

"Yes, yes, I will control it well," the female demon replied with a forced smile, her Death Qi domain was automatically released, and controlling the leakage of Death Qi required a lot of energy.

A person has to bow under the eaves, if she dared release Death Qi, these two Tribulation Crossing Great Powers would respond with Yang Qi and Sword Qi.

"Is it this direction?"

"Yes, we are very close to the Mountain Seal now."

...

"Lord Zhang, congratulations are in order, soon you’ll be promoted to the County Magistrate of Zhongshan County."

Lord Zhang blew away the steam from his teacup casually and asked indifferently, "Wangzhi, seeking me out so late, it must be about more than just this, right?"

"Nothing much else, just that I happened upon a treasure, quite delighted, and thought to share the appreciation with Lord Zhang."

"What kind of treasure?"

Wangzhi took out a Big Seal wrapped in red cloth from his bosom and pushed it in front of Lord Zhang.

Lord Zhang picked up the Big Seal and read the two big characters on the bottom: "Mountain Suppressing?"

"This seal is truly remarkable, look at this craftsmanship, these patterns, surely made using the long-lost Ninety-nine Stages of Refining Technique!"

"Absolutely, the pattern even hides the words ’Wang An’, a Divine Craftsman’s signature, absolutely genuine, definitely a work of the Divine Craftsman Wang Family!"

"However, for nearly a thousand years, the Divine Craftsman Wang Family hasn’t produced any notable figures, heavily declined, the artifacts passed down are decent but far from their peak works."

"Your Lordship has insight, according to my research, this Mountain Seal is indeed the peak work of the Divine Craftsman Wang Family."

Hearing ’Mountain Seal’, Lord Zhang became even more enamored with the Big Seal: "Indeed, this is the Mountain Seal, known for its power to suppress mountains and evil."

Seeing Lord Zhang’s reaction, Wangzhi knew the matter was settled.

Lord Zhang did not care for Spirit Stones or magic artifacts, only showing interest in Feng Shui, evident from the arrangement of his house.

This had been specially arranged by the people from the Heavenly Strategy Sect, facing south with its back against the mountain rocks, harnessing the Spiritual Energy of the mountains and rivers, and capturing the essence of the sun and the moon. The front of the house was open, and from a distance, one could see the water in the courtyard pond, signifying the gathering and retaining of wind and Qi.

Not to mention the relationships the Heavenly Strategy Sect’s people had used, or how many Spirit Stones they had spent, just take these mountain rocks and pond water—though seemingly ordinary, the mountain rocks were actually brought back from the mountains of the Demon Territory, and the water of the pond was melted from the Extreme Northern Lands.

He had discovered that the people from the Heavenly Strategy Sect had said that to achieve the best Feng Shui layout, it was ideal to use a Mountain Suppressing Seal with the power to suppress evil as a magic artifact.

Ordinary magic artifacts couldn’t catch the eye of Lord Zhang, only the best would be used.

He happened to find a record in ancient tomes, dating back five hundred years, where tomb raiders found a hidden tomb beneath the cemetery in the neighboring county. On the coffin, there was a Mountain Seal left; just as he thought to retrieve the Seal, dark clouds formed above the coffin and lightning struck out of nowhere, hitting the coffin and scaring away the tomb raiders.

Lord Zhang laughed heartily, "The people from the Heavenly Strategy Sect really are as prescient as gods, they said it would surely catch the attention of an incredibly prestigious official, eliminating any worries about my career from then on!"

Having thought that it would take ten or twenty years to be promoted to County Magistrate, now being able to advance ahead of time, he probably had already caught the eye of that incredibly prestigious official.

"Originally, I was hesitating whether to recommend you or Dinghai for the position of County Magistrate after I leave, but now it seems Dinghai, though capable, is straightforward and lacks adaptability. Promoting him might hurt him. Wangzhi, you are cunning and perceptive, so you should climb higher."

"Wangzhi thanks Lord Zhang for his recognition of my merits!"

Just as Wangzhi prepared to take his leave, suddenly three figures appeared in the room, two men and one woman.

Lu Yang drew his sword and aimed it at the two people, "Put your hands on the table and don’t move. We are constables from Quyi County, we’ve been watching you for a long time. The bribery you’re involved in has already been recorded by the Recording Orb; you’re under arrest!"

Initially, hearing that these two were just constables, Lord Zhang was unconcerned, but when he heard Lu Yang mentioning the Recording Orb, his expression drastically changed, for it could be used as leverage against him!

"You dare!"

Lord Zhang’s shadow split into two shadow wolves, which bit at the constables’ shadows—an extremely rare Shadow Divine Ability, unpreventable.

"Don’t think about harming anyone!"

The female Dry Corpse immediately stepped in front of the two men, stomping on one of the shadow wolves, and since Lord Zhang was connected to the shadow wolf, he felt as if his chest was being crushed, making it hard for him to breathe.

The female Dry Corpse excitedly turned to Lu Yang, "Sirs, does this count as a merit for me?"

"Who are you people to dare arrest me!"

Meng Jingzhou rolled his eyes, annoyed, and slapped Lord Zhang’s old face, "If you’re deaf, how can you still be a County Magistrate? Didn’t we just say, we are constables from Quyi County."

"I’ll have you know, I’ve already been noticed by that prestigious official, and no longer worry about my career path. My promotion to County Magistrate this time was that official’s doing, if you provoke me, you are opposing that official!"

"What a mess."

...

Early in the morning, Lu Yang and his companion originally wanted to wake Man Gu, but hearing the snoring sound coming from the room—Man Gu was sleeping soundly—it seemed inhumane to wake him, so they left a note overturned at the room’s doorway.

After a while, the secretary, carrying an official decree, also placed it at the doorstep of Man Gu’s room.

Rubbing his eyes, Man Gu opened the room door and saw two papers on the chair by the door, yawning as he picked them up to read.

"The decree states that the position of County Magistrate has been finalized and is assigned to Zhang Tongheng of Guyuan County."

"From Senior Brother, let me see, they caught a tomb raider last night, also stumbled upon the disposing process, the matter was urgent, they’ve already arrested the person, asking me to issue an arrest warrant now. The arrest warrant’s content is all prepared, just missing my signature?"

Man Gu unfolded the arrest warrant left by Lu Yang and his companion.

"Strange, how is this person also named Zhang Tongheng?"

Chapter 1222: 1221: The County Magistrate Position is Vacant Again

"How is this Mountain Suppressing Seal used?" Lu Yang weighed the Seal in his hand and asked the Dry Corpse.

"There’s a corresponding mantra; as long as you know the mantra, you can control me..." The Dry Corpse didn’t dare to play tricks and truthfully recited the mantra.

Lu Yang read it once, and when he had recited half of it, he saw the Dry Corpse painfully clutching her head.

"Can you two believe me now?" After finishing the incantation, the Dry Corpse asked cautiously. She still couldn’t understand why her Human Tribulation was so powerful.

Worried that the Dry Corpse was acting, Lu Yang silently recited the mantra in his mind again. The Dry Corpse reacted just the same, and only then did Lu Yang believe her words.

"So, you’re saying anyone who knows the mantra and holds the Mountain Suppressing Seal can control you?"

"Holding the Mountain Suppressing Seal and reciting the mantra can only give me a headache and cannot truly control me. To really control me, you need to drop blood to recognize the master and make a contract with the Seal."

Lu Yang and his companion nodded; it didn’t sound like a lie.

"Let’s go, let’s take her to the Corpse Chasing Sect."

...

"Why hasn’t the Sect Leader returned?" Zhao Po anxiously waited at the entrance of the Corpse Chasing Sect.

The Sect Leader not returning all night long was not a good sign.

Whether the Sect Leader had fallen or the Drought Demon had escaped, either would cause great turmoil.

Three specks rapidly moved across the sky, catching Zhao Po’s attention.

Zhao Po squinted hard to see. Finally, he recognized who the three specks were.

"Senior Brother Lu! Senior Brother Meng!" Zhao Po waved his hands excitedly and called out. He didn’t recognize the shabbily dressed woman sandwiched between the two men.

"So, it’s Junior Brother Zhao." Lu Yang landed with a smile. The last time they came to the Wild State, the first powerful sect disciple they met was Zhao Po. Zhao Po had accompanied them all the way to the Corpse Chasing Sect and even joined them on a mission to eradicate a Water Ghost; they had shared quite a bond.

Back then, Old Meng and he were both at the Golden Core Stage. So many years had flashed by.

"Why are you here? And this female Daoist, how shall we address her... no, now is not the time for this. A Drought Demon has emerged, disaster has struck, and the Sect Leader went to deal with it. He hasn’t come back all night. Please, Senior Brothers, go help!" Zhao Po hastily spoke. Now was not the time for reminiscing.

The Dry Corpse panicked upon hearing this: "Don’t spout nonsense, the Drought Demon is perfectly safe!"

She had to rush; to save her life, she had given up all the means to control herself. If this youngster blabbed more, causing the two esteemed ones to form a bad impression of her, how could she survive?

"How could I talk nonsense about such a serious matter? I stayed up all night looking through ancient texts and sought advice from every person I could find!"

"How could I talk nonsense about such a serious matter? I am the Drought Demon!"

"Even if you are the Drought Demon... wait, who did you say you are?"

Zhao Po finally noticed that although the Dry Corpse’s clothes were tattered, they were indeed the garments of their Corpse Chasing Sect.

"You, you are that Drought Demon?!"

Zhao Po’s eyes widened as he looked at Lu Yang and the other man, seeking a reasonable explanation.

Lu Yang scratched his head, somewhat vexed. This matter seemed pretty straightforward; there wasn’t much to explain: "Uh, it’s just that we happened to encounter her. Seeing that she was wearing the clothes of the Corpse Chasing Sect, we thought we should bring her to you for handling."

"We can’t handle her at all," Zhao Po said quickly. Even the Sect Leader, the first under the Transcendance Tribulation Stage, had less than a thirty percent chance of victory against the Dry Corpse. If it caused chaos in their custody, what could they do?

"No need to worry about that; we have the Mountain Suppressing Seal." Lu Yang explained how to use the Seal, and Zhao Po finally breathed a sigh of relief.

"Then when the Sect Leader returns, shall we have the Sect Leader hold the Seal?"

"We both have business to attend to, so why don’t you take the Seal and drop blood to recognize mastery? After all, you’re at the Void Refinement Stage and lacking life-saving methods."

Zhao Po could not win against Lu Yang and reluctantly accepted the Dry Corpse, taking the Seal and performing the blood rituals to recognize mastery.

To make Lu Yang and his companion feel at ease, the Dry Corpse even sweetly called out "Master."

"Junior Brother Zhao, strive hard in your cultivation; we’ll catch up another time." Lu Yang and his companion waved goodbye.

"By the way, the Grand Elder seems quite interested in the Drought Demon, we could write him a letter and ask him to come over and have a look."

After Lu Yang and his companion left, a worried Lu Baqian flew back to the sect.

He had searched all night and had not found any trace of the Drought Demon.

"The Drought Demon is so secretive and patient, it must be tough to deal with."

There was some good news, though; he had stumbled upon the heir of the Divine Craftsman Wang family, but unfortunately, his cultivation level was too low to refine the Mountain Suppressing Seal that could pose a threat to the Drought Demon.

But with the blueprints for the Mountain Suppressing Seal in hand, Lu Baqian could use the drawings to unleash part of the seal’s power and catch the Drought Demon off guard.

In high-level cultivator combat, a single oversight could lead to total defeat!

"Sect Leader, you’ve returned safely!" Zhao Po was still waiting at the sect entrance for Lu Baqian, his smile beaming with joy.

"Unfortunately, I didn’t find the Drought Demon... I’m going to face the Drought Demon?!"

Lu Baqian noticed the female Dry Corpse standing obediently by Zhao Po’s side, like a human puppet, and was startled.

Seeing that the Sect Leader was scared and about to take action, Zhao Po hurriedly stepped forward to explain the situation clearly.

Although he was also unclear about the specific details.

"So it was Senior Brother Lu and Senior Brother Meng Jingzhou who took action."

"Wait a moment, does this mean you are now the master of the female Dry Corpse, and she is controllable?"

"Let’s see who is stronger between us!" Lu Baqian said excitedly, having thought about how to deal with the female Dry Corpse all night, he didn’t want that effort to go to waste.

After seeking Zhao Po’s agreement, the female Dry Corpse willingly accepted Lu Baqian’s challenge.

The two Half-step Transcendence Tribulation Stage cultivators quickly engaged in battle, with entire mountain ranges crumbling as easily as paper punctured by a probe.

With Lu Yang and his companion no longer there to restrain her, the female Dry Corpse revealed her dominant and invincible side, and Lu Baqian was soon at a disadvantage.

The female Dry Corpse was even more excited than Lu Baqian. Right, this is the Human Tribulation I should be facing.

"Indeed tough to deal with..." Lu Baqian wiped the blood from his mouth; with the female Dry Corpse releasing her Death Qi domain, he was distracted fending off the Death Qi and was overwhelmed by her relentless attacks.

"But there is a way, Mountain Suppressing Seal!"

Lu Baqian executed the Mountain Suppressing Seal with his magical spells and divine abilities, but the female Dry Corpse casually released her own seal mantra against it, resulting in both mantras exploding in the air.

"How come you’re not scared of the Mountain Suppressing Seal?" Lu Baqian asked, perplexed. This shouldn’t be the case.

The female Dry Corpse said faintly, "What could be scarier than being ambushed by Sword Cultivators and Pure Yang Transcendence Tribulation Stage cultivators the moment you come into existence?"

...

An emergency meeting of the higher-ups in Wild State.

"Why did Zhang Tongheng get captured by Man Gu right after we just officially announced his promotion to County Magistrate?" When the upper echelons of Wild State heard this news, their reactions weren’t much better than before, remaining stunned for several seconds before accepting the reality.

"It’s outrageous, it’s like they are completely disregarding our resolve!" Mr. Song slammed the table in anger, having served as an official for centuries, he had never encountered such an absurd situation.

"If you ask me, we should be thanking Man Gu for rooting out the troublemaker."

"What do you mean rooting out the troublemaker, he’s just acting recklessly because he’s an Immortal Sect disciple!"

Seeing the group divided into two fervently disputing factions, Governor Lv frowned with dissatisfaction, "Enough, let’s set aside Man Gu’s case for now. The position of County Magistrate of Zhongshan County is vacant again, who should take the position?"

"How about selecting another one from among the County Magistrates under Zhongshan County?"

"That won’t work, the rest lack the seniority to command respect."

"What does Lord Lv suggest, then?"

Governor Lv stroked his chin, pondering the appropriate candidate, and slowly said, "We could assign an internal official from the state to take over as County Magistrate of Zhongshan County. I find Gong Cao Zhou Hai to be sharp-witted, able to read people and situations, and skilled at managing personnel, suitable for this position."

Chapter 1223: 1222: The Consequences of Eavesdropping

"Lord Governor Zhou Hai has taken up his post and requires all county magistrates in Zhongshan County to attend a meeting tomorrow?" Man Gu, who had been busy all day and had just gotten off work, received the notice.

He was currently dealing with the affairs of both the previous and the one before that governor and had been very busy the past few days.

Fortunately, with the help of two senior brothers here, he was able to leave work on time.

"Finally, a new governor has arrived," Man Gu sighed. It hadn’t been easy with two changes of governors in just a few days.

After thinking it through, Man Gu informed Lu Yang and the other about the meeting and hoped they could join him.

Typically, whenever a county magistrate stepped out, they would bring along attendants or government officials to take care of and protect the magistrate.

"Joining the meeting as well? Indeed, Junior Brother Man, you’re currently in the Void Refinement Stage. It’s alright within our local area, but outside the county town, you might encounter dangers."

This meant that Lu Yang and the other would be busy hunting for female ghosts on their night shifts and wouldn’t rest even after the night shift ended, as they would accompany Man Gu to the meeting the following day.

Although those in the Transcendance Tribulation Stage wouldn’t feel tired even without rest for a hundred and eighty days, Man Gu was still very touched.

...

In Quyi County, where governance flowed smoothly, the two newly appointed constables encountered nothing throughout their night shift and afterwards, accompanied Man Gu to Zhongshan County.

A few days ago, Lu Yang and his companion had come to Zhongshan County to resolve issues with the Hundred Sword Alliance, but it had been night then, and the streets were empty and silent.

Arriving in Zhongshan County during the day, it was bustling, and although Quyi County had thrived under Man Gu’s governance over the past years, it still couldn’t compare to Zhongshan County.

At the entrance of the Governor Mansion, they encountered several county magistrates who were arriving for the meeting. Some were in sedans, others in horse carriages, all looking quite spectacular. Only Man Gu and his companions were walking.

Despite Man Gu having walked here, no county magistrate dared to underestimate him. As they descended from their sedans or carriages, they all greeted Man Gu with smiling faces.

Capturing two governors within three days, with the second governor being imprisoned on the day he assumed office—such a feat was indeed terrifying.

"Lord Man, it’s been a while and you still look as distinguished as ever."

"Lord Man indeed handles affairs decisively and cannot tolerate the slightest fault in his eyes, truly a role model for us!"

"Disciple of Dao Seeking Sect, Golden Science Top Scorer—Lord Man, possessing both titles, is truly enviable."

Man Gu courteously responded, "In the future, I must exchange more ideas with all of you lords."

Upon hearing this, the other county magistrates hurriedly entered the Governor Mansion, hoping not to ’exchange ideas’ further, lest trouble arises from nowhere.

Man Gu scratched his head, puzzled—he had delivered the responses just as the secretary had instructed, so why were their reactions different from what the secretary said?

"Never mind, let’s go in." Man Gu was always broad-minded; not understanding something wasn’t worth overthinking and straining his brain.

Upon entering the Governor’s Mansion, Man Gu went into the council hall, while Lu Yang and the other, as attendants, could only stand guard outside.

Along with Lu Yang and his companion outside, there were other attendants and government officials brought by the other county magistrates, so they were not lonely.

"That’s quite supportive of you two senior brothers. Who knows how long this new governor’s meeting will last. Perhaps you two seniors can take a stroll around the county."

Lu Yang waved his hand and laughed, "No need, no need. We came specifically to protect you; we can’t leave."

Inside the council hall, Man Gu had just sat down when he was approached by an unfamiliar county magistrate.

"Lord Man, thank you."

"And you are?"

"I am Yang Dinghai, the newly appointed county magistrate of Guyuan County."

"So you are Lord Yang. But why the thanks?" Man Gu asked kindly with clasped hands.

"I hardly deserve to be called lord. Originally, Zhang Tongheng became the governor, leaving the position of county magistrate of Guyuan County vacant. Both I and Liu Wangzhi had a chance to become the county magistrate. However, Liu Wangzhi was adept at ingratiating himself with Zhang Tongheng, and the likelihood of him becoming the county magistrate was higher. But, Lord Man, you had Zhang Tongheng and Liu Wangzhi arrested, giving me the opportunity to assume the position."

Man Gu realized, "I see, it’s merely my duty; there’s no need for thanks."

"Lord Man, if you ever need my help in the future, just say the word, and I will do my utmost to assist."

The two chatted for a while, and Man Gu found it strange, "Speaking of which, we’ve been talking for so long, why hasn’t the County Magistrate arrived yet?"

The two senior brothers were waiting outside, and he was eager to wrap up the meeting early.

Not only was Man Gu waiting a long time, but the other county officials also felt it was a bit long, wondering why County Magistrate Zhou was late to his first meeting.

"Is this a display of authority to those newly in charge?"

...

A man stood outside the council hall, from his attire, he was clearly the County Magistrate.

County Magistrate Zhou, hearing the murmurs inside the hall, cracked a smile.

While in Wild State, he held the position of Gong Cao, responsible for personnel movement, wielding immense power, and even the County Magistrates were always courteous around him.

His assignment in Zhongshan State as the County Magistrate was merely a formality, a gilding of his resume, until he was eligible for a natural promotion to a higher grade.

His achievements today were inseparable from his cultivation technique.

His cultivation technique was a bit special, akin to the Buddhist telepathy, but not as powerful; it only allowed him to eavesdrop on conversations. Astute by nature, he could decipher relationships from these conversations, sowing discord or thriving on both sides, thus profiting from the situation.

The reason he was late was that he wanted to listen to the county officials’ conversation inside the council hall, as well as the exchanges among the government officials outside, seeking useful intelligence.

"It’s about time to go in," said County Magistrate Zhou with a smile, regretting not having heard the conversation between the two people brought by Man Gu.

Suddenly, the conversation of the two brought by Man Gu reached his ears.

"Mid-heaven Emperor Lord’s ’Devouring Dao Fruit’ seems powerful, it can even absorb other people’s Dao Fruits. Doesn’t that mean he could possess several Dao Fruits?"

"Guan Shanhai’s ’Imprinting Dao Fruit’ is also formidable; given the chance, he could possibly create a new world."

"I wonder what kind of Dao Fruit we can condense and what extraordinary effects it might possess."

County Magistrate Zhou was momentarily puzzled; what had just happened? He felt like he was missing a piece of his memory.

"It’s about time to go in," County Magistrate Zhou said with a smile, regretting not having heard the conversation of the two people brought by Man Gu.

The conversation content brought by Man Gu entered his ears.

"Yingtian Immortal’s senior ’Tribulation Dao Fruit’ controls the Heavenly Tribulation, just thinking about it feels majestic."

"Immortal Jiuchong’s senior Yin-Yang Dao Fruit uses the world as a chessboard, bestowing Yin Yang attributes, annihilating everything."

"I wonder which of the two seniors is more formidable."

"Why does the County Magistrate keep eavesdropping on our conversation? Can he understand it? The Dao Fruit knowledge has a forgetting effect, right?"

"I don’t know, let him listen if he wants, he won’t remember it anyway."

County Magistrate Zhou was momentarily puzzled again; what had just happened? He felt like he was missing a piece of his memory.

"It’s about time to go in."

"This Dao Fruit..."

...

"Why hasn’t the County Magistrate come yet?" Man Gu grew increasingly anxious, having waited from noon until the afternoon, nearly three hours passing by.

The other county officials also had their complaints about County Magistrate Zhou.

"I’ll go find the County Magistrate."

Man Gu opened the large doors of the council hall and saw County Magistrate Zhou, standing dazed.

"Lord Governor, why are you standing here?"

On his first day in office, County Magistrate Zhou was meant to show his authority, but instead, he ended up standing outside the council hall, punished for nearly three hours.

Chapter 1224: 123 Barely Retained the Title of Number One Below the Transcendance Tribulation Stage

The constables outside the meeting hall all witnessed County Magistrate Zhou being made to stand at the door for over two hours, and they transmitted messages to their respective County Magistrates, who began to look at Zhou with increasingly bemused eyes.

Is this some strange kind of hobby?

But even if he liked standing at the door, he couldn’t expect them to wait from noon until now.

Even Zhou, who had exceptional interpersonal skills and a top-notch mental resilience, felt embarrassed in this situation.

"There was a little accident during practice; let’s start the meeting," Zhou said lightly, then walked inside.

"Lord Governor, it’s time to finish work for the day," Man Gu kindly reminded him.

Zhou’s face changed color repeatedly. Although he was at fault, being persuaded by Man Gu in just a few words not to hold the meeting was a blow to his dignity.

With a stern voice, he said, "Lord Man, if we don’t hold the meeting today, we will have to continue tomorrow. Are you suggesting that all the colleagues come here again from their counties tomorrow? Are you trying to waste everyone’s time?"

Man Gu was puzzled, "But wasn’t it you who wasted our time by standing at the door and not coming in?"

Faced with the truth, Zhou had no defense against Man Gu and ordered, "We must have the meeting today!"

"Oh," Man Gu sat down obediently.

Seeing Man Gu’s indifferent attitude, Zhou’s blood pressure rose; he had never encountered such a person in his official career!

He made an effort to adjust his mentality and chaired the meeting.

In reality, it was just a simple introductory meeting. After all, he was new to the job and unfamiliar with the place; he couldn’t rashly decide the development direction of Zhongshan County.

At the end of the meeting, Zhou asked, "Does anyone else have something to say?"

No one responded.

Zhou scanned the room and fixed his gaze on Man Gu, who seemed eager to leave, "Lord Man, do you have anything to say?"

Man Gu shook his head, remembering the notes the secretary had written, "No."

Seeing that Man Gu was somewhat sensible and understood the rules of bureaucracy, Zhou’s tone softened a bit, "Lord Man, don’t be constrained. Here, you can speak freely."

Still, Man Gu shook his head, "Don’t believe it; those who say such things are liars, very hypocritical, can’t be trusted."

This was the explanation the secretary had given Man Gu about when a superior says to speak but not really to speak—all direct quotes.

Zhou clenched his fists tightly, his century-long cultivation almost breaking right there. He managed to say calmly, "Lord Man really has a sense of humor."

"I’m not joking, though."

Zhou stared at Man Gu intently, wishing he could swallow him whole.

It seemed that Man Gu had his eye on the position of County Magistrate, having captured the Governor twice already, but he didn’t expect himself to become the Magistrate, harboring resentment and repeatedly embarrassing him during the meeting.

Through clenched teeth, Zhou managed to utter, "...Meeting adjourned."

Lu Yang and Meng Jingzhou were curious about Man Gu’s performance during the meeting, observing the hall with their Spiritual Sense. Seeing this scene, they exchanged glances, shaking their heads in amusement.

"Truly worthy of Junior Brother Man," Lu Yang thought that after they returned, the secretary would have to add more notes for Man Gu.

Dealing with a superior like Man Gu was certainly not easy for the secretary.

"Hey, is there really anything funny about this?" Fairy Eternity, who was also paying attention to Man Gu, felt that Man Gu had done quite well in the meeting.

"Open and honest, says what’s on his mind, very much in the spirit of his ancestors."

Lu Yang held his forehead, thankful the Fairy hadn’t involved herself in officialdom. No, that’s not right, she had already reached the highest echelons of it.

The Fairy couldn’t be relied upon, after all. It seemed he and Old Meng, two good Senior Brothers, would have to take more care of Man Gu.

After the meeting, the three of them had no intention of lingering in Zhongshan County. Lu Yang and his companion still had patrolling duties that night.

Man Gu returned to his room in the Government Office to rest, while Lu Yang and his companion, invigorated, wandered around Quyi County and encountered someone unexpected.

"Master Ru?" Lu Yang and his companion were surprised. What was Master Ru doing here? Could it be that he didn’t know the Drought Demon was already at the Corpse Chasing Sect, and he was still searching for it here?

Lu Baqian was even more surprised. "Are you two the constables here?"

"That’s right, Man Gu is the County Magistrate here, and you two have come to serve as his Protectors."

Lu Baqian remembered that Man Gu was the County Magistrate of Quyi County.

"Isn’t this just a chance encounter with a scion of the Divine Craftsman Wang Family? In order to contend with the Drought Demon, I rushed to get the forging methods for the Mountain Seal from them and didn’t have time to repay them."

"The young blacksmith Wang has decent talents; the only issue is his young age. Once he’s older, he can bring my token to join our Corpse Chasing Sect, or he could go to another Sect."

"I wouldn’t dare speak for distant places, but the Sects of the Wild State would definitely give me face."

In the Wild State, every Sect knew the title of the number one person beneath the Pass Tribulation Stage, and none would dare not to give him face.

"By the way, I must thank the two Martial Nephews for delivering the female Drought Demon to our Corpse Chasing Sect."

"Master Ru, you are too kind. The female Drought Demon was originally a zombie of your esteemed Sect. We just happened to encounter her while on patrol. She has simply returned to her rightful owner."

"Still, I am grateful. She has exceptional combat capabilities and has become a significant asset to our Corpse Chasing Sect. I’ve exchanged blows with that female Drought Demon, and I’ve barely managed to maintain my title as the first person beneath the Transcendance Tribulation Stage."

"Does that mean you defeated the female Drought Demon, Master Ru?"

"No, she defeated me and then advanced to the Transcendance Tribulation Stage."

"..."

Lu Baqian had been bedridden with a serious injury and had gone over the battle with the female Drought Demon many times, arriving at a conclusion:

He could have won with the Mountain Seal keeping the female Drought Demon at bay, but since she had encountered Lu Yang, her courage had grown, and she was no longer intimidated.

"Then congratulations on your Sect’s advancement to Super-grade."

A Sect could be considered Super-grade if it had a Pass Tribulation Cultivator in its ranks, and the female Drought Demon could probably be counted as a Pass Tribulation Cultivator for the Corpse Chasing Sect... right?

Discussing this made Lu Baqian very happy. With him present, their Corpse Chasing Sect was considered half a step into Super-grade. With the female Drought Demon joining, they had finally officially become a Super-grade Sect, eligible for tax reduction policies from the Dynasty.

Lu Baqian felt that Lu Yang and his companion were indeed lucky charms. They had come to the Corpse Chasing Sect during their Golden Core Stage, and then he had advanced to the Half-step Tribulation Crossing Stage. This time they had brought a Transcendance Tribulation Stage female Drought Demon. Every visit seemed to double the strength of the Corpse Chasing Sect.

...

"To choose whether to join the Corpse Chasing Sect or another Sect?" The young blacksmith Wang faced the choice given by Lu Baqian with much distress.

Old Wang the blacksmith was also struggling to make a decision for his son.

"My child, don’t rush, you’re not yet sixteen. You can take your time to think," Lu Baqian said.

The young blacksmith Wang furrowed his brows deeply in contemplation. He had been pondering one question all along: "You said that the Corpse Chasing Sect excels at manipulating zombies and feeding them with one’s own blood."

"Then, my Bloodthirsty Evil Sword, being an inanimate object that grows stronger with bloodlust, wouldn’t that also be considered capable of controlling zombies?"

Lu Baqian fell silent for a moment, then said earnestly, "My child, join our Corpse Chasing Sect. What we lack in our Sect is precisely your kind of thinking."

Chapter 1225: 1224: Old, Can’t Keep Up with The Times

Zhongshan County, Governor Mansion’s rooftop.

The Governor Mansion was the tallest building in Zhongshan County. Standing here, County Magistrate Zhou could see the entirety of Zhongshan County’s beautiful scenery without any obstruction.

He reached out as if trying to grasp the nearby street scene, as though he could control the entire Zhongshan County with just a stretch of his hand.

The cool night breeze brushed through, stirring his thoughts.

He recalled Man Gu’s performance during the meeting tonight and could not help but clench his fists tightly, making them creak.

"With Man Gu here, this Zhongshan County is probably not mine alone."

"Sir, Man Gu was really disrespectful to you at the meeting," Zhou Hai’s personal guard stood behind him, expressing indignation on his behalf.

County Magistrate Zhou shook his head and laughed it off, "After all, he is a disciple from the Seek Dao Sect and the current top scorer. It’s normal for him to be arrogant and not take someone like me, the county magistrate, seriously."

A hint of coldness flashed through County Magistrate Zhou’s eyes, "But he also needs to know that it took me a hundred years to rise to this position today, and it was certainly not by being kind-hearted!"

"Even if he is a disciple from the Seek Dao Sect, after all, this world ultimately belongs to Great Xia, and the Seek Dao Sect is just one of the five Immortal Sects."

"Once in the official circles of Wild State, you need to follow the rules of Wild State’s official circles."

He had connections all over the official circles of Wild State!

Thinking of this, County Magistrate Zhou subconsciously touched the Storage Ring on his middle finger, his trump card in navigating through Wild State’s official circles.

"Where is the Storage Ring?!"

County Magistrate Zhou suddenly widened his eyes; his Storage Ring had disappeared without a trace!

That was something he absolutely could not allow others to get their hands on!

...

After the night shift, on the way back to the Government Office, Lu Yang looked at the streets, which were slightly busier than usual in the early morning, and felt puzzled.

"Old Meng, have you noticed that there seem to be more outsiders coming to our county?"

Quyi County wasn’t a tourist attraction, and logically, there shouldn’t be so many outsiders.

"Indeed, there are quite a few more, and have you noticed? All these people are cultivators, from the Golden Core, Nascent Soul, to the Spirit Transformation realms."

Compared to the number of mortals, cultivators were extremely rare. The sudden influx of so many cultivators suggested that this matter was probably not so simple.

After returning to the Government Office, the two decided to inquire with Man Gu.

"Oh, the increase in the number of cultivators? It’s because there’s a Secret Realm about to open," explained Man Gu.

"We have a Secret Realm in our county?"

"No, not here. There’s a Secret Realm left behind by a cultivator from the beginning of the Great Xia period on a mountain not far from our county. It’s called the Four Treasures Secret Realm."

Man Gu walked to the courtyard, pointing towards a distant mountain. It was early morning, and a misty fog enveloped the mountain, adding a mysterious aura to it.

"It’s that mountain over there, called West Mountain. Deep in West Mountain, there lies a Secret Realm."

"The Four Treasures Secret Realm opens once every hundred years. The Secret Realm has three levels, corresponding to the Golden Core Stage, the Nascent Soul Stage, and the Spirit Transformation Stage. Cultivators from these three realms can all participate in the trials of the Secret Realm."

"The counties near West Mountain will see a rise in cultivator appearances, and it will be lively for a while."

"We’ve only just reached the Spirit Transformation Stage," lamented Lu Yang and his companion, wishing there was a Tribulation-Passing Level Secret Realm that they could try to venture into.

The highest was Spirit Transformation Stage, so they wouldn’t interfere.

"Little Yang is really naive; only an Immortal can create a Tribulation-Passing Level Secret Realm."

"That’s also true, this level of Secret Realm isn’t something you just stumble upon."

"Even if you do run into it, there’s no need to go, right?" Fairy Eternity said matter-of-factly, "After all, it’s built by Immortals. If you want the rewards from the Secret Realm, just find the Immortal who built it and ask directly for the rewards."

"Uh, that’s not necessary either." Lu Yang, with his thin skin, could not bring himself to do such a thing.

In the following days, more and more cultivators arrived in Quyi County. Although there were many cultivators, probably because they did not want to cause trouble before the opening of the Secret Realm, they all behaved themselves, and the public order in Quyi County remained as usual.

Lu Yang and another strolled on the streets during the day, occasionally hearing exclamations.

"Look, that’s Kong Wen from White Horse Temple, it’s said that he has mastered one of the Six Spiritual Powers of Buddhism, Telepathy, no schemes can hide from him!"

"Master Shi Chan from the Suspension Temple highly praises him."

Draped in a kasaya, Kong Wen held one hand upright at his chest, chanting the Diamond Sutra, seemingly oblivious to the exclamations around him.

"The Azure Dragon Saint Son from the Quadruple Sect is also here, his Azure Dragon Killing Skill is recognized as unparalleled in this age, even comparable to a Sword Cultivator!"

"The Quadruple Sect even purchased Dragon’s Blood for him. After bathing in the Dragon’s Blood, his Azure Dragon Killing Skill has reached an initial mastery. Magic artifacts can hardly harm him!"

The Azure Dragon Saint Son, a muscular man with dragon horns, was a Dragon-man, followed by many followers.

"The Eldest Senior Brother from the Tinghai Sect is here too. Ever since most of the high-ranking members of Tinghai Sect were captured, it has been in decline. It’s said that the remaining leaders abandoned their differences, stopped their backstabbing, and started nurturing him as a seed of revival!"

"There’s also the Holy Daughter from the Seven Star Holy Land, rumoured to possibly be the next Lan Ting..."

"A direct disciple of the Sect Leader from the Shura Sect..."

Lu Yang and his companion exchanged glances, seemingly not recognizing any of these people.

"We’re old, out of touch with the times." Lu Yang sighed, his back to his companion; despite the reactions of the people around, he didn’t recognize anyone, surely a sign these were all rising talents.

"Time flies, leaving no one behind." Meng Jingzhou also shook his head and sighed.

"Wait, these people are about our age, and some are even older, like this Azure Dragon Saint Son."

Meng Jingzhou, using his Spiritual Sense with a slight probe, could determine the bone age of these individuals, all peers.

Having heard Old Meng say this, Lu Yang remembered; they had seen the Azure Dragon Saint Son for the first time, but had met the White Tiger Saint Heir during their Foundation Establishment Stage at the borders of Great Xia.

At that time, Lu Yang had just learned Imitation Fist and had kindly shaved the White Tiger Saint Heir’s head.

"Peers are only at the Spirit Transformation Stage." Lu Yang had thought peers would at least be at the Void Refinement Stage.

At least the peers he saw were all at the Void Refinement Stage, like Man Gu, Li Haoran, Tao Yaoye, Lan Ting, Shi Chan, etc.

Even Zhao Po was at the Void Refinement Stage.

"Boss, two fried dough sticks please." Passing by a stand, they suddenly felt hungry.

"Ah, this is the downside of cultivating too quickly, no common language with peers." Both sighed one after another; perhaps this was the cost of reaching the Transcendance Tribulation Stage.

Kong Wen and two constables brushed past, his pupils suddenly shrinking.

Due to his Telepathy just developing, he couldn’t yet control it well. He continuously used Telepathy to hear the thoughts of those around him.

But the minds of these two constables were terrifyingly calm, completely silent to his powers!

Who are they?!

Lu Yang, eating his fried dough stick, noticed Kong Wen using Telepathy on him and flashed a profound, inscrutable smile.

The two passed by Kong Wen, who stood frozen, the sound of one of their telepathic messages echoing in his mind.

"If I were you, I wouldn’t speak of our affair to anyone."

Chapter 1226: 1225: Government Office Inspection

"Why did you send him a message?"

"To add a sense of mystery."

...

Five days later, the Secret Realm opened, and the bustling Quyi County returned to its former tranquility.

Lu Yang and his companion couldn’t be bothered to join the crowd at the Four Treasures Secret Realm, as everything happening on West Mountain was visible from the yard. The dense fog surrounding the mountain couldn’t obscure their sight at all.

They saw Cultivators forming various camps, filled with personal grudges and factional disputes, complex indeed, as if they were about to start fighting at any disagreement.

However, the Cultivators kept their wits about them and held back, opting to wait until they were inside the Secret Realm before taking any action.

As for what happened inside the Secret Realm, Lu Yang and his companion couldn’t see, but it didn’t matter much—it was just a spectacle.

After all, West Mountain wasn’t in their jurisdiction, and thus not their problem.

Two more days passed, and the Secret Realm closed.

...

"Chase! While the snipe and the clam grapple, it’s the fisherman who benefits. After all our fighting, who would have thought Ji Zhong would be the one to snatch the fruit!"

"It’s obviously you who’re targeting me on purpose!" Ji Zhong retorted angrily, skillfully evading the attacks from behind.

He clutched a ripe Spirit Fruit, the most important treasure from the Four Treasures Secret Realm. This kind of Spirit Fruit matured only once every hundred years and could improve one’s Realm by a small degree when consumed by those below the Divinity Transformation Stage.

The Azure Dragon Saint Son and others were grinding their teeth in frustration—they had come to the Four Treasures Secret Realm precisely for this Spirit Fruit!

"Dare to compete with us, a mere Loose Cultivator!"

Before the Secret Realm opened, no one paid attention to Ji Zhong, the Loose Cultivator. However, after several Divine Transformation Stage Cultivators were successively defeated, everyone realized that Ji Zhong was the dark horse of the Secret Realm, or rather, a threat.

The collective targeting of Ji Zhong had backfired, and instead, he seized the opportunity to grab the Spirit Fruit.

Only then did someone reveal Ji Zhong’s background. Although he was a Loose Cultivator, he often had incredible luck, and his Cultivation progress was faster than the disciples of the Great Sects. He also had a few experiences in fighting above his level, but those victories were so coincidental that most had assumed they were due to luck.

Now, those who knew Ji Zhong realized he had been concealing his true ability!

"We must catch him!"

Ji Zhong quickened his pace and fled West Mountain, heading straight for Zhongshan County.

It was only when he reached the county town under the jurisdiction of Zhongshan County that he noticed the problem.

Deep in the night, the town was deserted. Running to and fro so conspicuously, those tracking him could easily spot his movements.

"It seems I can only hide in this county town. Is this Quyi County?"

Ji Zhong reached a corner and was suddenly bumped by someone who seemed to be in a hurry, apologized briefly, and quickly left.

Ji Zhong’s heart tightened, and he immediately checked the Spirit Fruit.

"The Spirit Fruit is still here; it seems I was overthinking it."

Ji Zhong found a run-down inn, where the innkeeper was drowsy and nearly jumped from the startle.

"Innkeeper, I need a room. Remember, don’t tell anyone I’m here, no matter who asks!"

Ji Zhong threw down ten silvers, took the key, and hid in an upstairs room.

The Azure Dragon Saint Son and others arrived in Quyi County, frowning tightly – after all, initiating a conflict in the county town could be problematic.

"He’s hiding? Search!"

"Don’t let the government office find out about us. Cover your faces!"

...

"We’ve been crouching here for several nights, how come there is not a soul in sight?" Two constables who were behind the times strolled on the street.

"Forget it, looks like there won’t be any ghosts tonight either, let’s continue checking the inns."

Lu Yang and his companion entered the decrepit inn. Upon seeing their uniforms, the innkeeper hurriedly greeted them with a smile, "Gentlemen, what brings you here?"

"You should’ve heard from your fellow tradesmen, we’re currently checking real-name registration at inns."

This was an order from the county office, stating that there were many unregistered inns in Zhongshan County that allowed people to stay just for money, and therefore all county towns were required to investigate.

"Show us the guest list for today and let’s see if you’ve registered guests according to regulations."

The innkeeper brought out a booklet, his back breaking out in cold sweat.

This place wasn’t an unregistered inn; they always registered guests by their real names. However, he was swayed by the sight of silver and rented out a room to a guest without registration. The guest also told him not to tell anyone he was here, which hardly sounded like good news.

Lu Yang noticed the subtle changes in the innkeeper’s expression and looked at him with an almost smile, "Innkeeper, if you confess now, we’ll just give you a verbal warning and let it go. But if we find something during our search, you’ll be shut down for rectification."

"Tell us, did you register anyone without following the rules?"

The innkeeper stammered in fright, "Th-there is one."

"Where?"

"Room one with the character for ’earth’."

Lu Yang and his companion went to room one with the character for ’earth’. There wasn’t a sliver of light in the room; it seemed unoccupied.

"Anyone there? Open up."

No response.

"Government office room inspection," Lu Yang stressed his identity.

Still no response.

Meng Jingzhou, growing impatient, pushed open the door and caught Ji Zhong, who was about to escape through the window.

"So it’s you who didn’t register with your real name?"

Ji Zhong was shocked. Not wanting to cause trouble and trying to escape, how could a constable have caught him?

He was a Divine Transformation Stage Cultivator!

Meng Jingzhou dragged Ji Zhong back to the room, where Lu Yang pulled out some papers, lit a candle, and began taking notes on the table.

"Name?"

"Ji Zhong."

"Produce your identification."

"Here."

"What’s your cultivation level?"

"Initial Stage of Spirit Transformation."

"Do you know it’s illegal to check in without real-name registration?"

"I do."

"Knowing that, you did it anyway?"

"I am carrying a valuable treasure. After I came out of the Four Treasures Secret Realm, I’ve been chased..."

...

The innkeeper heaved a sigh of relief when he saw that neither Lu Yang nor his companion had any intention of blaming him after they went upstairs.

Suddenly, a stout man led several people into the inn.

The innkeeper shuddered upon seeing the stout man—the Azure Dragon Saint Son from the Quadruple Sect, whom he had seen on the street a few days prior.

The Azure Dragon Saint slapped the table, causing the innkeeper to tremble with fear.

"Innkeeper, I need some information. Has anyone checked in recently and told you not to mention they’re here?"

The innkeeper was too scared to speak and could only nod in response.

"So they are here." The Azure Dragon Saint Son smiled. Ji Zhong’s Concealing Technique was really quite clever; even with Spiritual Sense, he couldn’t be found.

"Which room is he in?"

"Third Grade number one room."

"Is anyone else in the room?"

"There... there might be two more people inside."

The Azure Dragon Saint Son snorted coldly: "I knew there were accomplices!"

But the fact that there were accomplices didn’t matter to him; they outnumbered them.

Leading his followers, the Azure Dragon Saint Son stormed up to the second floor and kicked open the door of the Third Grade number one room.

"Ji Zhong, I advise you to hand over the goods obediently!"

Inside the room, Ji Zhong sat up straight, and two constables were busy taking notes.

This scene was entirely unexpected by the Azure Dragon Saint Son.

Especially when one constable stood up and sneered, "What’s this, a robbery right before our eyes?"

Constable Meng loosened his wrists and ankles; it had been a long while since he had encountered someone this brazen.

The Azure Dragon Saint Son, not wanting to get involved with government officials, attempted to flee, but before he could turn around, Constable Meng pinned him to the ground and tied him up.

His followers were tied up along with him.

"It seems you were telling the truth. You hid in the inn anonymously to evade pursuers, so we’ll overlook your offense," Lu Yang said.

"Continue. Who else is after you?" Lu Yang continued to take notes seriously, as Ji Zhong’s status had now changed to that of a victim.

"There’s someone from the Tinghai Sect..."

At that moment, the door was kicked open again by someone else, just as furious: "Azure Dragon, I heard you’re here, you won’t beat me to it—the Spirit Fruit is mine!"

The others chasing Ji Zhong took note of the odd situation with the Azure Dragon Saint Son; having been in the rundown inn for so long without leaving, Ji Zhong was definitely here!

"He’s the Eldest Senior Brother from the Tinghai Sect," Ji Zhong identified on the spot, and Lu Yang nodded, taking the information down.

Meng Jingzhou got up, tied up the Eldest Senior Brother from the Tinghai Sect, closed the door, and returned to his seat.

"Who else?"

"There’s the Sect Leader’s closed-door disciple from the Shura Sect..."

The door was kicked open yet again: "Ji Zhong, hand over the Spirit Fruit!"

"Is that him?"

"Yes."

In no time, the geniuses who had come for the Spirit Fruit were tied up one by one by Meng Jingzhou, filling the whole room.

Meng Jingzhou shook his head helplessly at the sight; taking notes on so many robbers would take a long time.

"Check the record to see if everything you’ve said is included and let me know if there are any mistakes."

Ji Zhong’s head was in a whirl—the swift change of events caught him off guard, and he couldn’t comprehend how all the geniuses chasing him had been captured?

These were all peerless geniuses, each displaying their Divine Abilities in the Four Treasures Secret Realm, favorites to claim the prize, yet not one could withstand a single move from the constables!

Still dizzy, he checked the notes and said in confusion: "There are no mistakes."

"Then sign at the bottom of each page, and on the last page write ’I have read the above notes and they are consistent with my statement.’ Wherever you write, press your fingerprint and also on the seal creases."

"Alright."

After signing and pressing his fingerprints, Ji Zhong took out the Spirit Fruit from his chest, only to abruptly change color.

It was a fake Spirit Fruit! Someone had switched the Spirit Fruit!

It was that person who had bumped into him!

He hesitated for a moment before saying, "Gentlemen, I’ve lost my Spirit Fruit. May I report it missing?"

Chapter 1227: 1226 Instigator

"The Spirit Fruit is gone?" Lu Yang asked.

Meng Jingzhou nodded.

Why do things keep happening one after another in the middle of the night? Not only have we captured a group of robbers, but now we’ve stumbled upon a theft case?

We came here to catch ghosts. Where are the ghosts?

Normally, after receiving a report, we should take a statement, but considering that taking a statement now could delay us and potentially allow the thief to escape, we’ll have to fill out the statement after catching the thief.

"Tell us what happened; when do you think it was stolen?"

"Someone bumped into me right before I arrived at the hotel..." Meng Jingzhou shared his suspicion.

After hearing this, Lu Yang and his companion had a general understanding of the case and got up to pack their things.

"Alright, you wait here; we’re going to catch the culprit."

Meng Jingzhou opened his mouth, looking at the room full of geniuses bound by Meng Jingzhou, terrified.

With Meng Jingzhou here, these people didn’t dare do anything, but once Lu Yang and his companion showed their capabilities, merely being tied up might not be enough to hold these geniuses.

Lu Yang noticed Meng Jingzhou’s concerns, swept his gaze over the geniuses, and commanded coldly,

"[No one move]"

The intangible rule was set, and the geniuses felt something beyond ropes binding them, rendering them unable to move even a little despite their efforts.

They panicked, this power was only ever felt when facing their sect elders.

This was the power of the [Rule]!

To establish such a rule so effortlessly that it suppressed them, this power was even more formidable than their elders’!

Who exactly are these two constables!

Isn’t it said that great powers like to disguise themselves as street beggars? Why have they now disguised as constables?

Lu Yang and his companion leaped out the window and flew over Quyi County. At the same time, they expanded their Spiritual Sense to envelop the entire city, searching for any suspicious cultivators.

The two locked onto a figure that blended perfectly into the night, strolling leisurely on the street. Numerous cultivators passed by him without noticing his presence, showcasing the extent of his stealth technique.

"Found him!"

The figure continued to leisurely walk down the street, humming a tune, tossing the Spirit Fruit around. Even when he saw two patrolling constables nearby, he ignored them.

Just as he passed by the two constables, they both struck, pinning him down and subduing him.

"You, you can see me?!"

The figure was shocked; this was impossible!

"A Nascent Soul Stage managing to steal from someone in the Divinity Transformation Stage? You’re quite skilled," Lu Yang teased.

Meng Jingzhou managed to obtain the Spirit Fruit in the Four Treasures Secret Realm, and it certainly wasn’t due solely to luck; his abilities were among the best even at the Initial Stage of Spirit Transformation.

But this little thief being at the Nascent Soul Stage and still being able to steal from Meng Jingzhou showed that his skills were formidable.

He must be kept under strict watch.

Meng Jingzhou snatched the Spirit Fruit, frightening the thief into silence.

He was proficient in Space Technique, capable of instantaneously moving to another location, but when he secretly tried to activate the Space Technique, he found that the surrounding space was completely locked; there was no escape!

It wasn’t even an intentional space lock; they were just so powerful that they unintentionally distorted the space!

Was it just bad luck stepping out today? Where did these two deities spring from?

"Instead of learning good, you only know how to steal," Meng Jingzhou said after confirming the Spirit Fruit was real, tapping the thief on the head.

"What’s your name?"

"Fang Ning, two lords above, this is really the first time I’ve made a mistake—please spare me, I dare not do it again!" As Fang Ning spoke, he burst into tears, his face streamed with mucus and tears, touching anyone who saw him.

Lu Yang and the other remained unmoved, deceiving others was one thing, but trying to deceive them with such fake crying was another.

Besides, having cultivated thievery to such a perfected extent at the Nascent Soul Stage, it was apparent that this wasn’t his first offense.

Considering his skills, it was feared that the local constables would find it hard to catch him once Fang Ning committed a crime.

"Search him."

Meng Jingzhou grabbed Fang Ning’s wrist, and Lu Yang searched him, quickly finding a storage ring that seemed very expensive just by its style.

"Is this the storage ring you stole?"

"No, this is my storage ring."

"Then open it."

Fang Ning hesitated, and from his reaction, Lu Yang concluded that indeed, it was the stolen storage ring.

Days had passed since Fang Ning stole the ring, and due to the overly complex seal attached to the ring, he couldn’t open it; he had been studying how to unlock the ring these past few days.
