"The seal is quite intricate," Lu Yang remarked. With a slight movement, he sent out a Sword Qi and forcibly broke the seal of the storage ring.

Lu Yang took out the contents of the ring—just a few items, suggesting that its owner didn’t use this storage ring often.

Among the items in the ring, a book caught Lu Yang’s attention: ’Hundred Officials’ Descriptions?’

Although unsure of the content, its title alone seemed completely unrelated to Fang Ning.

"Where’s your identity proof?" Lu Yang now suspected that even the name Fang Ning was fake.

"I didn’t bring it," Fang Ning said earnestly.

"Where did you leave it, take us there."

"In Yunzhou," Fang Ning deliberately named a distant place.

Lu Yang was unfazed, "No problem, we can make a trip."

Fang Ning was on the verge of tears; this was the first time his schemes had backfired.

A calm voice came from the end of the street as a middle-aged man in a black robe and cap walked towards them.

"Hehe, the young disciple was merely completing my test, my friends. Could you consider letting him off this time for my sake?"

"Master!" Fang Ning called out excitedly, deeply moved—had his master been secretly watching over him as he completed the test?

After the middle-aged man appeared, the surrounding air seemed to solidify with his presence. He emitted an aura like a fierce beast of the Desolate Ancient, terrifying to the extreme, chilling to the bone.

"Encouraging theft and still talking sense? Arrest him!"

Lu Yang and his companion moved simultaneously, frightening the middle-aged man so much that he withdrew his aura and dodged quickly. Why were they fighting without a word?

Couldn’t they all, being in the Transcendance Tribulation Stage, sit down and talk?

Just as Lu Yang and his companion were about to touch the middle-aged man, he instantly reappeared ten meters away.

"Space Technique? No, just pure high-speed movement." Lu Yang raised an eyebrow, such quick movement at the Transcendance Tribulation Stage was rare—this speed might even compare with the Black Feather Venerable of the Kunpeng Clan.

The middle-aged man was secretly shocked; usually, those who engaged him for the first time mistook his speed for a Space Technique. Who exactly were these two who saw through this at a glance?

Of course, Lu Yang and his companion could see through it; the Vaste Sea Ancestor often demonstrated the embryonic form of Spatial Dao Fruit before them, they couldn’t be more familiar with what a Space Technique looked like.

"It’s rare for someone of the Human race at the Transcendance Tribulation Stage to reach such speed, are you the Thief Ancestor?"

"No, the Thief Ancestor has only one leg which is why he claims to be unrivaled in speed, and no one disputes this."

"In that case, you must be the Thief King!"

"Ah? Master, you’re the Thief King?" Fang Jin was utterly shocked; his master had never revealed his true identity.

The middle-aged man remained silent, as if a single move from the opposition could expose all his secrets.

Chapter 1228: 127: Earthquake in the Wild State Officialdom

"Those proficient in Space Technique during the Transcendence Tribulation Stage were numerous, yet those skilled in the Rapid Method at that stage were few and far between. That’s why even when the Thief King made his move, very few could guess his identity."

"Today, it seems he had met his match here."

"Although his identity had been worked out, the problem wasn’t significant, as long as he wasn’t caught."

"Lu Yang and his companion charged at the Thief King once more, the Thief King’s eyelids twitched, their speed had increased so drastically since just before!"

"Weren’t these two just at the Early Tribulation Stage? Could this really be the speed one has at the Early Tribulation Stage?"

"Or did they specialize in speed as well?"

"Since their opponent was the Thief King, Lu Yang and his companion naturally went all out."

"The rumors had it that the Thief King could appear and disappear like a ghost, even freely entering and exiting the Imperial Palace at will, showcasing his astonishing speed."

"It was just that after the Thief King infiltrated the Land Money Trade Association, there had been no word from him. He had vanished from the Mortal World, and unexpectedly appeared here."

"Can we make a deal, please? Look, this isn’t really a big issue, you could just turn a blind eye and let the two of us go."

"The Thief King was full of regret. If only he’d known these two were so hard to deal with, he would not have shown off earlier and could have just taken his apprentice away while they were still unresponsive."

"The three of them engaged in tacit combat, none of them using highly destructive moves to avoid disturbing the peace."

"At their level, they had long since reached the realm where they could unleash or contain their power at will. Even if they were to fight with full force, not a trace of power would leak out. To outsiders, their fight would appear to be nothing more than a battle between martial masters."

"Fang Jin eagerly watched the sky, where only the afterimages left by the three remained, his eyes unable to catch their actual positions."

"Which family’s martial master has such speed?"

"The pressure on the Thief King grew and grew, he couldn’t allow his lifetime of fame to be buried here."

"In a one-on-one fight, he feared no one. Not only did he have the advantage of being in the Middle Tribulation Stage, but also, within the same realm, no one could match his speed."

"But the problem was that he was facing two opponents, and they coordinated flawlessly, always blocking his escape routes and entrapping him in a dilemma."

"Lu Yang and his companion also found the Thief King to be a tough nut to crack, slippery like an eel; even when they got a touch, they couldn’t seize him."

"The best way to deal with someone renowned for their speed was to employ broad area-of-effect attacks, like Lu Yang’s Swordmanship Domain."

"But this was the county city, and using the Swordmanship Domain would likely disturb the whole city."

"If that is the case, then we must break through to a higher realm."

"Just as the Thief King was looking for an opportunity to escape, he suddenly felt the two surpass him in realm, reaching the peak of the Pass Tribulation!"

"Meng Junzi had granted Meng Jingzhou the privilege of borrowing strength from him at will."

"What is this Secret Technique?!"

"The Thief King was dumbfounded. They had leaped from the Early Tribulation Stage to the Pass Tribulation Peak in an instant—He had never heard of such a Secret Technique before, not even life-consuming techniques like the Heavenly Demon Disintegration had such an effect!"

"The biggest issue was that against two at the Pass Tribulation Peak level, he had no escape!"

"The situation for the Thief King went from bad to worse, and he was subdued by two constables."

"The net of the law is large and meshed fine, avoiding nothing; the brazen Thief King was eventually captured."

"My two Daoist friends, there really is no need for things to come to this. Look at me, a formidable figure in the Transcendence Tribulation Stage—I wouldn’t covet just a mere Spirit Fruit. It was merely a test for my apprentice, and we were planning to return the Spirit Fruit after tonight."

"The Thief King tried to appease them with a smile. These two were devilishly good; using force was out of the question, so he could only appeal to their emotions, hoping they would show mercy."

"This at best counts as a mitigating factor," Lu Yang was unmoved. They could not fail the duty signified by their uniform.

"With the capture of the Thief King and his apprentice, Lu Yang and his companion began to examine the ’Hundred Officials’ Descriptions’ they had found in the Storage Ring."

"This book is interesting; it records the dirt on officials from Wild State. Look at this page; it’s describing bad habits, over here is the bribery history, and this one details instances of malfeasance. The records are definitely thorough."

"Meng Jingzhou was amazed after reading it. The author of this book had some skill, documenting such private information, presumably obtained through special methods of eavesdropping and spying."

"Especially since the book didn’t just record what leverage existed over the officials, it sometimes even included evidence."

"Probably wants to use this book to blackmail officials."

Unfortunately, the author’s name is not written in the book.

"Confess honestly, where on earth did you steal this Storage Ring from?"

Now that his master had been apprehended, Fang Jin could only confess honestly, "It’s the Storage Ring of Zhou Hai, the County Magistrate."

Lu Yang and his companion understood at once, everything clicked into place—Zhou Hai was skilled in eavesdropping, and the matters recorded in the book must have been overheard by him using his Cultivation Technique.

...

When Man Gu woke up in the early morning, there were more than ten capture orders at the door, all requiring his signature.

Man Gu felt ashamed—he had already been working hard to maintain the public order in Quyi County, yet there were still so many lawbreakers and officials violating discipline.

"Lucky I asked two Senior Brothers to come over and help."

Man Gu saw the note left by Lu Yang and his companion, and was even more moved.

"The two Senior Brothers finished their night shift without any rest, and now they have to go and confirm with the victims."

...

"Lord Zhou, this ’Hundred Officials’ Descriptions’ is yours, right?" Two constables came to verify with the victim.

They were particular about the chain of evidence in their cases; it wasn’t enough to just have Fang Jin’s statement that the ’Hundred Officials’ Descriptions’ was stolen from Zhou Hai—they needed the victim to identify it as well.

Zhou Hai’s eyes bulged when he saw the two carrying the ’Hundred Officials’ Descriptions’; that deadly book—how could it have ended up in their hands!

Right, these two were Man Gu’s subordinates, it must be Man Gu’s doing.

So it seemed, Man Gu’s outward appearance of being careless was all an act, but in reality, he was as sharp as a tack. He’d gone out of his way to remove two Prefectorial Governors and now he had even included himself in his calculations!

What a formidable opponent.

"This isn’t something you have the right to touch, give it to me!" Zhou Hai said sternly, sure that what the two were holding was the original.

"You haven’t even said if it’s yours yet. If it is, we’ll have to make a record of it," Lu Yang said earnestly.

"I said, hand it over!" Zhou Hai shouted angrily, stepping forward, trying to snatch it away!

Numerous Demon Beast images appeared around Zhou Hai, the Demon Beasts roaring ferociously, striking straight at the soul!

The Demon Beasts were all formed from Spiritual Sense; his Cultivation Technique was fundamentally a very rare method of cultivating Spiritual Sense.

The Demon Beasts pounced towards Lu Yang and his companion, Zhou Hai sneered, how could he, of such stature, be threatened by two constables—he could already picture these two reckless constables agonizingly writhing on the ground, begging for mercy!

But when the Demon Beasts touched the two, they instantly collapsed, and Zhou Hai suffered a backlash, rolling on the ground, head banging the floor.

"Look at you, why even put yourself through this?" Meng Jingzhou sighed and squatted beside Zhou Hai, they were originally there to verify the evidence, and now look what’s happened, the victim attacking the constables, turning into a criminal suspect himself.

...

An emergency meeting of the high-level officials in Wild State.

Compared to the previous meetings, this one had fewer attendees—some due to discipline violations, others due to illegal acts, all under control.

With several high-ranking officials captured, it’s not hard to imagine the level of control extending downwards, undeniably a massive shock to the bureaucratic field of Wild State.

Since the founding of Great Xia, there had never been such a tremendous upheaval in the officialdom of Wild State.

Rumor has it that Governor Lv almost fainted from anger upon seeing the ’Hundred Officials’ Descriptions’, having to take several bottles of Heart-Protecting Pills before he could recover.

He hadn’t anticipated that not only was the internal bureaucracy in chaos, but also, the reason he had appointed Zhou Hai as County Magistrate was because his subordinates continuously praised Zhou Hai.

Unexpectedly, the reason his subordinates kept singing Zhou Hai’s praises was that they had been blackmailed!

Zhou Hai coldly swept his gaze across the assembly; whether they felt guilty or not, all of them stayed silent and bowed their heads.

"Let’s discuss, who should be appointed as the County Magistrate of Zhongshan County? I think Man Gu is fit for the task."

Chapter 1229: 1228: The Third Path of Void Refinement Stage: An Official in the Mortal World

Great Xia Palace.

"The Wild State has decided to promote Man Gu to County Magistrate exceptionally?" Emperor Xia looked at the memorial with a peculiar expression, initially thinking that when he saw the two characters for Man Gu, it was someone with the same name.

But upon closer thought, that was not right; the surname Man belonged only to the Barbarian tribe, and in the last thousand years, only Man Gu had become an official.

Upon reviewing the detailed personal profile that followed, it indeed was Man Gu, Great Xia’s Top Scorer.

The previous three County Magistrates just needed to report the list to the dynasty, and the process would be completed with the Chief of Staff’s approval. However, Man Gu did not meet the tenure or experience requirements, and the appointment of a County Magistrate required Emperor Xia’s approval.

"Is Man Gu performing that well, for the Wild State to promote him exceptionally?"

Emperor Xia read the section of the memorial that detailed Man Gu’s record, and his expression grew increasingly strange.

"In just half a month’s time, the Prefectorial Governor of Zhongshan County has been caught by Man Gu?"

"The old Prefectorial Governor was caught, the one promoted from the lower ranks was caught, and the Prefectorial Governor sent down from above for training was caught."

This was not a matter of Man Gu doing too well, it was clearly that no one besides Man Gu dared to be Prefectorial Governor.

"However, it is indeed odd; Man Gu was doing a fine job as a County Magistrate, how come there have been so many incidents lately?"

Later on, Emperor Xia saw the frequently appearing names "Constable Lu, Constable Meng" within the detailed arrest reports and was silent for a long time.

Eventually, the confusion was resolved.

Emperor Xia wrote a "granted" character at the end of the memorial.

...

"His Majesty approved it so quickly?" Governor Lv was quite surprised when he received the outcome. Usually, His Majesty wouldn’t make a decision so quickly on an exceptional promotion.

It seemed that Man Gu held significant weight in His Majesty’s heart.

The news of Man Gu’s exceptional promotion to County Magistrate quickly reached the ears of the various magistrates of Zhongshan County; they each had their sources of information, which were quicker than official announcements.

"Man Gu became County Magistrate?"

"His experience is insufficient; how did they let him become County Magistrate?"

"Upon careful consideration, it’s within reason. Man Gu is upright and fair, an enforcer of the law, the Top Scorer personally appointed by the Emperor, and also a Disciple of Dao Seeking Sect. He should have become County Magistrate long ago."

"It’s not that simple. I have some connections above, and it is said that during the previous two decisions for Prefectorial Governor, Man Gu was on the list of candidates, but several dignitaries disagreed with him becoming Prefectorial Governor."

"Later, these dignitaries were implicated by the ’Hundred Officials’ Descriptions,’ and it’s uncertain if they can even retain their positions."

"Hiss, is this also within Man Gu’s calculation? Truly terrifying."

...

"Lord Man is divinely intelligent; I have utmost admiration!" The secretary repeatedly praised, having been Man Gu’s deputy since he first became a County Magistrate and having spent the most time with him.

After such lengthy contact, he had believed that Man Gu was a simple and honest person. To be frank, such people do not go far in the officialdom, and all he could do was to help cover for Man Gu as much as possible, so as not to become too estranged from colleagues.

So, when he learned that Man Gu had arrested the former former former Prefectorial Governor, he was extremely anxious. This was a big taboo in officialdom.

Who would have thought that Man Gu’s subsequent actions would be completely unexpected, and the subsequent Prefectorial Governors were all overthrown by him. To say it was a coincidence, he utterly disbelieved it; where could be so many coincidences in an event.

This must be Man Gu’s calculations!

Presumably, the demeanor he displayed on usual days was also to confuse the opponent, to let the enemy lower their guard.

This astuteness, this patience, this calculation—it is worthy of a Disciple of Dao Seeking Sect!

He had served six County Magistrates, and whether those magistrates would be promoted or retire from their original positions, he could tell at a glance. He thought he had trained a pair of discerning eyes, but he never saw through Man Gu.

"Ah? I’ve been promoted?" Man Gu held his transfer orders in complete bewilderment, examining them from all angles to determine their authenticity.

He had come to Red Dust Mortality, not to become an official in Red Dust Mortality.

Besides, he hadn’t done anything, so how had he been promoted?

"Congratulations, congratulations." Lu Yang and another, one on each side, clapped their hands. As Junior Brother got promoted, the two Senior Brothers were genuinely happy for Man Gu from the bottom of their hearts.

"Can I, can I really be a good Prefectorial Governor?" Man Gu lacked confidence.

"I can’t even manage a small county properly. Look at how overcrowded the dungeons are recently, filled with gang crimes and brazen robbery and theft. If it weren’t for the two Senior Brothers, I wouldn’t even know how chaotic the county was."

Meng Jingzhou kindly comforted Man Gu, "You can’t say that. In my opinion, other places in Great Xia are just as chaotic; they just haven’t noticed it. The fact that these people are caught in our county proves that your governance is effective."

"What’s more," Meng continued, "there are plenty of those who Refine the Void and turn to Red Dust Mortality. Many choose to become officials, but look at how quickly any of them has been promoted compared to you."

"You’re a natural-born official," encouraged Lu Yang. "Who knows, you might even carve out a third path in the Void Refinement Stage, becoming an official in Red Dust Mortality."

"I was that remarkable." Man Gu had no doubts; his Senior Brothers wouldn’t deceive him.

Then Man Gu started to feel a headache coming on. One Quyi County could capture so many unruly Cultivators; once he got to Zhongshan County, who knew how many Cultivators he might encounter.

Especially since his cultivation level was always unstable, and the two Senior Brothers would leave after dealing with the haunting incident, Zhongshan County could be thrown into chaos with no one to stabilize the situation.

Man Gu shared his worries, and Lu Yang and Meng Jingzhou thought that indeed posed a problem. It was fine while they were still there, but what would happen once they left?

Lu Yang had a bright idea, "Why not buy some insurance? Although it won’t prevent chaos, it can reduce losses."

Meng Jingzhou approved, thinking Old Lu’s idea was good.

Seeing the two Senior Brothers both thought it was a good idea, Man Gu also felt more confident.

"By the way, Chief Secretary Liu, would you like to come to the prefectural office with me?" Man Gu asked. Over the years, Chief Secretary Liu had taught him many rules of the officialdom. Having him in the new place meant he had someone to consult with if needed.

"Me?" Chief Secretary Liu was surprised; he didn’t expect an opportunity for promotion at his age.

He bowed excitedly to Man Gu, "Thank you for your generous affection, my Lord!"

"What about the two Senior Brothers?"

Lu Yang shrugged, "We’ll go to Zhongshan County with you, of course. It won’t interfere with ghost hunting."

After all, with Man Gu being a newcomer as Prefectorial Governor, they needed to give him support in the county.

...

"You want to buy insurance for the entire Zhongshan County?" The president of the Land Money Trade Association’s Huangzhou Branch was astonished. The news of Man Gu’s assignment as the new Prefectorial Governor of Zhongshan County had already spread. Why would the new Prefectorial Governor buy insurance as soon as he assumed office?

Buying insurance for an entire county was a huge deal.

"Yes, and I want the highest level of coverage," Man Gu declared seriously, with Lu Yang and Meng Jingzhou standing behind him, all displaying a businesslike demeanor.

"This..." The president of the branch was somewhat at a loss, sensing something odd about the request, but he kept his composure, "Your prudence in willing to purchase insurance is very much welcomed by us."

"I understand, it means your branch will send someone to our Zhongshan County to assess the risks and set the cost," Man Gu said, even though he didn’t really understand; he had learned these rules from what Lu Yang told him on the road.

The president shook his head, "That’s the old policy. Since experiencing the incidents with the Sword Tower and the Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect, our commerce has suffered significant losses, so the headquarters revised the policy."

"For a large order like yours, we need to report to headquarters. They will send personnel for the risk assessment, and only after the president gives the final approval can we have a result."

Chapter 1230: 1229: Land Money Trade Association President

"It really is a major trade association, their handling of affairs is always reliable," Lu Yang believed that the association’s rules were reasonable—significant deals required such thoroughness.

It was indeed a coincidence that he had witnessed the entire course of the two accidents at the Sword Tower and the Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect, both were purely accidental, so it was normal for the association’s risk assessment to be inaccurate.

But modifying the rules was also beneficial, perhaps the people at the headquarters were more capable than those at the branch, and could make accurate judgments.

Soon, Man Gu and his two Senior Brothers left the association.

If this deal went well, perhaps Zhongshan County would purchase insurance annually—it was definitely an excellent business. With this in mind, the branch president did not dare to delay and promptly submitted the report directly to the Imperial City headquarters.

"Full insurance for Zhongshan County?"

As a disciple of the Dao Seeking Sect, the top scorer of Great Xia, and a specially promoted County Magistrate, Man Gu’s triple status melded together, granting him sway over both the official and martial paths; the Imperial City headquarters also paid great importance to this.

"We have a risk assessment here that requires your expertise, seven predecessors."

The head of risk assessment sought out seven cultivators, who were no ordinary members. They were originally the old veterans from the Jingzhou Branch and were transferred to the headquarters for their outstanding performance and faultless record in risk assessments.

Although they had initially slipped up in the risk assessment of the Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect, the subsequent investigation made it clear it was a pure accident, who could have foreseen Celestial Master Guiyuan and the Great Qian God Lord, who had disappeared for eighty thousand years, battling at the sect?

Since it was an accident, it wasn’t the fault of the seven old veterans, and their talents shouldn’t be buried for it; this decision was personally approved by the Trade Union President.

"Alright, leave this matter to us," the seven old veterans confidently announced.

After arriving at the headquarters, they also proved their skills, with each risk assessment being highly accurate.

As the seven old veterans analyzed the data for Zhongshan County en route, they hurried on their way to the county without delay.

"This time, the insurance purchaser is the newly appointed County Magistrate of Zhongshan County, Man Gu, who is also a Disciple of the Dao Seeking Sect."

"A disciple of the Dao Seeking Sect?" Initially, they had not paid much attention to Man Gu’s status, but hearing the name of the Dao Seeking Sect naturally made their teeth ache.

The leader consoled the others, "Come on, haven’t we gotten over that hang-up yet? The problem was with Lu Yang and Meng Jingzhou, not the Dao Seeking Sect; such a reaction is unnecessary."

Even so, with Lu Yang and Meng Jingzhou being the talk of the town, it was easy to associate them with the Dao Seeking Sect whenever it was mentioned.

"By the way, is there any mention of Lu Yang and Meng Jingzhou being in Zhongshan County in the materials?" one of them asked.

Their failure with the Mountain Moving and Sea Filling Sect prompted their instinctive resistance to Lu Yang and Meng Jingzhou, and now they ruled out these two individuals in any risk assessment.

"I went through it; they’re not mentioned."

"That’s good to know."

The seven old veterans continued to analyze the files, praising the maneuvers of Man Gu.

"This young man is quite astute, biding his time as County Magistrate, until half a month ago, seizing the opportunity to remove three former County Magistrates. His methods were aggressive but legitimate and legal, effectively signaling to the higher-ups in Wild State that he was determined to secure his position."

"This also shows that Man Gu is cautious by nature; he wouldn’t have waited until now to make a move otherwise."

"It’s normal for a cautious person to buy insurance."

"But isn’t it strange to purchase insurance as soon as he takes office?" someone still questioned.

The leader shook his head; with his seniority and rich experience, he had seen all sorts of people and had already discerned Man Gu’s motives.

"You haven’t considered one thing, the insurance for the entire county is indeed expensive, and making such seemingly unreasonable requests upon taking office is very likely a way to observe the reactions of the Zhongshan County officials, to see who supports him and who opposes him."

"Calling a deer a horse, indeed I overlooked this point."

They conversed back and forth, gradually outlining the truth.

Upon their arrival in Zhongshan County, they visited the Quyi County Government Office, subtly questioning the government officials.

"Man Gu has enlisted two distant relatives as constables, and these constables have also been promoted to the county level, but everything else is normal."

"Finding jobs for relatives is a common sentiment; I often have people asking me to use my connections to get them into the commerce association."

"And did you notice, these two relatives also participated in the apprehension of the three County Magistrates? Ordinary constables wouldn’t dare take on such a task; these are trusted relatives who have Man Gu’s confidence."

"Indeed."

After a round of investigation, the seven old veterans submitted their report to the headquarters’ head of risk assessment.

"Extremely low risk?"

The head of risk assessment at the headquarters thoroughly read their report, which was well-argued and cited extensively, with exhaustive data analysis, proving to be very persuasive.

Now only the president’s agreement was necessary.

"The president isn’t in?" The head of risk assessment was informed by the president’s secretary that the president was not present.

"It’s been several days since we’ve had contact," said the president’s secretary, who was used to the president’s frequent disappearances.

"So, is this risk assessment approved or not?"

Man Gu was waiting on their decision; this was a major client.

The president’s secretary responded calmly, "According to regulations, if the president cannot be contacted and there’s an urgent reason, the risk assessment can be approved directly, with all consequences the responsibility of the president."

...

In the Zhongshan County Prison.

The criminals captured by Lu Yang and his companion were originally held in the Quyi County Prison. Once Man Gu became the County Magistrate, Lu Yang and his companion believed that capturing these people was to Man Gu’s credit and insisted on keeping them by side, thus transferring them to the Zhongshan County Prison.

Moreover, the Zhongshan County Prison conditions were much better than those in Quyi County, hence ensuring the prisoners’ human rights.

Inside the prison, three county magistrates were detained together in one cell. Despite their different experiences, their fates were the same, giving them much in common to discuss.

"Master, I can’t believe you are the Thief King!" Fang Ning looked up to his master in admiration; he knew his master was mysterious and powerful, but he did not know he was that powerful.

The Thief King, that was a legendary figure in their Thief Sect, second only to the Thief Ancestor.

The Thief King sighed, "How have I taught you? Be calm, guard against arrogance and rashness. The reason I didn’t tell you about my identity was exactly because I feared this reaction."

"The Thief King is just one of my identities; I have another."

"What is it?" Fang Ning looked at his master with anticipation.

With a slight smile, the Thief King began to reveal, "The current president of the Land Money Trade Association."

Now that his good disciple knew about his Thief King identity, there was no longer any reason to hide the other.

"What?" Fang Ning was struck as if by lightning, how could his Thief King master possibly be the president of the Land Money Trade Association? These two identities were worlds apart.

Stretching leisurely, the Thief King found a comfortable position to lie down, finding the place indeed more comfortable than the Quyi County Prison.

"But master, now that you’re locked up here, what happens to the commerce association?" Fang Ning was anxious for his master, who still appeared so calm.

"The president isn’t as busy as you imagine. I often disappear on adventures, and the commerce association can still function normally without any issues."

Chapter 1231: 1230: Haunted?

"Master, since you’re so rich, why did you even join the Thief Sect?" Fang Ning didn’t understand. "Is it a hobby?"

Fang Ning had never been rich, but he’d heard that wealthy people often do things beyond ordinary folks’ comprehension because of boredom.

The Thief King sighed, "A hobby my ass, I was the Thief King first before I was reluctantly made the president of the Trade Union."

"What do you mean?" Fang Ning didn’t understand.

"You must have heard plenty about my legend, so I won’t bore you with my early life experiences."

"Mhmm." As a member of the Thief Sect, who hasn’t heard of the Thief King’s legend?

Tales like "Night Exploration of the Imperial Palace," "Stealing Heaven and Changing Day," and "Seal Mysterious Trace" were countless. But ever since the Thief King declared that he’d steal from the Land Money Trade Association headquarters, he had vanished without a trace, and no one heard of him ever again.

"Back then, I had reached the Transcendance Tribulation Stage, was full of pride and believed there was nowhere I couldn’t go, nothing I couldn’t steal."

"Especially after I smoothly snuck into the Great Xia Palace and left undetected, I didn’t take anyone seriously. I even announced my thefts beforehand, and despite their utmost vigilance, they were powerless against me, only able to watch helplessly as I stole whatever I desired."

"There’s a rule in the Thief Sect ancestors’ teachings, forbidding members from targeting the Land Money Trade Association."

"At that time, I was the number one thief in the world and didn’t take the ancestors’ teachings seriously."

"Then I snuck into the Land Money Trade Association to steal and bumped into the old man."

"Who is the old man?"

At this point, the Thief King couldn’t help but cover his face, probably the most embarrassing and unfortunate event of his life: "The Thief Ancestor."

"After he got the title of Thief Ancestor, he washed his hands of his old ways and founded the Land Money Trade Association."

"Had I known the Land Money Trade Association was his, I must have been out of my mind to steal from there!"

Fang Ning: "..."

One could imagine the master’s emotions.

Everything the master knew was the Thief Ancestor’s old tricks. Stealing under the nose of the Thief Ancestor was like showing off one’s slight skill before an expert.

No wonder the Thief Sect’s ancestors’ teachings explicitly forbade theft against the Land Money Trade Association—it was a regulation left by the Thief Ancestor.

"Seeing my potential, the Thief Ancestor let me become the president to manage the Trade Union for him."

"So that’s how it was... but wait, master, wasn’t the Thief Ancestor a figure from 60,000 years ago? How is he still alive?"

Could someone at the Transcendance Tribulation Stage live for that long?

"Of course, the Transcendance Tribulation Stage cannot live that long, but the catch is, the old man is a Semi-Immortal, even possessing the outline of the Speed Dao Fruit."

Every time the Thief King thought about the Thief Ancestor mastering the outline of the Speed Dao Fruit, he felt like banging his head against the wall.

He was skilled at speed, sure, but no matter how skilled, could he match the speed of the Dao Fruit Outline?

His rapid movements were like slow motion in front of the Thief Ancestor.

"What exactly is the Dao Fruit Outline?"

"I’ve told you a bit too much already. Your Realm is not sufficient, and knowing too much now could make you overly ambitious. You’d better master the skills I’ve taught you first."

...

"We’ve been constables for a month now; shouldn’t there be some ghosts popping up by now?" Meng Jingzhou, dutiful at his post, was patrolling Quyi County.

Although the two constables had risen in rank with Man Gu to county level, they still remembered their roots and persisted in patrolling the streets.

"Is it possible that there were originally ghosts at the cemetery but they were eaten as snacks by the female Dry Corpse?" Lu Yang speculated.

"That can’t be right, do zombies have a habit of eating little ghosts?"

"I don’t know, that’s why I’m asking you. I don’t understand zombies either."

"How about we go ask the female Dry Corpse at Corpse Chasing Sect after work?"

"Sounds good."

As they were chatting, Lu Yang suddenly gestured, and Meng Jingzhou tactfully shut up, choosing to communicate via Spiritual Sense instead.

"What’s wrong?"

Lu Yang pointed down the street ahead: "Something’s up."

The midnight streets were deserted, but not far from them, several fuzzy figures were standing in pairs or groups, conversing.

Meng Jingzhou blinked in surprise, noting the puzzled expression on those figures.

"Are they ghosts? But that can’t be right. If there were ghosts, I would feel their Yin Energy. Why can’t I sense anything?"

Meng Jingzhou was highly sensitive to Yin Energy and Yang Qi, yet he couldn’t sense any life force from these people at all.

Could it be that they are neither human nor ghost?

The two felt that the situation wasn’t ordinary, so they concealed their presence and quietly approached the figures.

"The ritual is nearly complete, make sure you don’t screw up at the critical moment."

"Don’t worry, have we ever let you down? There won’t be any issues."

"Let’s hope so."

Lu Yang and his companion listened to their conversation, puzzled by what they heard. What ritual?

"Who are you people?" Lu Yang and his companion dropped their Concealment Technique and asked the figures with a frown.

But these people acted as if they could not see Lu Yang and his companion at all, continuing to converse among themselves.

Lu Yang reached out to touch these people, but his hand passed right through, visible but intangible.

"What’s going on here? An illusion?" Meng Jingzhou furrowed his brow; in all his years of wandering, he had never encountered such a situation before.

Lu Yang shook his head: "It’s not that, don’t forget what my Cultivation Technique is. An illusion can’t fool me."

Despite his words, Lu Yang was also unsure about the situation.

It was as if these people weren’t really there.

"Immortal Fairy, what’s happening here?" Lu Yang had to admit his inexperience and sought help from Immortal Fairy Eternity.

"Space Projection?"

"It happens when a Space Crack appears and the people in that space are standing right atop it, casting part of their projection here."

"The true bodies of these people are in another space. Of course, you can’t touch them."

"Are Space Projections rare?" Lu Yang considered how many Secret Realms he had been through and how many spaces he had seen. His Green Peak Sword even came with a small space of its own, yet he had never encountered a Space Projection before.

Immortal Fairy Eternity nodded: "Quite rare. Immortal has only seen it a few times."

Shock crossed Lu Yang’s face; indeed, the occurrence was very uncommon.

"Mainly because only unstable spaces with cracks can cause a Space Projection, and spaces become unstable when the Cultivator building them lacks the necessary skill."

"Usually, Cultivators are aware of their level, whether they are good at the Space Skill or not. Those who are skilled build spaces, those who aren’t skilled, don’t."

"It is rare to see someone who is unaware of their own level and dares to haphazardly build spaces."

"The hauntings the Barbarian lad spoke of must be the Space Projections, which have been occurring for four months now."

"After four months, the master of that space is still unaware of the Space Crack; you can imagine his skill level."

The figures still caught up in the Space Projection were talking.

"Don’t worry, the official ritual will definitely have no issues. Look, we’ve conducted a trial run of the ritual every month, four times now, isn’t every time a success?"

Lu Yang: "..."

I think your trial runs weren’t very successful.

Chapter 1232: 1231 Fengdu

Lu Yang mentioned the spatial projection to Meng Jingzhou, who suddenly realized what was happening.

"Spatial projection? This is actually the first time I’m hearing about it."

"No wonder Junior Brother Man thought it was a haunting."

Spatial projections were visible but intangible, and each time one appeared, Man Gu’s cultivation level would fluctuate to a very low state, preventing him from making an accurate judgment.

Whether it was a haunting or spatial projection didn’t matter anymore; after laying in wait for so long, they had finally found the main event.

"Fairy, is there a way to enter this space?"

Lu Yang was a cultivator who knew his own limitations, aware that he was not adept at the art of space and needed to consult an expert.

"It’s quite simple, really. This space doesn’t seem very stable. You could just strike a slash at the crack with a sword, or the Meng Family Kid could throw a punch, and that would create an opening."

"Is there another method?"

A twitch ran through the corner of Lu Yang’s eye; he merely wanted to inform the space’s owner to patch up the crack so as not to frighten people with these spatial projections.

If he were to attack with a sword and Old Meng with a punch, then tell the master of the space that there was a fissure, that would be no different than provocation.

"Self-detonation would also work, and make an even larger opening."

"Any methods that don’t create openings?"

Fairy Eternity spread her hands: "Then I can only ask you to have the Vast Sea Ancestral Master pick the lock."

Lu Yang scratched his head in some difficulty. Technically, both he and Old Meng were at the Transcendance Tribulation Stage, nearly at the peak of their powers. It was somewhat embarassing that they would need to call on an Ancestor for help when facing difficulties.

After Lu Yang explained the situation, Meng Jingzhou also felt embarassed.

Just as the two were considering other approaches, they both felt a warmth in their chests. Upon checking, they saw that it was a message from Mu Baiyi, sent through the Jiuyou Order.

"This year, after years of effort, Fengdu is finally complete, towering and magnificent, interfacing with the heavens and the earth, sharing existence with the nether. This event is a once-in-a-millennium occasion, stirring attention from all corners. We hereby set the celebration for the completion of Fengdu on July 15, and on behalf of the entire Jiuyou Sect, we sincerely invite the Young Sect Master to grace the event."

"Entrance location: Great Xia Desolate State Zhongshan County’s ’Come Again BBQ Shop.’"

The two silently put away the Jiuyou Order.

It seemed they now knew who the owner of the space was, so there was no need to trouble the Vast Sea Ancestral Master for help.

"If I remember correctly, has the Jiuyou Sect always dreamed of establishing Fengdu?" Lu Yang had heard from Li Haoran about the significance of Fengdu to the Jiuyou Sect, but constructing Fengdu was too costly, and Qin Haoran had always disagreed with the initiative during his tenure.

Now that the barbecue shop and solar energy business continued to grow stronger, they finally had the funds to construct Fengdu.

Thinking about it, Lu Yang could be considered one of the founders of Fengdu.

"July 15? So that’s in seven days?"

Now that they knew the reason behind the hauntings, there was no need for them to patrol every night.

After informing Man Gu about Fengdu, Man Gu was quite surprised; he had said there was something sinister about the haunting incidents, and it turned out to be this.

"Will the two Senior Brothers be going?"

Lu Yang smiled, "This is a major event happening in our jurisdiction; naturally, we should take a look."

Seven days later, Lu Yang and Meng Jingzhou changed their appearances to that of the Young Sect Master Lu and the Dragon Saint and arrived at the Zhongshan County branch of the ’Come Again BBQ Shop.’

Mu Baiyi stood at the entrance of the barbecue shop, eagerly anticipating, and finally his wait was over as the two men arrived.

"Young Master Lu, Dragon Saint, it’s been a while since we’ve seen each other."

To Mu Baiyi, these two were no less than lifesavers. The barbecue shop not only helped him pay off his loans but also supported the establishment of Fengdu.

Alongside Mu Baiyi, the higher-ups of the Jiuyou Sect were there to greet the two men, showing their respect and recognition for the Heavenly Court Sect.

"I have long heard of the great names of Young Sect Master Lu and Dragon Saint," said the Grand Elder of the Yaoyang Sect, Liu Zhen, upon their first meeting. "This is my first time meeting you."

In the Extreme Northern Lands, Lu Yang and his companion had previously met Liu Zhen with their true identities, but they had not yet met him while assuming their roles within the Heavenly Court Sect.

Mu Baiyi hesitantly asked, "May I know where your Protectors are? Could they make an appearance? Ah, of course, if it’s not convenient, then never mind."

In his memory, every time he met Young Sect Master Lu and the Dragon Saint, there were always Protectors by their side. Why were they absent today? Was it because they were very confident in their surroundings at the Jiuyou Sect?

Lu Yang and his companion simply smiled, and a terrifying aura enveloped the entire barbecue restaurant.

"Does Sect Leader Mu think we still need Protectors now?"

Mu Baiyi, Chi Sect Leader, and Liu Zhen suddenly felt as though they were suffocating, staring at Lu Yang and his companion in disbelief.

They were all at the Transcendance Tribulation Stage—to be able to exert such oppressive force that made them feel suffocated was impressive.

"Pass Tribulation Peak?!"

Upon first meeting, they had discovered they could not see through the Cultivation Levels of Young Sect Master Lu and the Dragon Saint, but they hadn’t thought much of it, assuming it was due to magic artifacts that hid their true strength.

They hadn’t considered the possibility that it might be at the Transcendance Tribulation Stage. After all, considering their youth, even prodigies like Lu Yang and Meng Jingzhou would be at the Early Tribulation Stage at most.

Had these two actually ascended to the Pass Tribulation Peak without any signs?

Was this the legacy of the Heavenly Court Sect?

No wonder Protectors were unnecessary; with such a Cultivation Level, they could roam the world with impunity.

People often debated who was stronger between Lu Yang and the Young Sect Master Lu. As the years passed, with the Young Sect Master Lu seldom active and Lu Yang creating various legends in the Cultivation World, people gradually leaned toward the view that Lu Yang was stronger than the Young Sect Master Lu.

Even people like Mu Baiyi thought so.

Today, the truth was revealed: Young Sect Master Lu’s infrequent activity was due to his intense Cultivation in seclusion.

While everyone thought Lu Yang was the pinnacle among Geniuses, it turned out Young Sect Master Lu’s talents far surpassed Lu Yang’s!

Liu Zhen felt that he had lived his years in vain.

"Please come inside," they said as the two retracted their aura, and Mu Baiyi quickly invited them into the Fengdu Space, his attitude even more enthusiastic than before.

In the courtyard of the barbecue restaurant stood a swirling Spatial Gate.

As they walked through the Spatial Gate, the scene changed from light to dark; the Fengdu Space was dimly lit, casting an oppressive atmosphere.

The Fengdu Space was vast, with various buildings forming a city greater than Zhongshan County, its scale grand enough to rival that of the Imperial City.

No wonder Junior Brother Li said that building Fengdu was expensive; just from these buildings, it was clear this was a major project.

Moreover, the architectural style didn’t resemble that of Great Xia at all, giving off an ancient and old-fashioned aura.

Ghost fires were the only source of light in Fengdu Space. Two green flames drifted beside them, casting their faces in a greenish hue, perfectly matching the style of Fengdu.

Chi Sect Leader and Liu Zhen were also experiencing the splendor of Fengdu for the first time, overwhelmed by the grandeur of the Jiuyou Sect’s undertakings.

No wonder Mu Baiyi was always so particular about money; it turned out all the funds had been funneled into this place.

"This architectural style is quite interesting, who designed it? Sect Leader Mu, was it you?" Lu Yang asked with a smile.

Mu Baiyi shook his head. "These were based on the sketches left by the former sect leader, Qin Haoran."

"Sketches?"

"His sketches depicted Fengdu, featuring a very unique architectural style. We’ve analyzed his designs, and they do not match the architectural styles of Ancient Times, nor those of Great Qian, Great Yu, or Great Xia."

"It is said that Qin Haoran was a man of Reincarnation. We believe the Fengdu he drew wasn’t the product of imagination, but rather that his memories of the Reincarnation Land had not completely faded, and he still remembered what it looked like."

"It’s this space that I constructed myself." Mu Baiyi said proudly.

In order to build the Fengdu Space, he had specifically learned the Space Skill; he had no experience prior to this, but now it seemed he had a natural talent for spatial matters, learning it with ease.

Chapter 1233: 1232: Ghost Food Source

"Is it different from the architectural style of Ancient Times?" Lu Yang asked with a laugh, seeming to have heard something very interesting.

"Could it be... it isn’t?" Mu Baiyi’s confidence wavered at the sound of Lu Yang’s laughter, considering that he was a true genius from the Ancient Times and the most authoritative on ancient architectural styles.

"Let’s walk and talk," Lu Yang said with a smile, signaling Mu Baiyi to lead the way.

"This way."

Mu Baiyi gestured invitingly, leading everyone to a riverside.

The fog thickened in the river, and a wooden boat anchored on the bank awaited them. Vice Sect Hierarch Shi Huagu untied the ropes, ready to ferry the group across.

"This place is called Wangchuan River, built according to the manuscripts left by Qin Haoran. Wangchuan River surrounds Fengdu, and to enter Fengdu, one must be transported by the Ferryman to cross the river."

"This river can detect dangerous items among the possessions of its passengers. Of course, you all need not be checked," Mu Baiyi added quickly at the end, so as not to offend Lu Yang and the others.

Ghostly green large lanterns floated in the middle of the river, the same color as the ghostly flames lighting their path above.

"It does have its charms," Meng Jingzhou remarked offhand as if he was very knowledgeable about these things.

"Young Sect Master Lu, where did Fengdu’s architectural style originate from?"

Instead of answering directly, Lu Yang posed a different question, "Sect Leader Mu, when do you believe the Ancient Times ended?"

"Clearly, with the Ancient Immortals turning stars into continents. After that, the Ancient Times ended, and the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord and others built the Great Qian Dynasty on the ruins."

Lu Yang shook his head. "That’s wrong. The time between turning stars into continents and the establishment of the Great Qian Dynasty spanned a hundred thousand years. During these hundred thousand years, a massive entity emerged, known as the Fire Dynasty."

"What?!"

Both Mu Baiyi and Chi Sect Leader Liu Zhen were shocked, and Shi Huagu, rowing the boat, nearly lost his oar and fell into the river upon hearing this secret history.

A massive dynasty spanning a hundred thousand years was unheard of by any of them.

"Do you remember me saying that back in the Ancient Times, the Heavenly Court and the Underworld stood together? One governed the living, and the other the Reincarnation."

"The Underworld that governed life and death was established during the era of the Fire Dynasty."

"The Fengdu that your sect built, indeed, has some resemblance to the locales of the Underworld."

Yingtian Immortal, Time Immortal, Immortal Jiuchong, Qinghe, and Ao Ling had lived through the Fire Dynasty’s era. Lu Yang had a general idea of what the Underworld looked like from their descriptions, which closely resembled Fengdu before him.

It was uncertain whether Qin Haoran had recalled memories from his first life by chance or had fully awakened them when he drafted the blueprint of Fengdu.

Lu Yang leaned toward the former; after all, if Qin Haoran had fully regained his memories and still failed to Pass Tribulation, that would be quite preposterous.

"So that’s how it is!" Mu Baiyi was convinced that inviting the Heavenly Court Sect was the right decision, as only someone from there would know such ancient secrets.

Everyone disembarked, and the magnificent and imposing ancient city of Fengdu revealed itself, solemn and majestic, exuding an indescribable sense of antiquity.

This ancient feeling manifested not only in the architectural style but also in the construction materials.

Lu Yang touched the city wall and asked, "These bricks aren’t newly made, are they?"

"Young Sect Master Lu has a keen eye; we happened upon the ancient capital city of the Great Qian Dynasty during our tomb exploration and used its bricks to construct Fengdu."

"All these bricks are?"

"All of them."

Lu Yang fell silent for a moment; Fengdu’s scale was comparable to the Great Xia Imperial City. If the bricks used for Fengdu all originated from an ancient capital city, it meant that this ancient city was as large as the Great Xia Imperial City.

Which ancient capital city of Great Qian could be this vast? It seems you might have stumbled upon Great Qian’s Imperial City itself.

The city gates, as heavy as mountains, creaked open to reveal a dense multitude of ghosts within. Their forms varied widely—some as tall as two-story buildings, others merely the size of a palm, with their numbers reaching into the tens of thousands.

During the grand opening festival of Fengdu City, the streets were adorned with lanterns and banners, and large red paper was pasted on every ghost—although Meng Jingzhou and Lu Yang knew that Sect Leader Mu’s intentions were good, the scene combined with Fengdu’s dim environment and the green ghostly fires, looked incredibly eerie, no matter how one looked at it.

"Starving Ghost, Five Ghosts, Beautiful Ghost, Ears and Eyes Ghost..."

Lu Yang considered himself knowledgeable about various specters, yet there were many whose names he could not recall, and whose origins he did not recognize.

Sect Leader Mu introduced with pride, "The ghosts of Great Xia are almost all gathered here."

Originally, the Jiuyou Sect did not have this many ghosts, but with the proliferation of barbecue restaurants, employees would seek out ghosts in their spare time. If they were successful in recruiting them to the Jiuyou Sect, they would receive a considerable reward depending on the ghost’s cultivation level.

This would have been entirely unfeasible for the previously impoverished Jiuyou Sect.

"Based on the needs of the ghosts, we have also established academies, taverns, teahouses, and other places, enough for them to live here indefinitely."

Lu Yang had an epiphany, "So you’re saying that Fengdu City was specifically built for the ghosts to live in?"

No wonder the Jiuyou Sect was so intent on establishing Fengdu City; it wasn’t just a matter of faith, but also this.

"What do these ghosts eat, then?"

As they say, without Yin there is no growth; without Yang, there is no life. Although ghosts were beings of extreme Yin, they still needed a significant amount of Yang Qi to maintain basic activities.

This is why ghosts would siphon off the living’s Yang Qi.

With so many ghosts living in Fengdu, the Jiuyou Sect surely couldn’t be capturing people regularly to feed them?

"That’s where we have to thank Chi Sect Leader."

Chi Sect Leader smiled slightly, raising his hand modestly, "I’ve only provided a bit of help. The main solution was your own idea."

"Please take a look at this," said Sect Leader Mu, pulling out a lamp from behind.

"A solar-powered lamp?" Meng Jingzhou was puzzled, weren’t solar-powered lamps supposed to restrain ghosts?

"This one is different from the ordinary solar-powered lamps."

Sect Leader Mu pressed the switch. The solar-powered lamp lit up faintly, not as bright as the ghostly fires above everyone’s heads.

"Ghosts feed on Yang Qi, and if we talk about the largest source of Yang Qi, it has to be the Sun."

"However, the Sun is too strong. Only the King of Ghosts can operate under the Sun. Ordinary ghosts would perish at its sight and cannot absorb the Sun’s energy."

"Therefore, the solar-powered lamp can perfectly absorb the Yang Qi of the Sun. I asked Chi Sect Leader to research a less bright solar-powered lamp. With lower brightness, the released solar energy would be less, and the ghosts could then assimilate it."

"I just wanted to try it out; I didn’t expect it to actually work," laughed Sect Leader Mu, having been unsure initially.

Having resolved the ghosts’ food source issue, the spirits of Great Xia would naturally flock to join the Jiuyou Sect.

The Yaoyang Sect was very willing to cooperate with the Jiuyou Sect, even offering to provide solar-powered lamps for free. Their creed was the promotion of the great Sun, and now even the ghosts had to rely on the Sun, which was sufficient to demonstrate the Sun’s greatness.

"Does this mean that these ghosts will all live in Fengdu from now on?" Lu Yang asked, sensing an unexpected boon.

Ghosts were universally acknowledged as difficult to manage by the dynasty, and there was endless debate over whether they even had rights, with no consensus in sight.

If all the ghosts resided in Fengdu, it would be ideal.

"Not all will live in Fengdu. We still need ghosts to run the barbecue joints and to work with the Yaoyang Sect on researching new products."

"Can’t you use living people?" asked Meng Jingzhou.

Sect Leader Mu waved his hand, "Using people would necessitate paying them a salary, which is too expensive. Using ghosts only requires providing them with Yang Qi."

Chapter 1234: 1233: The Strange Sapling

Hearing this, Lu Yang secretly transmitted a message to Meng Jingzhou, "That said, Old Meng, if you shed some blood, could you also attract a large group of ghosts to work for you?"

Moreover, the ghosts that would be attracted are King of Ghosts, the lower-level ones wouldn’t even have the life to drink Old Meng’s blood.

Meng Jingzhou rolled his eyes, "If only the King of Ghosts could outcompete the Vast Sea Ancestral Master."

Meng Jingzhou felt that the happiest person about his ascension to the Transcendence Tribulation Stage was his own ancestor, the second happiest was undoubtedly the Vast Sea Ancestral Master.

Mu Baiyi led everyone into the core area of Fengdu City, and Lu Yang noticed that Fengdu City had not only ghosts but also many people who seemed to be followers of the Jiuyou Sect.

That made sense, establishing Fengdu was a major event, so naturally, the Fengdu Festival was an occasion where all Jiuyou Sect members would gather.

Lu Yang guessed correctly; today was a big day for the Jiuyou Sect. All the barbecue shops had been closed for days, all rushing here in advance, with Lu Yang and his group being the last batch to enter Fengdu.

"Are these bricks also moved from the Great Qian Ancient City?" Meng Jingzhou stomped his feet a couple of times, feeling that these bricks and the city walls weren’t from the same era.

"No, only the city walls of the Great Qian Ancient City are usable. The quality of the stone bricks isn’t good enough to be used."

"Then where did these floor tiles come from?"

"I asked the people from the Heavenly Strategy Sect to calculate where there could be a large number of very old bricks. They came up with a general location, and after we dug around, we discovered an ancient capital city from the Great Yu Dynasty and moved all the bricks here."

Meng Jingzhou: "..."

Why does it sound like your Fengdu is put together with bits and pieces from everywhere?

"Speaking of which, your sect believes in reincarnation. In ancient times, the Underworld was indeed the land of reincarnation. Does Fengdu have the function of reincarnation as well?" Lu Yang asked.

Chi Sect Leader and Liu Zhen perked up their ears, they too were very interested in the reincarnation preached by the Jiuyou Sect.

They had also heard from the Jiuyou Sect that the previous sect leader Qin Haoran was a person of reincarnation. At first, they didn’t really believe it, but just now when Young Sect Master Lu mentioned that the manuscript left by Qin Haoran depicted the exact image of the Underworld from ancient years, this matter became very intriguing.

The affairs of the Fire Dynasty should be known to no one, how did Qin Haoran know about it?

Mu Baiyi shook his head gently, "Young Sect Master Lu and Dragon Saint have overestimated us. Currently, Fengdu City is only being used as a residence for ghosts. Although we have built Fengdu strictly following the manuscript left by the former Sect Leader, establishing the Three-Life Stone, Yellow Springs Road, and other places, they are merely replicas without the function of reincarnation."

"However, we believe that as long as our faith is strong enough, one day Fengdu will become the land of reincarnation!"

Lu Yang actually wanted to say don’t get your hopes up too high. Even the Underworld of the Fire Dynasty didn’t have the function of reincarnation, and in ten thousand years, only Li Hao became a person of reincarnation.

Thinking about it, he decided not to voice this thought; it’s not easy for them to build a Fengdu after all, so better not to discourage them.

Lu Yang thought the number of ghosts upon first entering the city was already plenty, but it wasn’t until they arrived at the heart of Fengdu City that they really understood what a mountain and sea of ghosts meant.

They even saw the true forms of the few figures from the space crack a few days ago; they were all ghosts.

So, it couldn’t be said that Man Gu made a wrong judgment; there really were ghost disturbances in Quyi County.

"Here is the sacrificial altar."

Many people and ghosts surrounded the altar, and upon seeing Mu Baiyi and the others coming over, they consciously made way.

The altar was circular and had four levels, corresponding to heaven, earth, humans, and ghosts. It was pure white, the only white in Fengdu, and very eye-catching.

"Please take your seats."

Lu Yang, Meng Jingzhou, Chi Sect Leader, and Liu Zhen, being at the Transcendence Tribulation Stage, sat in the front row, with the high-level members of the Jiuyou Sect beginning from the second row.

"Sect Leader Mu, why is there a tree in the middle of the altar?" Lu Yang asked, puzzled.

In the middle of the altar was a circular hollow, growing a sapling over two people tall, with branches holding something unknown, tightly wrapped by leaves.

Lu Yang had seen many spirit trees in the Medicine Garden, but none that matched this sapling.

"Old Meng, do you recognize this tree?"

Meng Jingzhou was also wondering what the Jiuyou Sect was up to, "I don’t recognize it."

"Fairy, do you recognize it?"

Fairy Eternity frowned slightly, "Strange, Immortal has not seen such a thing before."

Mu Baiyi explained, "Actually, we don’t know what it is either."

"It didn’t exist when the altar was first established. Suddenly one day, a subordinate came to report that the altar had been shattered by a sapling."

"At that time, I was very surprised. The altar was made of flawless jade, which is extremely hard. We had a tough time just processing the jade stones. How could a sapling shatter it?"

"When I went to see for myself, it was indeed the case. The altar was cracked into pieces, and a never-before-seen sapling was growing."

"I asked around, but no one knew the origin of the sapling."

"Considering the sapling was quite peculiar, I didn’t dare to pull it out. Instead, I had to find another place to build the altar. But then the sapling moved locations and shattered the new altar again."

"By the third time I was rebuilding the altar, I watched the sapling day and night. I noticed that the sapling would gradually become illusory, and then a phantom of it would appear inside the altar nearby."

"When the sapling on my side completely vanished, a real sapling would appear inside the altar and then explode, shattering the altar."

"Having no alternative, I had to order a hole to be dug in the center of the altar to allow the sapling to grow. Now, it’s grown this tall."

After listening, Lu Yang was still thoroughly confused. What in the world was this sapling?

"Are those hanging from the branches fruits?"

Mu Baiyi still shook his head, "I don’t know."

The appearance of the sapling didn’t interfere with the completion of the Fengdu Festival celebration. The believers simply took the sapling planted here as having some kind of symbolism.

As the ceremony’s host, Mu Baiyi put on a black ceremonial robe and ascended the altar.

Seeing Mu Baiyi take the stage, the members of the Jiuyou Sect all fell silent simultaneously, looking up at Mu Baiyi with devout and clear eyes.

Mu Baiyi’s expression solemn, he took out the willow branch that was inserted in the jade vase and placed it in the four cardinal directions - east, west, south, and north.

The willow branch symbolized rebirth, and the four directions represented the world. The essence of reincarnation is rebirth, and the act symbolized Fengdu as the reincarnation land for the entire world, the starting point of new life.

Then, with one hand holding the ritual sword and the other the jade ring, he struck them together, producing a clear sound, all the while reciting ancient blessing words in the Ancient Language.

Lu Yang, even when translating it into modern language, still couldn’t understand what Sect Leader Mu was saying.

As Sect Leader Mu reached the midpoint of his recitation, those below started to chant along, accompanied by ancient ceremonial music.

"Lucky day, propitious hour, joyfully celebrate Jiuyou..."

The entire Fengdu City was enveloped in a solemn and majestic atmosphere, as if in resonance with heaven and earth, the space itself trembling.

Lu Yang was gradually moved by this atmosphere, and settled down to experience the process.

At the top of the sapling, the leaves unfurled and revealed a glimpse of the true nature of what was enveloped inside.

It was a fruit so illusory that it was nearly transparent, fantastic and bizarre, as if it should not exist in this world.

Witnessing this scene, Lu Yang’s pupils contracted sharply. The altar was in the center of Fengdu City, the focal point of all the believers’ attachments; in other words, it was where the Power of Faith was most abundant. The fact that the sapling only grew on the altar implied that its existence was inextricably linked to the Power of Faith!

Now, as the festival officially began, the fruit on the sapling grew at a speed visible to the naked eye.

Could it be that this thing is...

Just as Lu Yang reached this point, an untimely laughter echoed overhead, interrupting the festival proceedings, and with the laughter, the ghostly flames in the sky above Fengdu City one-by-one extinguished.

"Jiuyou Sect, you do have some skill. You’ve actually managed to create the outline of the Reincarnation Dao Fruit!"

Chapter 1235: 1234: Robbing in front of our Heavenly Court Sect?

"Indeed, it’s the Reincarnation Dao Fruit Prototype!" The sudden figure confirmed Lu Yang’s guess.

The situation of the Jiuyou Sect was somewhat similar to that of the Eternity Sect; both used the Power of Faith to condense a Dao Fruit Prototype.

The difference was that the Eternity Sect worshipped an "Immortal Eternity" that didn’t exist, so they not only used the Power of Faith to condense the Initial Form of Immortal Dao Fruit but also created a Fourth Housekeeper.

Whereas the Jiuyou Sect simply believed in reincarnation, so they only condensed the Reincarnation Dao Fruit Prototype.

"Who are you, daring to intrude upon our sect!" Mu Baiyi roared with rage. The Fengdu Festival was held in the highest regard by the Jiuyou Sect, seen as the pinnacle of the Power of Faith with the emergence of the Reincarnation Dao Fruit Prototype as the best example.

The Jiuyou Sect wouldn’t allow anyone to disturb this celebration.

That figure took out a blood-stained long stick, pointing it at Mu Baiyi, the stick’s body pitch black, dyed by the blood of slain enemies, turning from red to black over the years.

"I am Si Heng God Monarch, here under the orders of the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord to pluck the Reincarnation Dao Fruit Prototype. If you know what’s good for you, retreat immediately. If not, you can’t blame me for being unfeeling!"

"Si Heng God Monarch?" Lu Yang narrowed his eyes. According to confessions from prisoners of the Great Qian Dynasty, Si Heng God Monarch was a capable lieutenant of the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord, in possession of a Yin Yang Dao Fruit Prototype, not condensed from the Power of Faith but comprehended through his own enlightenment.

No wonder his timing was impeccable, showing up just as the Reincarnation Dao Fruit Prototype appeared. The Great Qian Dynasty was best at using the Power of Faith to condense a Dao Fruit Prototype.

Si Heng God Monarch had likely been waiting for the Reincarnation Dao Fruit Prototype in the Fengdu Space.

The appearance of Si Heng God Monarch shocked all Jiuyou Sect believers, but they were not frightened, rather enraged, throwing things like teacups, plates, and solar lamps at the airborne Si Heng God Monarch.

With a cold snort, Si Heng God Monarch opened a barrier that prevented these objects from hitting him.

"Fools, it seems the world has forgotten my legend after I haven’t acted for three hundred thousand years!"

"Then try me!" Standing his ground in Fengdu, Mu Baiyi showed no signs of fear.

Moreover, since he had already stated that the fruit on this sapling was the Reincarnation Dao Fruit Prototype, he couldn’t afford to back down.

Si Heng God Monarch swung his bloody long stick at Mu Baiyi.

"Abyss Hell’s Big Grinding Plate!"

The Big Grinding Plate appeared, laying before Mu Baiyi.

"You think something like this could stop me!"

A boom resounded—before the Big Grinding Plate could show its mystery, it was smashed to pieces by Si Heng God Monarch.

However, behind the Big Grinding Plate was not Mu Baiyi, but six pitch-black coffins.

"Rise!"

Mu Baiyi shouted, and the lids of the coffins flew open as six corpses stiffly bounced out of them.

"These six are what Junior Brother Li spoke of as the Jiuyou Sect’s strongest foundation, right?" Lu Yang asked Meng Jingzhou through a psychic message.

"Pretty much."

Li Haoran and Lu Yang had spoken of the Jiuyou Sect’s strongest foundation, which was the bodies of six Jiuyou Sect Sect Masters. These Sect Masters had all reached the Transcendance Tribulation Stage in life, each representing a glorious period in the history of the Jiuyou Sect.

In times of life and death, the Sect Masters of the Jiuyou Sect would control these corpses for battle.

Si Heng God Monarch’s view was blocked by the Big Grinding Plate and did not see where the six coffins came from. Lu Yang, as an observer, saw clearly; the six coffins were buried near the altar, occupying the six most favorable directions in terms of Feng Shui.

Mu Baiyi’s fearlessness toward Si Heng God Monarch didn’t mean he was unaware of his opponent’s strength; alone at the Early Tribulation Stage, he certainly wasn’t a match, so it was better to use the Jiuyou Sect’s strongest trump card from the start.

The Jiuyou Sect hadn’t developed Reincarnation Techniques, but they had done much research on Corpse-Driving Spells, enabling these bodies to temporarily maintain a battle consciousness and retain eighty percent of their capabilities from life.

"Great Heaven-tearing Hand!"

"Ruyi Seal!"

"Mad Demon Fist Technique!"

"All poisons are boundless!"

"Netherworld Heaven!"

"Wangchuan Bu Gui!"

The huge hand that tore through the heavens, the Ruyi Seal that suppressed everything with a thought, the Mad Demon Fist Technique that one would not survive without going mad, the poison fog that could kill a swath of creatures with just a trace...

Six cultivators at the Transcendence Tribulation Stage struck simultaneously. In the underground world at that time, they were all figures of notorious reputation. They all attacked Si Heng God Monarch together, each executing their own best techniques. Even those at the peak of Pass Tribulation would fall dead on the spot!

"You mere Transcendence Tribulation Stage dare to attack me. Mortals, today you shall learn what the Dao Fruit Prototype is."

Si Heng God Monarch’s expression was icy as he activated the Yin Yang Dao Fruit Prototype, causing six areas of space to distort, and six figures appeared.

These six figures were identical to the six successive sect leaders of the Jiuyou Sect.

"Go."

Si Heng God Monarch commanded, and the six figures he conjured were not exactly the same as the actual people; they were the exact opposites. Whatever techniques the opposing six Transcendence Tribulation Stage cultivators knew, these six figures would execute the exact opposite, thus achieving a stalemate.

Lu Yang discerned the principle behind it: "The Immortal Jiuchong’s Yin-Yang Dao Fruit can define yin and yang, thereby resulting in the neutralization of yin and yang. The Yin Yang Dao Fruit Prototype cannot take this step but can only create puppets. It allows the puppets to possess the completely opposite techniques of the enemy, indirectly achieving the effect of yin and yang neutralization."

"Is this the power of a Dao Fruit Prototype?" Mu Baiyi could not believe the scene before him. Had their Jiuyou Sect’s ace, cultivated over tens of thousands of years, been neutralized by the opponent in one move?

"Mortals, I have told you that you will pay the price for your foolishness."

Chi Sect Leader and Liu Zhen, who had intended to help, showed signs of fear. With such magical and crafty methods, even if they helped, it wouldn’t change the situation.

Si Heng God Monarch descended, his staff sweeping away Mu Baiyi, who was still preparing to strike, aiming to uproot the Reincarnation Dao Fruit Prototype along with its seedling.

Suddenly, Si Heng God Monarch’s hairs stood on end, foreseeing a lethal danger, and he hastily retreated backward.

A streak of sword light grazed the edge of his body, which, a moment slower, would have split him in two.

The sword light vanished, leaving the altar split into two uneven halves.

Si Heng God Monarch was both shocked and enraged. Was this Sword Qi from a Sword Cultivator?

Weren’t Sword Cultivators always known for their upright and grandiose style? Since when did they resort to sneak attacks?!

"Tsk, you really aren’t easy to kill." Lu Yang, holding the Green Peak Sword, moved forward in a relaxed manner, finding that Si Heng God Monarch truly was a cultivator who had fought his way out, with a strong sense of impending crisis.

"Taking things right in front of us from the Heavenly Court Sect, isn’t that a bit improper?"

Mu Baiyi, helped away by others, was thrilled to see Lu Yang take action.

"Young Sect Master Lu!"

"People from the Heavenly Court Sect?" Si Heng God Monarch sneered coldly; he had seen Lu Yang and Meng Jingzhou when he was lurking in the Fengdu Space. "The Emperor had already told me that the Heavenly Court Sect didn’t even exist during Ancient Times!"

"What could your Emperor possibly know? He doesn’t even know about the Fire Dynasty."

Meng Jingzhou exerted force in his legs and shot toward Si Heng God Monarch like a cannonball. Si Heng God Monarch blocked with his staff, and his palms split open instantly!

Realizing he couldn’t take the punch head-on, he swung his staff in a full circle, guiding Meng Jingzhou to the other side with the momentum.

Meng Jingzhou was flung away by Si Heng God Monarch and landed next to Lu Yang.

"Old Lu, why waste words with him, let’s capture him first!"

"What’s your hurry? I was just about to draw some information when you interrupted."

"Why bother with him? He doesn’t even know about the Fire Dynasty, a complete illiterate. What could he possibly know?"

"Are you two done yet?" Si Heng God Monarch roared in anger, hating most when others called him illiterate.

Chapter 1236: 1235 Exposed Identity

Si Heng God Monarch admitted that when he was young, he had a fondness for battle and read too little, his knowledge of history was lacking, and he often mistook unofficial histories for the official ones.

His colleagues would nickname him the Illiterate God Monarch behind his back.

But what of it? The Emperor praised him for having "a mind free of distractions and a resolve to move forward." Would anyone else receive such praise from the Emperor? Anyone calling him illiterate was just jealous of him!

"You can ask anything, I know it all!" Si Heng God Monarch was boiling with rage, his hair standing on end.

"What’s the Fire Dynasty?"

"I don’t know."

"What effects does the Reincarnation Dao Fruit Prototype have?"

"I don’t know."

"What does the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord want with the Reincarnation Dao Fruit Prototype?"

"I don’t know." Si Heng God Monarch became increasingly uneasy, realizing he couldn’t answer a single question.

"Where is the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord?"

"I know that... No, you’re trying to trick me!"

Lu Yang and his companion were quite disappointed. How had he caught on so quickly?

The appearance of Lu Yang and his companion greatly boosted everyone’s confidence. The combatants on both sides were legendary figures, and it was said that the older they were, the stronger. With more people on Young Sect Master Lu’s side, naturally, they had the advantage.

Mu Baiyi, on the other hand, was worried. He had long heard of the unbelievable divine abilities of the Dao Fruit Outline, and that a Semi-Immortal could completely overwhelm those at the Transcendance Tribulation Stage. Seeing it for himself today confirmed it to be true. Although Lu Yang and his companion were young and talented, after all, they were only at the Transcendance Tribulation Stage, so it was uncertain whether they could defeat Si Heng God Monarch.

He hoped they had a trump card capable of overcoming Si Heng God Monarch.

"Everyone, fall back!"

Mu Baiyi shouted loudly, maintaining order and instructing the followers to retreat. In battles at the Transcendance Tribulation Stage, the destruction could shatter heavens and earth, so staying meant certain death.

"You’re seeking death!" Si Heng God Monarch was completely enraged. Whether these two were from the Heavenly Court Sect or not, without a Protector, they were no match for him!

"Weak Water Stick Skill!"

Si Heng God Monarch wielded his long stick, and a mist gradually started to form around him.

"I really want to see what use this soft and weak stick skill could possibly have!" Meng Jingzhou charged in fearlessly. A Body Cultivator at the peak of Pass Tribulation could sweep through the current age; no Cultivator would dare to engage him in close combat.

The moment Meng Jingzhou’s fists touched the stick, he understood Si Heng God Monarch’s intention. This stick skill was meant to mimic "water," using softness to overcome hardness, able to dissipate an opponent’s force.

But could you really dissipate it?

Meng Jingzhou’s aura shifted, as if he were an emperor above all, eyeing nothing but victory.

I Am the Only One Fist!

Si Heng God Monarch instantly felt the increased force, even with his stick skill diverting it, his arms still tingled numbly.

What kind of monster is this!

"Ha ha, you dare call yourself a God Monarch with such skills?"

"Old Lu, see what I told you? There’s no need to waste words on this type. A good beating will make him spill it all."

"The prisoners we have locked up are stronger than you."

The angrier Si Heng God Monarch got, the more he resembled a lion in a frenzy. Taking advantage of Si Heng’s entire attention focused on Old Meng, Lu Yang pointed his Green Peak Sword at Si Heng God Monarch from afar. Si Heng’s body uncontrollably collapsed to the ground, kneeling with perfect form.

Meng Jingzhou had been waiting for this chance. His fists exploded with force, each punch capable of shattering stars, all greeting Si Heng God Monarch’s face, bashing it beyond recognition.

"This is not only illiteracy, but your combat skills also fall short."

"Fluent in speech, I hope you’re just as tough when you’re fighting your own shadow!"

Si Heng God Monarch stepped back continuously while keeping Meng Jingzhou locked in his sights, urging the Yin Yang Dao Fruit Prototype. During the confrontation, he noticed that the opponent’s Yang Qi was exceedingly vigorous to an outrageous extent, even surpassing that of a Pure Yang Immortal Body.
