"Come on, I want to see what kind of shadow you can produce!" Meng Jingzhou was full of confidence in himself.

Space twisted and the shadow Meng Jingzhou gradually solidified, his face softer and more gentle than the real Meng Jingzhou’s, with flowing long hair and a graceful figure. Each gesture carried an inexplicable charm, with a bulging chest.

"How is it a woman?!" Si Heng God Monarch exclaimed in surprise upon seeing the shadow Meng Jingzhou.

"It’s bullshit!" Meng Jingzhou flared up this time.

Lu Yang was stroking his chin and seemed to understand. Immortal Jiuchong had said that he could change Old Meng’s Pure Yang physique to a Pure Yin physique, though it would also require a gender change, which was rejected by Old Meng.

Si Heng God Monarch’s Yin Yang Dao Fruit Prototype could create a person with completely opposite thoughts, thereby manifesting the Pure Yin physique of Old Meng, which exactly confirmed Immortal Jiuchong’s words.

Meng Jingzhou was so irritated seeing his Pure Yin self that he didn’t wait for his shadow to seek him out; he took the initiative to fight his shadow self.

Today was destined for a decisive battle of sexes!

Si Heng God Monarch’s gaze fell on Lu Yang: "You’re up next!"

Si Heng God Monarch repeated his old trick, once more urging the Yin Yang Dao Fruit Prototype.

Lu Yang stayed calm, using a supreme Causality Supernatural Power he rarely resorted to: "Invert Yin and Yang."

Just when Si Heng God Monarch had locked on to Lu Yang, about to create a completely opposite shadow, he discovered Lu Yang’s Yin and Yang shift, as if he had become another person, rendering his preparation completely in vain.

"So what if you change Yin and Yang, I want to see how many times you can change!"

Si Heng God Monarch once again urged the Yin Yang Dao Fruit Prototype, while Lu Yin continued to use Invert Yin and Yang.

After several repetitions, Si Heng God Monarch was close to cursing out loud, realizing that if the stalemate continued, they could fight until tomorrow with no result.

"You think you can do whatever you want with the Causality Supernatural Power?"

His Yin Yang Dao Fruit Prototype had other ways to be used.

In Si Heng God Monarch’s left and right hands appeared black flames and suspended ice crystals, naturally far inferior to the Immortal Fire and Profound Ice created by Immortal Jiuchong, but this move was enough to put someone in the Transcendence Tribulation Stage in a life-and-death crisis!

Lu Yang was fearless in the face of this move, facing the incoming endless flames, he slightly shook the Green Peak Sword, deploying gusts of Sword Qi to envelop these flames, restraining them.

Ice crystals turned into countless ice spikes aimed at Lu Yang, coming into contact with even one would instantly freeze the body rigid, a fatal crisis in an equal-level battle!

Lu Yang swung the Green Peak Sword, keeping anything within three feet away from him, splitting any approaching ice spikes into two with his sword.

"This is impossible, what kind of sword is that?"

Even a sword of Immortal Treasure Level couldn’t ignore the extreme cold of the ice spikes, unless it was an Immortal Sword!

However, there were only two in the whole world capable of forging Immortal Swords, one was Yingtian Immortal and the other Emperor Wu Yao of Yu, both being mysterious and elusive, their traces unseen for aeons—the Great Qian Dynasty didn’t even possess a single Immortal Sword!

Lu Yang revealed a meaningful yet insubstantial smile, leaving Si Heng God Monarch to understand on his own.

Si Heng God Monarch couldn’t afford to think too much, as he sensed an intense crisis emanating from Lu Yang’s swordsmanship—a single misstep could lead to decapitation.

What swordsmanship was this, so exquisitely peerless!

Chi Sect Leader, watching the battle from not too far away, hesitantly asked the people to his left and right: "Do any of you feel that Young Sect Master Lu’s swordsmanship is very similar to Lu Yang’s Immortal Sword Technique? We have seen it before in the Extreme Northern Lands."

Mu Baiyi and Liu Zhen’s expressions changed drastically upon hearing this, they quickly pounced on Chi Sect Leader and covered his mouth firmly.

You’ve got eyes, can’t you see that Young Sect Master Lu is using the Immortal Sword Technique? The implications are self-evident.

But such matters couldn’t be outspoken!

While engaged in battle, Lu Yang still managed to keep an eye on his surroundings and heard Chi Sect Leader’s words, turning his head to reveal a warm smile.

"Ah, I originally wanted to act for a while longer. Chi Sect Leader, why did you have to spill the beans? What am I to do now?"

Chapter 1237: 1236: Fragile Space

Regarding exposing his identity, Lu Yang expressed his helplessness.

His opponent was the renowned Si Heng Divine Monarch; winning without putting up a full fight was very unlikely.

Using his full might, including his signature moves, made it rational for people from Yaoyang Sect and Jiuyou Sect to recognize him.

Well, after playing this role for so long, it was time to bring it to an end.

Mu Baiyi and Liu Zhen saw Lu Yang smile at them, their faces turned deathly pale.

Both were influential figures in their respective sects, far more intelligent than Chi Sect Leader, who only studied solar energy.

Run?

Lu Yang was the Young Sect Master, which meant that Heavenly Court Sect was actually Seek Dao Sect—a sect with a pair of immortals and too many half-immortals to count on one hand. Facing this power, they didn’t even feel the urge to run.

No, no, no, they shouldn’t think like that. If they insisted that the Young Sect Master had possessed Lu Yang, there might still be a way out.

"You are Lu Yang," Si Heng Divine Monarch also laughed, having heard Chi Sect Leader’s words.

Before this, he only knew that Heavenly Court Sect was fake, but not the real identity of the Young Sect Master. Now he finally understood.

Hearing Si Heng Divine Monarch’s words, Mu Baiyi and Liu Zhen felt desperate. It was bad enough that you were after the Reincarnation Dao Fruit Prototype, but now you were also cutting off their way of life.

Upon learning Lu Yang’s true identity, Si Heng Divine Monarch’s eyes showed a ruthless glint, "To be honest, dealing with you directly is quite difficult."

"Do you know, this Fengdu Space is filled with ghosts and heavy with Yin energy, which can be considered the Netherworld."

"If there’s a Netherworld, then there must be a World of the Living. What do you think constitutes the World of the Living?"

A bad premonition rose in Lu Yangxin’s heart; his sword glare became increasingly fierce. "What are you planning to do!"

Suddenly, countless mortals appeared in Fengdu Space, all looking lost and confused.

"Where is this?"

"It’s so cold."

Si Heng Divine Monarch had transported all the citizens of Zhongshan County to Fengdu Space. Now, this space was not only filled with Jiuyou Sect followers but also countless innocent citizens!

This was how he stealthily entered Fengdu Space.

Lord Man, seeing the two Senior Brothers in the sky, was surprised too. Weren’t the Senior Brothers supposed to go to Fengdu Space? Is this place really Fengdu Space, and who are they fighting against, why are people from Zhongshan County here?

Holding black fire and ice crystal in his hands, Si Heng Divine Monarch clapped them together. The extreme substances collided and fused into a black tornado mixed with ice crystals.

This was an ice-fire tornado that could wreak havoc in Fengdu Space; if it descended, the casualties would be severe.

"I wonder if you’ll catch or dodge this move!" Now all the citizens of Zhongshan County were his hostages, and he didn’t believe Lu Yang would dare to avoid this move.

Lu Yang’s expression was cold as water, "It seems I can only take it head-on... Did you think I would do that!"

Suddenly, he moved like lightning, wielding the Green Peak Sword to slash the black tornado. Si Heng Divine Monarch laughed, believing a sword could not cut through wind.

However, in the next moment, he was proven wrong. The black tornado was directly sucked into the Small World of the Green Peak Sword.

"Truly worthy of being an Immortal Sword, it even has its own small world!"

"But can you absorb my attacks that easily!"

Lu Yang looked at him with a half-smile, "Si Heng Divine Monarch, it seems your mastery of space spells is somewhat lacking, isn’t it?"

"What does that mean?" Si Heng God Monarch didn’t understand why Lu Yang had asked this.

Lu Yang didn’t answer; while speaking, he unleashed a charged strike with his sword. Si Heng God Monarch blocked it with his bloody staff, yet the sword’s force was still so overwhelming that it sliced open a crack in space, directly knocking Si Heng out of the Fengdu Space.

Si Heng God Monarch was both shocked and furious. Who built this space? It’s so fragile it breaks on contact!

Such a level shouldn’t even be used to construct spaces; it’s embarrassing!

He had considered the possibility of Lu Yang knocking him out of the Fengdu Space, but he quickly dismissed it. The reason was simple; space is supposed to be extremely sturdy, impossible to be split open with a single sword strike, and during their fight, he wouldn’t let Lu Yang have the opportunity to sever space repeatedly.

Who would have thought this rotten space would crack with a single sword strike!

Since countless Qingfeng Swords appeared beneath Si Heng God Monarch’s feet, he hurriedly jumped away, only to find the Qingfeng Swords multiplying in an inconceivable number, encircling him.

Swordmanship Domain!

By this time, Zhongshan County had turned into a ghost town. While in Fengdu Space, Lu Yang was somewhat restrained in his movements, but now he could fully engage in battle!

Lu Yang landed and spawned a Wooden Avatar, which used the Qingfeng Sword Elephant Form Boxing to turn into another Qingfeng Sword.

"Ten Thousand Swords Returning to Origin!"

The Inheriting Shadow Sword, Seven Star Sword Set, Wooden Avatar’s Qingfeng Sword, and the real Qingfeng Sword fused into one. Lu Yang’s Sword Intent soared, causing Si Heng God Monarch’s hair to stand on end—his opponent was more dangerous than he had estimated!

Buddha Land in Palm appeared; a colossal handprint descended from the sky, enveloping the whole Zhongshan County. The ridges formed by its fingerprints were large enough to hold mountains.

"Don’t think you are the only one who can use this move; Universe in Palm!"

Si Heng God Monarch unleashed a similar Divine Ability, forming a huge palm to counter the giant sky-covering handprint.

The two massive handprints collided, causing space to fluctuate.

Si Heng God Monarch was so tired he was gasping for air; this move was extremely draining of spiritual essence. He assumed his opponent must be catching his breath just like him.

However, he didn’t know that Lu Yang’s power came from his Buddha Country Avatar. Drawing from a seemingly inexhaustible source, using Buddha Land in Palm was nothing to him.

Moving like a specter, he appeared behind Si Heng God Monarch. Before the latter could react, he cleaved down. Si Heng God Monarch had a layer of Protective Golden Light over his body, but in front of Lu Yang, it was as flimsy as paper, easily pierced through. Si Heng God Monarch was split in two!

But Si Heng God Monarch did not die; each half turned into an independent entity—they were his Yin Yang Two Forms.

...

In the Fengdu Space, Man Gu had roughly figured out that this experience was the result of his Senior Brothers fighting the enemy.

The priority now was to stabilize the public’s spirit. Those here were citizens from Zhongshan County and the twenty counties under its jurisdiction. It would be chaotic even for Cultivators suddenly transported here, not to mention the civilians. With such chaos, if they weren’t affected by the battle above, major incidents like stampedes and robberies would occur!

He hastily gathered everyone, issued orders, while the secretary assisted on the side, reminding Man Gu of any details he had overlooked.

Man Gu had just assumed the position of County Magistrate and was supposed to still be establishing his authority. However, who in the higher echelons of Zhongshan County wouldn’t fear Man Gu’s notorious fierceness? None dared to disobey openly or secretly.

After dispatching the orders, the upper echelons received their orders and left. Man Gu, parched and dry-mouthed, guzzled down water and felt much better.

"You are Lord Man Man Gu, right?" Suddenly, a group approached Man Gu, and he recognized them.

Because he knew them, Man Gu was all the more astonished and even began to feel wary.

"What do you plan to do?"

Mu Baiyi rubbed his hands and smiled, "I am here representing the Jiuyou Sect to surrender."

Following him, Chi Sect Leader and Liu Zhen said, "We are here representing the Yaoyang Sect to surrender."

"Ah?"

Chapter 1238: 1237 Thief King: Who Signed the Insurance for Zhongshan County?

Mu Baiyi had no doubt about the outcome of this battle. With the advantage of the Heavenly Court Sect, it wouldn’t matter even if Si Heng God Monarch himself showed up, let alone the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord.

Rather than waiting for the fight to end and be captured, it would be better to take advantage of the ongoing battle and surrender to Man Gu.

As a Disciple of Dao Seeking Sect and closely related to Lu Yang and Meng Jingzhou, as well as being a dynasty official and the Top Scorer, Man Gu had dual identities, making him the most suitable choice for self-surrender.

"This is the name list of our sect," Mu Baiyi said, bringing a Confession with him.

"This belongs to our sect," Chi Sect Leader stated.

"Alright, I’ve taken note of your surrender. Can you guarantee the safety of the people in this county?" Man Gu asked bewilderedly as he collected two Confessions. Now was not the time to consider his achievements; they were still not out of danger.

The bodies of Meng Jingzhou and six Jiuyou Sect leaders were all fighting against their shadows, meaning there were fourteen Transcendance Tribulation Stage beings fighting in the sky. Such high-level battles were exceedingly rare throughout history.

Any attack that landed would result in countless injuries and deaths.

"We guarantee to complete the mission." It was a significant opportunity for meritorious service, and Mu Baiyi and his companions would naturally not refuse.

With three Transcendance Tribulation Stage experts joining, the safety of the people in this county was assured, but a strategy to break the deadlock in the sky was still needed. With that in mind, Man Gu headed towards the direction of the prison.

The citizens of Zhongshan County had been moved by Si Heng God Monarch as they were, without changing their positions. Therefore, it was not difficult for Man Gu to find the original prison location.

At the prison, the prisoners were stacked on top of each other like Arhats, wailing in pain; Thief King sat at the very top, waving at Man Gu: "Yo, Lord Governor is here."

He slapped the buttock of the person he was sitting on and casually said, "These prisoners wanted to escape, but I caught them all. How about it, does this count as a merit for me?"

"Certainly, it does."

Man Gu bowed respectfully to Thief King: "May I request the senior to join the battle?"

The fourteen figures in the sky were evenly matched, their moves counteracting each other’s yin and yang, making it difficult to determine a victor. To break the stalemate, new combatants needed to join.

And Thief King was the most suitable force for the job.

"Sure," Thief King readily agreed. If the Thief Ancestor heard that he did nothing in this battle, he’d probably hang him up and whip him.

Moreover, he enjoyed seeing those officials beseech him for help. It was a perfect opportunity to do a good deed when it suited him.

He took out a dart coated with lethal poison and threw it at one of the six shadows; the shadow couldn’t react in time to the dart and got an arm wounded.

Thief King frowned: "Doesn’t work, huh?"

The poison on the dart was the kind that could seal one’s throat upon entering the bloodstream, and even those at the Transcendance Tribulation Stage would need to divert their focus to suppress it. However, since the shadow seemed unaffected by the poison, it indicated that the opponent had a unique constitution and was immune to poison.

"I’ll have to try another method."

Thief King vanished in an instant, so fast it was incomparable, pulling out eight darts that encircled him as a shield.

He joined the battle and with just one strike, severed a finger from one of the opponents.

"You’re not up to standard," Thief King commented as he weaved in and out of the battleground. The shadows all tried to attack him, but not a single strike could hit him. On the contrary, his attacks were always fruitful.

"Lord Man, what do you think of my skills?"

"Thief King Senior is impressive."

"Good to know that. However, I can’t handle the battle outside, so you should prepare yourself mentally," he warned.

Thief King couldn’t help but laugh at the thought of what Zhongshan County would look like after the battle: "With two Semi-Immortals fighting in Zhongshan County, regardless of who wins or loses, your county will have to be rebuilt, right?"

He had seen both Lu Yang’s and Si Heng God Monarch’s capabilities, and with his cultivation level, not only would he be of no help, but there was also a risk of getting caught in their crossfire.

Man Gu hadn’t expected Thief King Senior to be so thoughtful. He respectfully expressed his gratitude: "Thank you for your concern, senior. I’ve already purchased insurance for this county, so there will be compensation from the Land Money Trade Association after the battle."

Thief King: "..."

Damn it, who signed such a huge insurance policy, and how come I, as the president, do not know about it!

Thinking of this, Thief King wished he could hurry to Zhongshan County, maybe he could lend a hand.

However, the shadows’ attacks did not cease, and Thief King had no opportunity to leave Fengdu Space.

"Damn it, damn it, you’re forcing my hand!"

Thief King got infuriated by the shadows, since you won’t let me leave, then don’t think you’ll have it easy!

He struck out aggressively, shedding his previously frivolous style, and his combat became extremely fierce.

The magic skill attached to the eight darts was fully activated; these eight darts represented the eight directions, the technique of Qimen Dunjia. He also used it to nurture eight different spirits, capable of employing divine abilities like calling the wind and summoning the rain, shaking mountains and earth.

"To death with you!"

The shadows were attacked one after another by Thief King, the balance of the battlefield visibly tilted toward him at an observable rate.

Not fast enough, still not fast enough!

Thief King’s attacks became even more severe, with every second that passed, another building in the outside world would be damaged, and it would all be his fault!

Faster, still faster!

Thief King was so fast he was panting and parched, yet still frantic, wishing he could end the battle in an instant.

Suddenly, he felt lighter, as if something had appeared inside his body that could make him even faster.

But his speed just now was already at its limit.

He inspected himself and, to his surprise, found that a rapidly spinning fruit had materialized within him at some point, the fruit seemed real yet illusory, like a dream.

"Is this the Speed Dao Fruit Outline that the Thief Ancestor mentioned?"

Thief King rejoiced internally. He had always lamented his inability to condense the Speed Dao Fruit Outline, but he had unexpectedly broken through his limits and condensed the Dao Fruit Outline in this battle.

He could now consider himself a Semi-Immortal.

No, this isn’t the time to think about this, he needed to quickly dispose of these enemies!

For the first time, Thief King felt weightless in his own body, truly embodying what it meant to be swift.

So, this was the kind of speed the Thief Ancestor possessed, no wonder I could never escape from his palm during my spellcasting.

The movements of the shadows seemed like slow motion to Thief King, posing no threat whatsoever.

He, like the autumn wind sweeping away fallen leaves, heavily injured six shadows.

He initially wanted to join the fight between Meng Jingzhou and the shadows, but the battlefield there was alternating between extreme cold and extreme heat; even with incredible speed, he couldn’t get involved, couldn’t get near the battlefield.

Forget it, they don’t need me here; it’s time to help out in the outside world.

Thief King tore open the space and arrived in Zhongshan County.

However, contrary to expectation, Zhongshan County was largely intact, showing barely any signs of battle.

What’s going on?

Suddenly, darkness loomed overhead, and a vast, boundless handprint appeared; Thief King had no doubt that if it landed, Zhongshan County would be reduced to rubble.

Si Heng God Monarch’s Yin Yang Two Forms, in order not to be hit, had to resist with full force: "Universe in Palm!"

Buddha Land in Palm descended, Universe in Palm ascended, the two giant handprints collided in the sky, and Zhongshan County narrowly escaped disaster amidst this delicate balance.

"Ha ha ha, let’s see how long you can hold on!"

Lu Yang laughed wildly, continuing to exert Buddha Land in Palm, since his strength came from his Buddha Country Avatar, he could never run out, and he didn’t believe Si Heng could outlast him!

Thief King silently observed the battle, caught in a dilemma.

Whom should I help?

Chapter 1239: 1238 Qilin Immortal

Fengdu Space.

"Even fakes dare to fight me!" Meng Jingzhou roared, his body flaring up with something similar to flames.

This wasn’t fire, but rather his qi and blood power was so abundant that his pure yang aura burnt the surrounding spiritual energy.

Anyone who got close would find their own pure yang aura ignited, which was why the Thief King dared not approach Meng Jingzhou.

The burning of pure yang aura had never happened from ancient times till now. If one had to give it a name, it could be called the Pure Yang Domain.

As for the shadow Meng Jingzhou, what he exhibited was naturally the Pure Yin Domain.

Meng Jingzhou’s fist technique was supremely yang and incredibly domineering, while shadow Meng Jingzhou’s fist technique was supremely yin and ultimately soft, as good as water. With yin and yang clashing, it was like the sun and moon competing in splendor, resembling Tai Chi, making the onlookers marvel.

The six shadow figures sustained heavy injuries from the Thief King’s attacks, and the six former Jiuyou Sect Masters soon claimed victory. Mu Baiyi intended to control these corpses to help Meng Jingzhou.

However, with the extremely intense pure yang and pure yin energies on Meng Jingzhou’s side, the corpses couldn’t get close, so they could only watch from a distance.

Fighting against his complete opposite, Meng Jingzhou grew more and more agitated. Even Old Lu had started to engage with a Semi-Immortal, yet here he was, trapped by the little tricks spun by the Si Heng God Monarch.

Whatever move he made, his opponent would counter with the exact opposite.

"I just don’t believe it!"

Meng Jingzhou, determined, stripped off his upper clothing ready for a big battle.

Then he saw his opposition also beginning to undress.

"Don’t copy that!"

Meng Jingzhou, in desperation, unleashed the Supreme Pure Yang Dharma Image and smashed it towards his opponent, blocking his further actions.

While everyone watched Meng Jingzhou’s fierce battle, a figure appeared at the edge of the altar, stepping onto it and poised to dig out the sapling and the Reincarnation Dao Fruit Prototype.

"Who’s there!" Mu Baiyi was the first to notice the commotion, bellowed out, and the six corpses of the Tribulation Crossing Sect Masters made their move, bombarding the figure with six ultimate techniques.

After they acted, Mu Baiyi regretted it, as the figure was too close to the sapling and could harm it.

But to Mu Baiyi’s astonishment, once the smoke cleared, the figure and the sapling were completely unharmed.

How did he do it?

"As a subordinate of the Emperor, you may also call me Lord Si Shi," said the shadowy figure.

Man Gu was startled; Lord Si Shi was the last of the Great Qian’s Four God Monarchs. Could it be that Great Qian had sent two Divine Monarchs to the Fengdu Space?

It was said that Lord Si Shi’s survival of the fittest Dao Fruit Prototype was condensed through the power of faith. In terms of talent, he was not as good as Si Heng, but due to the special nature of his Dao Fruit Prototype, nobody wished to clash with him.

Upon suffering the same attack twice, it would become very difficult to harm him.

To fight with him, one must employ a different tactic each time.

"Well, I’ll be taking this Reincarnation Dao Fruit Prototype. No need to see me out; I know the way."

Lord Si Shi smiled, revealing eight snow-white teeth, and picked up the sapling and ran.

By the time Mu Baiyi reached the altar, the other party was nowhere to be seen.

Humming a tune, Lord Si Shi left the Fengdu Space and arrived in Zhongshan County.

He came to a tea house in Zhongshan County, which at this time was empty except for one tea customer who had not been moved from the uppermost floor.

The tea patron was watching the confrontation between Lu Yang and Si Heng God Monarch, not expecting Si Heng God Monarch to fare so poorly, being suppressed by someone from later generations.

"This youngster named Lu Yang truly possesses some extraordinary abilities."

Lord Si Shi bowed his head with clasped hands, "Reporting to Emperor Lord, the Reincarnation Dao Fruit Prototype has been retrieved."

"Oh?"

Mid-heaven Emperor Lord ceased to watch the battle, the outcome of the fight between Lu Yang and Si Heng God Monarch was of no consequence, retrieving the Reincarnation Dao Fruit Prototype was the mission that had now been accomplished.

"So this is the Reincarnation Dao Fruit Prototype, that after swallowing and refining one could live another life even after perishing?" A rare smile appeared on the face of the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord.

As the founder of the Great Qian Dynasty who ruled over the world, Mid-heaven Emperor Lord wielded countless rare treasures, but none as precious as the Reincarnation Dao Fruit Prototype.

He had arrived in Zhongshan County very early, waiting for the Reincarnation Dao Fruit Prototype to mature. He hadn’t come in person to retrieve it, because he held a psychological shadow from the Extreme Northern Lands, worrying that Yun Zhi might suddenly appear; hence he dispatched Si Heng God Monarch and Lord Si Shi in succession.

With the Reincarnation Dao Fruit Prototype in hand and Yun Zhi still nowhere to be seen, it seemed he had been overly concerned.

"Emperor Lord, is this Reincarnation Dao Fruit Prototype really as miraculous as it sounds? How come I’ve never heard of such a thing before?" Lord Si Shi curiously inquired.

"Could it be false? This is... not something you should know."

Mid-heaven Emperor Lord couldn’t wait to grab the Reincarnation Dao Fruit Prototype, but saw Lord Si Shi retracting it.

The face of the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord turned ugly in an instant, "Si Shi, what do you mean by this!"

Lord Si Shi simply smiled, as a strange voice emerged from his body.

"No meaning at all, just that the Reincarnation Dao Fruit Prototype is known by only a few in the Fire Dynasty, and I want to know where you learned about the Reincarnation Dao Fruit Prototype!"

Boom—

Mid-heaven Emperor Lord furiously slammed the tea table, demanding from Lord Si Shi as the teahouse collapsed with a crash, "Who are you!"

A figure exuding a ferocious aura floated out of Lord Si Shi’s bracelet, its head topped with two coral horns, its cheeks and the backs of hands covered with red scales, its pupils dragon-like, tiger-like, as if it were the supreme Venerate among demons.

"I am the Qilin Immortal that you have been searching for."

"Qilin Immortal, you’ve been hiding in Si Shi’s bracelet all along!"

Mid-heaven Emperor Lord was enraged, Lord Si Shi’s bracelet looked ordinary, he claimed it was a relic from his mother that he had worn all along, but to think that the Qilin Immortal was concealed within!

"This cannot be, if you’ve been in the bracelet all this time, why did the Seeking Immortal Token not react!"

The Seeking Immortal Token was a proud creation of Mid-heaven Emperor Lord, it would heat up whenever the Four Ancient Immortals were nearby, the closer they were, the hotter it would become.

Qilin Immortal laughed casually, "I’m afraid the Emperor Lord has forgotten my Dao Fruit is about survival of the fittest, a move only works once against me. I let Si Shi use the Seeking Immortal Token to get close to me once, after that, it became useless against me."

"Teacher." With the guidance of Qilin Immortal, Lord Si Shi could condense his own Dao Fruit Prototype, but that would make him stand out among the God Monarchs, so he chose to use the Power of Faith to condense his Dao Fruit Prototype.

Qilin Immortal took the sapling and Reincarnation Dao Fruit Prototype, reverted his head to its original form, opened his mouth, and swallowed them, temporarily storing them in his stomach, "Si Shi, step back for now."

Si Shi tactfully stepped back, the tension between them palpable, far from a situation that looked as though it could be resolved peacefully.

"I’ll ask you one more time, who told you about the Reincarnation Dao Fruit Prototype!"

Mid-heaven Emperor Lord glared daggers at the Qilin Immortal, his teeth clenched in anger. He had tried every means to find the Four Ancient Immortals, not expecting that they were right beside him, playing him for a fool, "If I told you, would you give me the Reincarnation Dao Fruit Prototype?"

"No."

Mid-heaven Emperor Lord immediately expanded a vortex that devoured everything, "Then there’s no more need for words, let’s see who’s truly stronger!"

Chapter 1240: 1239: All Too Familiar

The Thief King finally ended his inner struggle and decided to go against his conscience to help Lu Yang.

"I’ll help you..."

Before the Thief King could finish, he heard a thunderous boom behind him. A monstrous tidal wave of energy erupted, flinging him into the air, making it impossible for him to stand steady.

Turning his head, the Thief King saw two figures emanating terrifying auras in a standoff, surrounded by ruins under their feet.

And that wasn’t all. As the auras from the two figures grew even more terrifying and rapidly spread outward from them as the center, wherever his gaze fell, Zhongshan County turned to ruins.

Frozen in place and allowing the cold wind to howl through him, the Thief King’s heart sank in despair.

What use is it that I’ve outlined the Speed Dao Fruit?

"Mid-heaven Emperor Lord?"

"Qilin Immortal?"

Lu Yang and Si Heng God Monarch were startled to see the two combatants and, in a tacit understanding, ceased their fighting, with the Yin Yang Body of Si Heng God Monarch merging into one.

Lu Yang was utterly confused seeing the Immortals battle. It made some sense for the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord to appear here since it could be explained that he, being cautious by nature, sent Si Heng God Monarch to test the waters while he waited for good news outside.

But the question was how the Qilin Immortal had suddenly appeared, and why were the two of them fighting each other?

Fairy Eternity was lying on a bed within the Spiritual Space, legs kicking up and down in the air, instructing Lu Yang on how he should fight Si Heng God Monarch: "Little Yang, you should fight like this... Huh, why is the Qilin Immortal here?"

Seeing the Qilin Immortal in battle, Fairy Eternity suddenly perked up, climbed up from the bed, and watched the Immortal Battle intently.

"That’s not the right way to fight. Why defend now? Go for a mutual injury strike. Little Yang, go over there and tell him how to fight!"

Lu Yang looked towards the center of the battlefield, felt a sense of futility, and quietly withdrew his gaze.

Fairy, leave me a way out, will you?

"Are you Lu Yang?" Lord Si Shi flew over—or rather, was blown over by the blast of the battle.

"My name is Survival God Monarch Si Shi. If it’s a hassle, feel free to call me Lord Si Shi," Lord Si Shi introduced himself in a rather familiar manner.

Having captured so many Great Qian prisoners, Lu Yang of course recognized Lord Si Shi.

"What are you here for!" Lu Yang watched Lord Si Shi guardedly. The God Lords of Great Qian were supposed to be his enemies, yet something about this didn’t feel right.

"My teacher said if he got into a fight with the Emperor, I should stand by your side. If you’re lucky, you might survive."

"Who’s your teacher?" Lu Yang, utterly confused, thought it might also be due to Fairy Eternity constantly prattling in the Spiritual Space, affecting his thinking.

"Si Shi, why are you standing with the enemy!" Si Heng God Monarch shouted angrily.

Lord Si Shi spread his hands: "Isn’t it obvious? I’ve defected."

"You!"

Si Heng God Monarch was so choked up he couldn’t speak.

"I advise you to stand over here too, it’s safe." Lord Si Shi kindly offered advice to his former colleague, noting that observing the Immortal Battle up close was extremely dangerous.

Si Heng God Monarch, though not book smart, was no fool. He sneered, seeing through Lord Si Shi’s intention: "You think I would believe a traitor’s words? I’ve already seen through your schemes. You must want to lure me over there and then join forces with Lu Yang to capture me, making me a Confession!"

Just then, without any sign, Lu Yang shuffled a few steps to the side, and Lord Si Shi quickly followed.

Si Heng God Monarch did not understand the move, but suddenly from the center of the Double Immortal Battlefield, an attack flew over, directly hitting where Lu Yang was just standing. It struck Si Heng God Monarch, who instantly exploded with injuries all over his body.

"Cough cough cough—" Si Heng God Monarch’s body rapidly healed.

Every living creature is composed of Yin and Yang, and as long as one maintains the balance between the two within the body, one can prevent all illnesses and heal wounds spontaneously.

Suddenly another aftershock flew over, and Si Heng God Monarch couldn’t dodge in time. He was blasted beyond recognition. On the other hand, Lu Yang seemed to foresee the future, predict where the aftershock would appear, and avoided it in advance.

Si Heng God Monarch chose to trust the traitor’s words and approached Lu Yang cautiously, step by careful step, and only when he saw that neither Lu Yang nor Lord Si Shi intended to strike him did he relax a bit.

It was Fairy Eternity who had anticipated the incoming attack and advised Lu Yang to move aside earlier.

"How come you all are together?" The Thief King regained his senses and, finding Lu Yang and the two Great Qian God Lords standing together, chatting and laughing, felt like he had missed something and curiously flew over.

"Young Sect Master," Lu Yang greeted respectfully.

The Thief King waved his hands frantically, "No, no, don’t call me ’senior.’ You are the senior."

The strikes that Lu Yang had directed at Si Heng God Monarch a moment ago had nearly caused him to faint from fright; he had been praying the whole time for Si Heng God Monarch to hold on.

Thankfully, Si Heng God Monarch lived up to expectations.

But considering the current situation, it didn’t seem to count for much.

As they were talking, a black vortex flew by from the Emperor Lord, passing close but not harming anyone. Nonetheless, it still made the four of them shudder.

Where the black vortex landed, only a large pit remained – the buildings and land had completely vanished.

Devouring Dao Fruit, devouring all things.

"Teacher was right; staying close to Fellow Daoist Lu Yang really does save lives," Lord Si Shi remarked with a smile. If it had been just him, he would have been severely injured long ago.

"You’re Lu Yang?" the Thief King asked in surprise.

"Yeah, what about it?" Lu Yang wiped his face, reverting it to its original look. Since entering Fengdu Space, he had been using the appearance of Young Master Lu, and even before that, when he was a constable, he had used another face. It was normal for the Thief King not to recognize him.

The Thief King’s expression turned increasingly strange: "My master is the Thief Ancestor."

The Thief King had become a disciple after being captured by the Thief Ancestor, and it was from the Thief Ancestor that he learned the key to condensing the Speed Dao Fruit Outline. However, the Thief Ancestor had stopped teaching him during the final step, leaving him to comprehend it on his own.

"Your master is the Thief Ancestor?" Lu Yang heard the insinuation in the Thief King’s words; the Thief Ancestor and Daoist Buyu were sworn friends, which made them peers.

The Thief King nodded, pretending to chat while probing for information: "By the way, I heard from Lord Man that Zhongshan County bought insurance. Do you know about this?"

"Yeah, I suggested Junior Brother Man buy it."

The Thief King recalled all the large transactions that had been signed at the commerce association, and to his knowledge, Zhongshan County had never bought insurance before: "When did you buy the insurance?"

"Seven days ago."

The Thief King: "..."

So, they had captured me and immediately insured Zhongshan County afterward!?

If Lu Yang didn’t know the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord and Qilin Immortal, he would have suspected that Lu Yang had colluded with these two Immortals.

Wait, didn’t one of the Great Qian God Lords just refer to Lu Yang as "Daoist"? What’s Lu Yang’s relationship with the Great Qian Force?

"Who’s the teacher you’ve been referring to?" Lu Yang turned to ask Lord Si Shi.

"The Qilin Immortal. He said you know him."

Lu Yang nodded; of course, he knew the Qilin Immortal. Although they had never met face to face, he was quite familiar with him: "What else did Qilin Immortal Senior say?"

"He said you should be quite familiar with the Immortal Fairies."

Lu Yang’s face turned ashen.

Listening from the side, the Thief King felt more and more that there was something extraordinary about Lu Yang.

Chapter 1241: 1240: Mid-heaven Emperor Lord Battles Qilin Immortal

The Mid-heaven Emperor Lord stood in the midst of a pitch-black vortex, the space near the vortex distorted, and time slowed to a crawl.

Suddenly, a black vortex emerged behind the Qilin Immortal, and one of his arms was viciously torn and devoured, blood streaming down.

The Qilin Immortal’s face was dark and grim as he concentrated his will on the severed limb, which regrew as if by self-healing.

"Hahahahaha, after all those grand claims, Qilin Immortal, is that all the power you possess!"

The Mid-heaven Emperor Lord laughed wildly. His Devouring Dao Fruit could swallow anything; even an Immortal Body was consumed entirely by him. If this happened a few more times, even if the Qilin Immortal could regenerate a severed limb, he wouldn’t last long.

The Mid-heaven Emperor Lord was not worried about accidentally harming the Reincarnation Dao Fruit Prototype in the belly of the Qilin Immortal. Rather, it would be just right to devour and refine it.

"This much power is enough to deal with you," came the response.

As his words ended, the Qilin Immortal let out a long howl, as if tilting heaven and earth, in the star-studded Ancient Times. Yet, this roar alone could cause the galaxy to dissolve into countless meteors streaking across the universe.

The Buddhist Lion Roar was like an ant shaking a tree in the face of the true Qilin Roar.

The Mid-heaven Emperor Lord stamped his foot fiercely, creating ripples in space that collided with the Qilin Roar. The sky shook, the earth sank, and Lu Yang and three others not far away joined hands to withstand the impact of the shockwave.

"Quick, cover your ears!" the Thief King used his Spiritual Sense to urgently remind everyone as he saw the scene unfold.

"Don’t cover your ears; it won’t block it, counter with a soundwave attack!" Lu Yang also used his Spiritual Sense to shout.

All four were somewhat proficient in soundwave attacks, and even though their skills varied, they all knew moves like the Buddhist Lion Roar.

Si Heng God Monarch, Lord Si Shi, the Thief King, three of them mimicked the howls of Demon Beasts, while Lu Yang’s voice stuck out among the four, using the Fairy Shout.

However, his was also the most effective, blocking the majority of the soundwaves that reached them with the Fairy Shout.

It was just that the others gave Lu Yang strange looks.

The winds changed, thunder sliced across the sky, and Zhongshan County was covered by dark clouds, with countless tornadoes spiraling into being, wrapping wind, rain, thunder, and lightning within.

As the only Kirin among heaven and earth, the Qilin Immortal was a favorite of nature, inherently intertwined with natural phenomena. Although his mastery over thunder and lightning wasn’t on par with the Yingtian Immortal, aside from the Yingtian Immortal, no other Immortal’s lightning spells could surpass his.

"As the Emperor who shares a lifespan with heaven and earth, how could thunder and lightning harm me!"

The Mid-heaven Emperor Lord stood immovable in the face of the apocalyptic onslaught of thunder and lightning.

The sky reverberated with the deafening boom of thunder, aiming straight for the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord, but the pitch-black vortex devoured the thunder, leaving him unscathed.

The Mid-heaven Emperor Lord snapped his fingers, and the dark vortexes spread across the entire space. The newly regrown arm of the Qilin Immortal, along with a leg, was once again swallowed. That was the source of the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord’s confidence—his Devouring Dao Fruit was both offensive and defensive, capable of guarding against Immortal Techniques and devouring Immortal Bodies, leaving his foes powerless.

The Qilin Immortal’s body grew back once more. This was not some magic skill, but a natural self-healing ability he was born with.

"I’d like to see how many times you can heal!"

The dark vortex once again swept in, completely engulfing the Qilin Immortal, distorting space, nullifying any Space Spells there, and twisting time to make the Qilin Immortal’s movements extremely slow.

The previous two times, the Qilin Immortal somehow managed to dodge the vortexes, but this time, he made no move to escape and merely stood there.

"Have you resigned to your fate?" The Mid-heaven Emperor Lord activated his Devouring Dao Fruit, and several vortexes suddenly expanded to fully envelop the Qilin Immortal, the ease of it making the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord wonder if the one standing there was just a Puppet of the Qilin Immortal.

"Emperor, is that all the power you have?"

The Qilin Immortal stood amidst the black vortex, completely unharmed!

"If it were the Yingtian Immortal, dealing with you would indeed be restricting. Unfortunately for you, you’re facing me," the Qilin Immortal commented with a smile.

"The Dao Fruit of the Fittest! You intentionally took the hits the first two times to adapt to my attacks!"

The Mid-heaven Emperor Lord instantly realized that the same attacks could no longer injure the Qilin Immortal.

Facing the assault of the Dao Fruit Prototype, the Lord Si Shi could only avoid most of the damage, and couldn’t remain untouched. The Mid-heaven Emperor Lord hadn’t expected that the Qilin Immortal, as a bearer of an Upper Dao Fruit, could so remarkably adapt even to attacks from the Dao Fruit itself.

The Mid-heaven Emperor Lord’s face gradually became overcast with shadows, "It seems that to defeat you, I must use different techniques."

"Three Flavored True Fire!"

"Qingtian Prison Suppressing Hand!"

The Hand of Heaven descended from above, ablaze with the mighty Three Flavored True Fire, and the palm was filled with Magic Runes that bore the power to suppress and destroy. A single rune could obliterate all of Zhongshan County. The vast number of runes were clenched within the Hand of Heaven, and their collective force was unimaginable.

The Qingtian Prison Suppressing Hand was a technique the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord had chanced upon in ancient tomes, one that he had never used before, and even The Controller didn’t know he could perform it.

This technique was tremendously powerful and was not inferior to the Buddha Land in Palm.

With a thunderous crash, the Hand of Heaven suppressed the Qilin Immortal, who simply raised a hand without a hint of Mana fluctuation, and caught the formidable punch.

"Three Flavored True Fire and Qingtian Prison Suppressing Hand, huh? You’ve mastered them well. Unfortunately, these techniques are useless against me, as I have already seen them in Ancient Times," the Qilin Immortal retorted nonchalantly.

The Mid-heaven Emperor Lord’s face turned ashen. A great many of his techniques originated from Ancient Times, and without anyone to teach him, he had managed to master the essence on his own—a rare talent in the world.

But this also meant that the more he had mastered the essence, the more useless his techniques became against the Qilin Immortal. Most of his repertoire had no effect on his opponent.

"As your senior, I’ve already allowed you three moves. If you have no other techniques, it’s my turn now," the Qilin Immortal confidently declared.

With a shake of his form, the Qilin Immortal transformed into a golden Qilin, stepping on auspicious clouds, rising through the mists, a sight of majestic awe. In Ancient Times, even placing his image on a door could ward off evil and protect the household.

"Do you think I only know ancient techniques?"

The Mid-heaven Emperor Lord, always appearing with the air of a senior in the present era, was inwardly prideful. Now the Qilin Immortal was claiming to be his senior, boldly asserting to have given him three moves, which immediately incensed him.

"Taiyi Seven Killings Palm!"

"Liangyi Micro Dust Formation!"

"Creation Heaven Technique!"

The Mid-heaven Emperor Lord unleashed three successive Immortal Techniques, each a self-created move powerful enough to establish a sect and serve as the trump cards for those at the Pass Tribulation Peak and at the level of a Semi-Immortal.

The Liangyi Micro Dust Formation trapped the Qilin Immortal, with the Taiyi Seven Killings Palm claiming life with each strike. The seven palms layered over one another were so potent that not even Heaven could save the victim, and the Creation Heaven Technique could prevent the Qilin Immortal from healing his wounds.

"How have I never seen these techniques even once?" Si Heng God Monarch exclaimed in surprise.

As the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord’s confidant, even if the Emperor Lord had kept some techniques from him, it shouldn’t have been so many.

Trapped in the Liangyi Micro Dust Formation, the Qilin Immortal defended while taunting, "Ha, sealed by Wu Yao in the Sun for one hundred thousand years, you really have come up with quite a few tricks."

"Shut up!"

The Mid-heaven Emperor Lord grew furious. The life span of Semi-Immortals was limited, and in order to wait for the Great Era, they had been slumbering throughout the entire Great Yu Dynasty. The fact that Wu Yao had sealed him in the Sun was only known to The Controller.

Little did he expect that the Qilin Immortal had been hiding in Lord Si Shi’s body and witnessed the whole process.

Chapter 1242: 1241: The Strength of Qilin Immortal

The Taiyi Seven Killings Palm not only contained a killing intent in each palm strike, but the seventh strike could stack the killing intents of the previous six strikes, an assault even Immortals couldn’t withstand.

He had now executed six palm strikes, each accurately hitting the Qilin Immortal. After receiving the six strikes and having his healing abilities suppressed by the Creation Heaven Technique, the Qilin Immortal’s blood wouldn’t stop flowing.

"Taiyi Seven Killings Palm, the seventh strike!"

The seven killing seals overlapped, and dark strength erupted within the Qilin Immortal’s body, but the expected bleeding from seven orifices did not occur. The Qilin Immortal remained unharmed except for spitting two mouthfuls of black blood, which compared to the first six strikes, was not a severe injury.

What was happening, had the seventh strike been adapted by the Survival Of The Fittest Dao Fruit?

This shouldn’t be, the move was called the Taiyi Seven Killings Palm, but it was actually a combination of seven techniques. Logically, the Qilin Immortal shouldn’t have adapted to this move, and if he had, he wouldn’t be spitting blood.

Why exactly?

The Mid-heaven Emperor Lord realized the Qilin Immortal was absolutely no ordinary foe, donned his Immortal Gold Armor, and went to battle a second time.

Though it seemed the Qilin Immortal was at a disadvantage, continuously taking hits, he felt an unsettling sensation within, as if he were overlooking something.

The Qilin Immortal had adapted to the Liangyi Micro Dust Formation and could no longer be restrained by it. With a long cry, he charged once more at the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord, emanating the supreme pressure of the Supreme Demon, actually completely overshadowing the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord in terms of aura.

But how could the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord be suppressed by mere aura? He struck with formidable power, carrying a tyrannical air exclusive to the supreme of this world.

"Kill!"

The sky stirred violently, like thunderclaps roaring. The Immortal Gold Armor, moving with his will, split and transformed into a great spear, clashing with the blood of the Qilin Immortal.

Just as the Qilin Immortal was about to make contact with the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord, he suffered an invisible attack and staggered, breaking his momentum.

The Mid-heaven Emperor Lord seemed to have anticipated this, sweeping his spear across and colliding with the Qilin Immortal’s antler. The spear, unable to withstand the antler, shattered upon contact but immediately reformed.

"Is this the method of The Controller?" Lu Yang frowned. The scene he had just witnessed was too reminiscent of The Controller—using spiritual essence for attacking the Qilin Immortal, the Immortal Gold Armor’s transformation at will, and its autonomous defense capabilities.

He recalled the three Divine Monarchs he encountered at the Laurel Fairy Palace, who mentioned that the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord could consume other Dao Fruits but needed the Laurel Tree Ancestor to enhance his spiritual power.

"Does that mean he’s consumed the Enlightenment Dao Fruit Prototype, or a part of its powers?"

The Qilin Immortal, naturally hiding within the body of Lord Si Shi, could come to the same conclusion.

However, the time was ripe, and it no longer mattered.

"Soul Chasing and Soul Capturing!"

Seeing that he couldn’t overcome the Qilin Immortal by force, the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord unleashed a mental attack, striking directly at the primordial spirit and soul, undeniably unblockable.

But the Qilin Immortal merely shook his head and continued to look vibrant.

"This is impossible!"

The Mid-heaven Emperor Lord had tried this move on The Controller before. Only with precaution could The Controller barely withstand it; the Qilin Immortal couldn’t be unharmed.

The Qilin Immortal had adapted to the Creation Immortal Technique, and his injuries began to heal on their own. His blood roared vigorously in his veins, resonating with heaven and earth, a sight truly horrifying.

He galloped on all fours, each movement seeming to manipulate the world. The spiritual attacks commanded by the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord could not halt the Qilin Immortal’s steps or momentum. The wisps of aura he emitted crushed the earth and shattered mountains, revealing ravines.

The Immortal Gold Armor sensed danger, transformed into a shield, and positioned itself entirely in front of the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord.

But it was all futile; the Immortal Gold Shield only slightly slowed the Qilin Immortal’s pace before shattering upon impact. The antler struck horizontally, blasting two large holes in the chest of the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord.

The Mid-heaven Emperor Lord’s eyes widened in disbelief. When he first battled with the Qilin Immortal, the latter was never this powerful.

Had he been hiding his true strength previously?

No, he was getting stronger in battle!

"Fool, fighting a protracted battle with the Qilin Immortal, the longer you fight, the stronger he gets!" Fairy Eternity laughed heartily in the Spiritual Space, never having expected that the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord would engage the Qilin Immortal for so long.

"Little Yang, did you figure out the reason?"

"The Mid-heaven Emperor Lord was deceived by the name of the Dao Fruit," Lu Yang roughly guessed the reason.

"The Survival Of The Fittest Dao Fruit is not only the Upper Dao Fruit for the ’Survival Of The Fittest Dao Fruit Prototype’ but also for the ’Survival of the Fittest Dao Fruit Prototype’ and ’Natural Selection Dao Fruit Prototype.’"

"In other words, he has the abilities of these three Dao Fruit Prototypes and they have been enhanced."

The reason Ao Ling was known as the top among Semi-Immortals, is because she possessed the Natural Selection Dao Fruit Prototype, growing stronger the more she was attacked.

"That’s also why the Qilin Immortal predecessor’s early stage was about defense, rarely attacking; he grew stronger in this manner."

Originally, battles among Immortals emphasized the use of Dao Fruits. However, if the gap between the two parties was too great, and considering most of the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord’s moves were ineffective against the current Qilin Immortal, the outcome of this battle was predictable.

The Qilin Immortal reverted to human form and grabbed the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord by the neck: "I’ll ask you once again, who told you about the Reincarnation Dao Fruit Prototype!"

Recognizing the gap between himself and the Qilin Immortal, the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord, devoid of the will to resist, truthfully replied, "It was the one who had fought with you, the Four Ancient Immortals. He told me that refining the Reincarnation Dao Fruit Prototype could allow one to live another lifetime even if the Immortal perished."

Seeing this, Lord Si Heng panicked; the Emperor was captured, and three Semi-Immortals stood beside him, one renowned for his speed, making escape impossible.

What did I say, this is a trap!

"So, do I get any special treatment if I surrender now?"

Lu Yang thought seriously and asked, "Are you with the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord or with The Controller?"

"The Emperor Lord."

"Then I’ll arrange for you two to be in adjacent cells."

Lord Si Heng began to ponder whether he should say thank you or declare ’this is not the special treatment I wanted’.

Watching the Qilin Immortal fly this way, everyone, regardless of their affiliation, was somewhat anxious.

"Teacher!" Lord Si Shi, the most carefree among them, was the most relaxed.

Thief King, facing a legend like the Qilin Immortal, the foremost among the Supreme Demons, and a revered figure by the Demon Race, was instinctively nervous.

Lu Yang had multiple reasons to be nervous.

"So you are Lu Yang?"

"Lu Yang has met the Qilin Immortal predecessor," Lu Yang performed a standard ancient ceremony.

"Lu Yang, you stay. The rest of you three, stay away from here," the Qilin Immortal told the two god monarchs and the Thief King.

The three Semi-Immortals obediently left, and Lord Si Heng even voluntarily tied up his hands and feet to express his surrender.

He had wanted to ask the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord to move further away as well, but then he thought, the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord might just run away.

Never mind, it wasn’t a particularly secretive matter; letting the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord listen would do no harm.

"Lu Yang, upon our first meeting—if I’m not mistaken—it was you who resurrected the Immortal Fairy."

"Yes."

The Qilin Immortal sighed deeply, with a mix of emotion and a smile, "Indeed, you are the destined one for the Immortal Fairy."

Chapter 1243: 1242: I Killed Suiyue

(Daoist friends who haven’t seen the sick leave note, I recommend you check out yesterday’s note.)

"Kirin, long time no see!"

Fairy Eternity floated out from Lu Yang’s body and greeted him with a beaming smile.

Old friends had been apart for four hundred thousand years, and now that they were finally revived, Kirin Immortal was also very happy.

However, when he clearly saw Fairy Eternity’s state, his expression became puzzled, "Eternity, why is only your soul left?"

This wasn’t the situation he had anticipated.

He had thought that like himself, Eternity was hiding in some small world, carried around by Lu Yang.

Fairy Eternity happened to cough twice, her face looking exceptionally pale. She feigned that nothing was wrong, not wanting to worry her old friend, "It’s nothing serious, just got a bit injured in a previous battle, and it’s somewhat hard to recover."

"Are you okay, Fairy?" Lu Yang quickly showed his concern for Fairy Eternity.

Fairy Eternity waved her hand, "It’s nothing, just feeling a bit weak. I’ll just need to rest more later."

"Staying here with you all the time isn’t quite right; I should recover my strength as soon as possible."

"No, no, no, Fairy, you can stay in my Spiritual Space as long as you want."

The sight startled Kirin Immortal, was this something Eternity would say?

Mid-heaven Emperor Lord looked at Fairy Eternity with uncertainty, his thoughts spinning. Kirin Immortal addressed her as Eternity?

He carefully recalled all matters regarding immortals since ancient times; there had never been any female cultivator who became an immortal and was named "Eternity."

Who is she? An immortal from the Ancient Times?

And that Yun Zhi, also a Female Immortal, but he had never heard of Yun Zhi before.

Mid-heaven Emperor Lord suspected that during the hundred thousand years he was sealed in the Great Yu Dynasty, Yun Zhi had become an immortal, which is why he knew nothing about Yun Zhi.

That so-called title of Eldest Senior Sister of the Seek Dao Sect must undoubtedly have been a fabricated identity to conceal her real identity.

"Kirin, why did you only appear now? Little Ling Lianyi and the others have been looking for you for a long time without success." Fairy Eternity spoke up for Little Ling Lianyi and Little Tiger, the three of them had been searching all over the world for Kirin Immortal, reducing the time they could spend playing together as Kirin Immortal kept himself hidden.

Lu Yang was also puzzled; if Kirin Immortal was worried about being chased after by Ashy Bean, then Immortal Ying Tian’s appearance during his Void Refinement Stage Pass Tribulation was itself a signal, informing those in the know that Ashy Bean was no longer a threat.

It was straightforward to explain Immortal Jiuchong since the Extreme Northern Lands were extremely isolated in terms of news, but Kirin Immortal didn’t have this problem.

Kirin Immortal was hidden within the Great Qian Force, which was vigorously searching for the clues of the Four Ancient Immortals, aware that Immortal Ying Tian had appeared.

"Indeed, I know about Lianyi and Little Ling waking up, I know they were looking for me, I know that Immortal Ying Tian’s appearance means the downfall of Silent Extermination Immortal, and I could even guess about your revival."

Lu Yang couldn’t help but ask, "Then why exactly ..."

Kirin Immortal had a conflicted expression, his face changing again and again, until he finally closed his eyes in agony and let out a long, heavenly sigh, "If it weren’t for this matter involving the Reincarnation Dao Fruit Prototype, I probably would never appear before you all in my lifetime. I... I’m a bastard, I don’t have the face to see you all."

"Ah? Why?"

"You must have seen Sui Yue’s Corpse at the Five Elements Sect and learned about Sui Yue being assassinated at the end of the Fire Dynasty."

"It was I who killed Sui Yue."

"It was you?!"

Lu Yang and Fairy Eternity were greatly shocked. According to the Time Immortal, the four of them were connected by an Array Method, and the mastermind behind the scenes couldn’t directly harm him; it must have been that the mastermind possessed one of them to assassinate him.

Was Kirin Immortal possessed by the mastermind?

That reaction didn’t seem right.

Qilin Immortal lowered his head guiltily, not daring to face Fairy Eternity or see her reaction, "At that time, the mastermind behind the scenes threatened me with the lives of Little Ling and Lianyi, saying that if I didn’t kill Suiyue, he would kill Little Ling and Lianyi."

Qilin Immortal showed a pained expression, but with his head lowered, no one could see his face.

"I had no other choice but to kill Suiyue."

Although Ao Ling and Jiang Lianyi were in a state of Playing Dead Technique at the time, the mastermind could even wipe out immortals; his methods were unimaginable. If he were to take action, his two wives would certainly not survive.

After killing Time Immortal, Qilin Immortal regretted it and knelt on the ground, breaking down in tears; what had he done, whom had he lived up to, and whom could he face?

Mid-heaven Emperor Lord, standing nearby, was completely confused. Although it wasn’t the appropriate atmosphere to ask questions, he couldn’t hold back.

"Fire Dynasty, what’s that? I’ve never heard of this power before."

Does this mean the Great Qian Dynasty I worked so hard to establish wasn’t the first dynasty?

I have been proud of this for three hundred thousand years.

Was my pride misplaced?

"Time Immortal has perished? Then who has been engaging in Time Retrospection?"

Qilin Immortal sighed again, "Although I feel somewhat sorry for Suiyue, as the most prominent among the Four Ancient Immortals, I am confident in my strength. Once I make a move, Suiyue is assuredly not going to survive."

"The frequent time retrospections must be someone acquiring the Time Dao Fruit and researching it continually through time retrospection."

Lu Yang, "..."

It seems I’ve discovered that you, the Four Ancient Immortals, all claim to be the foremost among yourselves.

"But Suiyue is still alive, right? The time retrospection is his doing." Fairy Eternity said.

"What! He’s still alive?"

Qilin Immortal suddenly lifted his head, even losing control of his human form, his head directly transforming back into that of a Qilin, giving Lu Yang quite a fright.

At that moment, Lu Yang noticed that Qilin Immortal’s eyes were slightly moist.

"Yes, he abandoned his body at that time, his soul escaped to the west to establish Buddha Country, using the Power of Faith to create an immortal body for himself."

"Suiyue is still alive, Suiyue is still alive! He’s still alive!"

Qilin Immortal excitedly grabbed Lu Yang’s shoulders, "Is Suiyue really still alive?"

The grip was so painful that Lu Yang felt his bones might shatter; he could only nod in response to Qilin Immortal.

Excited beyond measure, Qilin Immortal had lived for three hundred thousand years in guilt and self-recrimination, moving through life dazed, like a zombie, and had even contemplated ending his own life several times.

"I knew it, I knew he was still alive!"

In his excitement, Qilin Immortal disregarded his form, reverting to his original shape and bouncing around in the sky, knocking the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord around with his Qilin horns.

"That old block of wood really isn’t that easy to kill!"

After celebrating for a while, Qilin Immortal realized his own impropriety and quickly reverted to his original form pretending nothing had happened.

"Ahem, I got a bit carried away there."

Unable to stop grinning and overwhelmed with excitement, Qilin Immortal couldn’t help but ask again, "He’s really still alive, right?"

Lu Yang nodded.

"Where was I just now?"

Lu Yang hesitated. They had just reached the part about killing Time Immortal, but seeing Qilin Immortal’s reaction, it seemed best not to continue on that note.

Changing the subject, Lu Yang asked, "When you first saw me, you called me the destined one of the Immortal; you didn’t seem surprised by my appearance?"

Chapter 1244: 1243: The Designated Person

"It all started after the fall of the Fire Dynasty."

"After the Fire Dynasty was destroyed, I thought about ending my own life, but I had not witnessed the resurrection of the Immortal, and the bold claims of the past remained unfulfilled. Xiao Ling and Lianyi would never have a chance to meet the Immortal, and I truly couldn’t bear to die with things left like that."

The Qilin Immortal sighed, actually reluctant to recall this experience, as it represented the most hesitant and melancholic moment of his life.

"To evade the pursuit of the Silent Extermination Immortal, I hid inside a rosary. The rosary changed hands several times but never encountered someone destined to find me, until it was picked up by Si Shi’s mother, who was also not destined to discover my presence."

"After her death, she passed the rosary on as a relic to Si Shi, who inadvertently entered the small world within the rosary. That’s when I decided to mentor Si Shi."

"Si Shi, this young man, possessed notable talent in cultivation. He grasped many concepts with ease. Under my guidance, his Cultivation Level steadily improved, far surpassing his peers, and he successfully went through layers of selection in the Great Qian Dynasty to become a candidate for Divine Monarch, and subsequently, he became the Divine Monarch."

Every time Lu Yang heard about the Four Ancient Immortals earnestly teaching their disciples, he would compare them to Immortal Fairy and then sigh quietly.

What could he do but accept his fate, since the one he met was a Fairy?

"Later on, Emperor and the Controller, to reduce the impact of the Power of Faith on themselves, led a group of Semi-Immortals and those in the Transcendance Tribulation Stage to hide in the Sun’s Fairy Palace. They applied a seal, and during the early period of the Great Yu, Emperor Wu Yao of Yu added a second seal."

"When the Emperor and the Controller undid the seal left by Wu Yao, it was already the late period of Great Yu, and chaos was beginning to emerge."

"After the seals were undone, I sensed that Wu Yao had tampered with the World Seal. Considering Wu Yao’s Dao Fruit was a Swap Dao Fruit, it wasn’t hard to guess what he had done. I saw this as an opportunity."

The World Seal was in fact a seal applied by the Four Ancient Immortals using their Dao Fruits’ powers at the base of the six continents.

"Originally, I planned to use Si Shi to find clues about the Four Ancient Immortals and leave the Sun, but at that time Si Shi was still asleep, so I had no choice but to leave the rosary and meet with Wu Yao."

"As I had expected, I needed Wu Yao’s help, and he needed mine."

"Wu Yao let the Great Yu Dynasty fall into ruin precisely to sacrifice it in order to swap it out of the Box World."

"But he failed. The cost he paid was not enough to swap him out."

"Also, on the World Seal was a capacity left by the Survival of the Fittest Dao Fruit. The same trick could no longer affect the seal."

"This made it impossible for Wu Yao to swap himself out again, unless with my permission."

"For this, he hoped that I would temporarily undo the seal of the Survival of the Fittest Dao Fruit to allow him to continue the swap."

"I agreed, but in return, I asked him to swap in the fated person of the Immortal Fairy. After the previous failed attempts to resurrect the Immortal, to effectively call out the name of the Immortal, no tricks could be used, it had to rely purely on luck."

"According to my thoughts, this person would need enough luck to revive the Immortal Fairy."

"Later, when I heard that Little Ling Lianyi had appeared, I guessed that the Immortal was already resurrected. The battle in Demon City that tore heaven and earth asunder further confirmed my guess, as only the Immortal Fairy could fight the Silent Extermination Immortal to such extent."

The Qilin Immortal looked into Lu Yang’s eyes and said word by word, "Little Friend Lu, you resurrected the Immortal. You are her destined person."

Lu Yang felt as though he was struck by lightning.

Although he had his suspicions, logically, he shouldn’t have been surprised, but when faced with the reality, Lu Yang still felt a sense of unreality.

Was I really swapped in by Wu Yao?

Right away, Lu Yang’s expression turned weird, considering my situation before my death, it seems Wu Yao’s situation after swapping isn’t looking too good.

All of Wu Yao’s equipment and Dao Fruit were in Eldest Senior Sister’s possession, and his own body was blown to bits. Was Wu Yao’s Immortal Body swapped out, or was it smashed by Eldest Senior Sister?

Forget it, don’t think too much. Wu Yao is deeply blessed, he’ll be fine for sure.

Mid-heaven Emperor Lord couldn’t believe his eyes as he looked at Lu Yang, having always wanted to leave the Box World to see the outside, only to discover that Lu Yang was indeed from beyond the realm. He quickly asked, "What exactly is the outside world like?"

"Ah, Little Yang, you truly are my fated one!" Immortal Fairy laughed heartily as she hugged Lu Yang, patting his head.

"Fairy, Fairy, please don’t do that," Lu Yang stammered, momentarily flustered with no idea how to react, and hurriedly pushed Immortal Fairy away.

Having been with Fairy for so long, Lu Yang still wasn’t accustomed to such intimate contact.

"Little Yang, are you blushing?" Immortal Fairy saw Lu Yang blush for the first time, quite intrigued.

"Not at all," Lu Yang said, his expression panicked as he quickly changed the subject.

"Fairy, aren’t you surprised that I come from the outside world?"

Immortal Fairy cheered, "What’s so surprising about that? I’m from the outside world too."

Lu Yang thought about it and realized she was right—before her demise, there wasn’t a Box World, so she indeed counted as someone from beyond.

Poking Lu Yang’s arm curiously, Immortal Fairy asked, "Quick, tell me what you’ve experienced in the outside world. Have you ever self-detonated?"

"Ah?"

Lu Yang was even more surprised, "Fairy, you can tell that?"

Immortal Fairy proudly placed her hands on her hips, lifting her chin with pride, "Of course, do you think I’ve self-detonated countless times? I can tell with just one look."

"I’ve always said so, you’re naturally suited for our Immortal Lineage!"

Lu Yang opened his mouth instinctively to argue, but the words to do so just wouldn’t come out; it indeed was fate.

Suddenly, Lu Yang remembered another issue, "Right, Qilin Immortal senior, since you’ve left the bracelet, doesn’t it mean that Ashy Bean knows your location now?"

Ashy Bean—who was that? Mid-heaven Emperor Lord really wanted to ask this question.

Qilin Immortal paused for a moment, then asked on behalf of Mid-heaven Emperor Lord, "Who is Ashy Bean?"

"That’s Silent Extermination Immortal."

Qilin Immortal made a sound of realization, but Mid-heaven Emperor Lord still didn’t understand and voiced out, "Who is Silent Extermination Immortal?"

Nobody answered his question, and Qilin Immortal said, "Of course she’ll know my location now."

"You’re not afraid of her?"

Qilin Immortal chuckled gently, recalling the Time Immortal, no longer with the desolation of the past: "Are you joking? What do I have to fear from her?"

Lu Yang didn’t expect Qilin Immortal to be so powerful, no wonder he dared to claim himself as the foremost of the Four Ancient Immortals; he truly was remarkable, "Did you fight with her?"

"Not at all, I had Wu Yao intercept Silent Extermination Immortal. He had to; with the seal still intact after my death, he wouldn’t be able to leave this lifetime."

Recalling the scene at the time, Qilin Immortal couldn’t help but praise—Wu Yao really was a great help, very honest and incredibly sturdy, creating the opportunity for his escape.

Lu Yang: "..."

Wu Yao really drew the short straw making a deal with you.

Chapter 1245: 1244: Unexpected Change

"Later on, during the era of the ’Great World Struggle’ prophesied by the Zuo Shi Immortal, the subordinates of the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord successively awakened, and the emperor commanded them to continue searching for clues about the four of us."

"One by one, his subordinates disappeared: those sent to the Laurel Fairy Palace vanished, those sent to the Buddha Country vanished, Si Lei secretly slipped away and vanished, The Controller journeyed to the Extreme Northern Lands and vanished. This left the emperor living in constant fear and paranoia, to the extent that he even moved out of the Sun, abandoning such a magnificent Fairy Palace."

Upon hearing the Qilin Immortal say this, the face of the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord darkened. Could you really blame him for being afraid? It was like dealing with ghosts—anyone would be frightened by such occurrences.

"And just recently, he somehow learned that the Jiuyou Sect was about to condense the Reincarnation Dao Fruit Prototype, so he decided to send Si Heng and Si Shi to steal it and bring it to him."

"Very few know of the Reincarnation Dao Fruit Prototype, and among them, the most likely to tell the emperor had to be the backstage manipulator."

"The backstage manipulator wouldn’t be so kind as to inform him of this intelligence without an ulterior motive."

"I then resolved to confront the emperor after Si Shi obtained the Reincarnation Dao Fruit Prototype, to pry out the exact words the backstage manipulator had spoken to him, and then figure out a way to relay that intelligence to you."

"Who would have thought that Immortal Fairy and Little Friend Lu would also be here."

At this point, the Qilin Immortal gave a wry smile. He had determined to avoid human contact for a lifetime; he thought with Immortal Fairy here the emperor surely wouldn’t be able to take the Reincarnation Dao Fruit Prototype, and he wouldn’t need to reveal himself.

Yet, strangely enough, the emperor’s plan went exceptionally smoothly. Si Shi almost handed over the Reincarnation Dao Fruit Prototype to the emperor, and he had no choice but to intervene and battle the emperor.

The Qilin Immortal spat out the spoils of war—a sapling and the Reincarnation Dao Fruit Prototype.

"So this thing is the Reincarnation Dao Fruit Prototype, eh? Little Yang, bring it here and let me take a look." Immortal Fairy hadn’t had the chance to see such a novel Dao Fruit Prototype in person and couldn’t help being curious.

"Here." The Qilin Immortal handed the sapling to Lu Yang, though it was less than perfect, with the sapling covered in a sticky, gooey substance due to having been retrieved from inside his stomach.

"Little Yang, don’t disdain it; this is Qilin saliva, which during Ancient Times was even more precious than Dragon saliva and was incredibly hard to come by," whispered Immortal Fairy covertly.

Lu Yang: "..."

Then why don’t you hold onto it, Fairy?

Lu Yang and Immortal Fairy examined the Reincarnation Dao Fruit Prototype closely; the surface of the prototype had a slowly spinning vortex that could easily mesmerize and captivate those who looked upon it for too long.

The Qilin Immortal then seriously interrogated the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord: "How exactly did the backstage manipulator speak to you?"

The Mid-heaven Emperor Lord replied without hesitation, "He told me that refining the Reincarnation Dao Fruit Prototype would allow me to live another life. However, before that, I had to first hand the prototype over to him. He would return it to me after he used it. I agreed to his terms outwardly, but never really trusted him, determined to refine the prototype myself the moment I got it."

The Qilin Immortal frowned, the answer from the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord was somewhat beyond his expectations: "What does the backstage manipulator want with the Reincarnation Dao Fruit Prototype?"

The Mid-heaven Emperor Lord shook his head: "He didn’t tell me."

After saying this, he quickly covered his mouth, staring fixedly at the Qilin Immortal: "What have you done to me!"

Even as a captive, he wouldn’t normally reveal everything so readily!

The Qilin Immortal scoffed, "Forgotten, have you? My Dao Fruit is the Upper Dao Fruit of the ’Survival Of The Fittest Dao Fruit Prototype’!"

The Survival Of The Fittest Dao Fruit Prototype grants the victor dominion over the vanquished—Zhu Tian relied on this power to firmly secure the throne of Demon Emperor.

As both the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord and the Qilin Immortal were Immortals, naturally the former wouldn’t be entirely dominated. However, it was effortlessly simple for the Qilin Immortal to compel the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord to speak truthfully.

With the sapling in hand, Lu Yang also pondered the question on the sidelines: What does the backstage manipulator want with the Reincarnation Dao Fruit Prototype?

Does he too wish to live another life?

"Emperor Lord’s description doesn’t sound quite right, does it? If he wanted to live another life, would he really return the Dao Fruit Prototype to Emperor Lord?"

"But if he’s lying, this lie is too easy to poke through."

"Moreover, how could such a careful mastermind behind the scenes ensure that Emperor Lord wouldn’t covet the Dao Fruit Prototype for himself?"

At this thought, Lu Yang’s expression suddenly changed drastically, "Not good, the mastermind behind the scenes is hiding nearby!"

With the power of the mastermind behind the scenes, he wasn’t afraid of Qilin Immortal getting the Reincarnation Dao Fruit Prototype at all!

"Heh heh, you young ones do react quickly."

A black hand suddenly appeared beside Lu Yang, intent on snatching the Reincarnation Dao Fruit Prototype.

"How dare you!"

Qilin Immortal immediately reacted, facing the black hand with his own. The shockwave from their collision sent Lu Yang and Mid-heaven Emperor Lord flying away; the only difference between the two was that one was blown far while the other was blown close. Qilin Immortal didn’t fare much better; overpowered by the owner of the black hand, he was struck and sent flying.

A figure shrouded in black mist slowly emerged, having been there all along, unnoticed by anyone!

"Qilin, it seems that in three hundred thousand years, your abilities have improved a tiny bit. If it were before, you would’ve been sent flying much further than now with one blow from me."

The figure’s laughter accompanied the ever-shifting black mist.

Qilin Immortal burst out from the ruins, shaking off the debris, and stared intensely at the shadowy figure, roaring out the identity of the other.

"Mastermind behind the scenes, Sixth Immortal!"

Qilin Immortal’s palm throbbed with a faint pain as broken bones swiftly healed.

The Survival Of The Fittest Dao Fruit had another function; after defeating an enemy, one would become stronger. He was now in the strongest state of his life, even better than when fighting with Emperor Lord, but still no match for a single palm strike from the mastermind behind the scenes!

The mastermind behind the scenes laughed nonchalantly, "Immortal Fairy is seriously injured and not yet recovered. Do you really think you alone can protect the Reincarnation Dao Fruit Prototype?"

"Let’s give it a try and see!" Qilin Immortal transformed back into his true form with a shake of his body, the Kirin roaring as he trod the skies, eyes bloodshot with rage.
