The more he hated himself from three hundred thousand years ago, the more he wanted to slay the mastermind behind the scenes!

"Who in the world are you!" Immortal Fairy Eternity, changing from her usual lackadaisical demeanor, demanded of the shadowy figure, attempting to penetrate the black mist with her Immortal Sense but being repelled.

The mastermind behind the scenes did not answer, his eyes within the mist glaring coldly at Immortal Fairy Eternity, his voice as cold as eternal ice, filled with endless resentment, "Immortal, you are truly hard to kill."

"I am asking you who you are!" Immortal Fairy Eternity was furious, her vast Immortal Sense causing the entire space to tremble.

"I’ll tell you before you die!"

The mastermind behind the scenes instantly moved in front of Lu Yang to snatch the Reincarnation Dao Fruit Prototype, but Qilin Immortal had anticipated this move. Employing a Space Spell, he arrived beside Lu Yang as well.

"Get past me first!"

Qilin Immortal opened his giant maw and lunged to bite!

The mastermind behind the scenes’s expression turned cold as well, raising his hand to shatter Qilin Immortal’s teeth, "Ignorant of death, how dare you attempt to bother me!"

"What naturally born monster, nothing but a beast with no father or mother!"

Chapter 1246: 1245: The Strength of the Puppet Master

In the Fengdu Space, the Great Sun Crossing the Sky, Meng Jingzhou had a breakthrough in the midst of the battle, with his fist technique undergoing a transformation, truly reviving the ancient unstoppable might. He looked disdainfully in all directions, shattering his shadowy self with a single punch in the duel.

Many spectral entities huddled together, trembling at this sight. Meng Jingzhou’s Yang Qi was too vigorous, a natural enemy for them.

"At last, that’s settled."

Meng Jingzhou wiped the sweat from his forehead, lamenting the trouble of not having the Dao Fruit Outline. It took him half a day just to defeat an opponent.

"Senior Brother Meng, are you alright?" Man Gu came over concerned for Meng Jingzhou, handing him a towel.

Meng Jingzhou flashed a dashing smile, "What could possibly be wrong with me? I have no idea what’s happening outside with Old Lu; he hasn’t returned for so long. I’ll go out and take a look."

Meng Jingzhou opened the door to the Fengdu Space and stuck out half his body. Zhongshan County was a sight of devastation, with remnants scattered everywhere. Sharp killing intents swept across the sky, enveloping the land in their approach.

He saw two immortals fiercely fighting in the sky. One of them looked identical to the legendary Kirin, while the other was shrouded in black mist, with an indiscernible identity.

Marks of the fierce battle between the two were everywhere, on land and in the sky, with space fragmented, chaotic protective winds, and traces of claws and bites.

Meng Jingzhou: "..."

What had happened? The outside world was supposed to be a fight between Old Lu and Si Heng God Monarch, wasn’t it?

Just as Meng Jingzhou was about to withdraw back into the Fengdu Space to clear his head, he saw three figures rushing over from not far away, running desperately as if their lives depended on it. The aftereffects of the combat burst around them, blowing them away, yet they quickly regrouped and kept running.

"Wait, let us in too!" Thief King, dragging Si Heng God Monarch and Lord Si Shi, saw Meng Jingzhou and rushed over joyfully toward him.

Zhongshan County was shattered beyond recognition; they couldn’t tell where the original entrance to the Fengdu Space was. Luckily, Meng Jingzhou had opened the door.

For the first time, Thief King felt how useful the Speed Dao Fruit Prototype could be, truly life-saving indeed.

Thief King and the two God Monarchs rushed into the Fengdu Space headlong, knocking over Meng Jingzhou in their wake.

"There’s no way to repay your life-saving grace. I support you to be the Meng Family Patriarch," Thief King collapsed on the ground, gasping for air, having just saved his own life.

Meng Jingzhou grabbed Thief King by the collar: "Who cares about being a patriarch right now? What’s happening outside?"

Thief King shook his head, the reactions of the two God Monarchs were no different: "We don’t know."

Originally, they had followed Qilin Immortal’s orders to hide far away, daring neither to watch nor listen to whatever was happening on Qilin Immortal’s side.

Soon after, they heard the sounds of intense fighting coming from the sky. Qilin Immortal seemed to be incredibly angry, looking as though he could devour someone. Lord Si Shi had never seen his master with such an expression since he started his apprenticeship.

The three of them didn’t dare think too much and ran for their lives all over the world. They certainly didn’t dare to run towards Lu Yang; that would be even more dangerous.

...

At this time, Lu Yang was running around Zhongshan County with the Green Peak Sword on his back, having placed the seedlings and the Reincarnation Dao Fruit Prototype inside the Green Blade Sword Small World.

Had it not been for his possession of the Initial Form of Immortal Dao Fruit, he would have been severely wounded by the shockwaves by now.

Although Qilin Immortal was surprisingly angry, almost seeing red, the anger obviously did not enhance his combat power. He was being completely suppressed by the hidden hand behind the scenes.

The Mid-heaven Emperor Lord was nowhere to be found; it was unclear whether he took the chance to escape or hide somewhere.

The true target of the mastermind behind the scenes was the Reincarnation Dao Fruit Prototype, so wherever Lu Yang fled, the battlefield would shift there, making it so that Lu Yang dared not escape into the Fengdu Space nor leave the confines of Zhongshan County. If the battlefield were to move elsewhere, it would bring catastrophe upon that place.

Lu Yang wasn’t idle either, persistently using "predict the future." Impossible futures unfolded before him, through which he confirmed that the mastermind had not used the Dao Fruit of time manipulation.

Thinking this, Lu Yang turned his head to look at the Qilin Immortal who, despite being riddled with wounds, was still roaring and howling furiously. The speed of his injuries far exceeded his self-healing rate, allowing the mastermind to suppress the Qilin Immortal with pure strength alone, without the need for Dao Fruits.

"Qilin Immortal Senior, he is trying to enrage you, don’t fall for it!" Using Spiritual Sense to communicate, Lu Yang noticed that as soon as the mastermind appeared, the Qilin Immortal couldn’t suppress his fury. After being provoked, his rage intensified—the strength of his Qi and Blood boiled over, flooding Zhongshan County, fighting recklessly as if he was courting death.

This also fit Lu Yang’s impression of the mastermind: although powerful, he preferred to use strategies. Except for the time when the Fairy Eternity met her demise and the mastermind had no time to escape and was forced into a battle with the Four Ancient Immortals, the other times the mastermind made his move, it was never done in person.

Strange, why would he take matters into his own hands this time?

Upon hearing this, the Qilin Immortal calmed down and regulated his breathing. That’s right, he was already at a disadvantage against his opponent. If he allowed himself to be led around by the nose, it would only get worse.

"Shattering Mountains and Rivers!"

The Qilin forced back the pain and stomped down powerfully, collapsing the mountains and rivers. Though calmer now, every strike he delivered was still filled with lethal intent, showing no mercy.

Even if it meant dying and his Dao dissipating today, he would take a chunk of flesh from his opponent!

"Heh," the mastermind let out an almost imperceptible laugh. With his Cultivation Level, he naturally could hear Lu Yang’s spiritual communication. But what could a mere Qilin do even if it calmed down? It would still be the loser.

Boom—

The mastermind raised his hand to block the Qilin Immortal’s hoof, yanked it downwards fiercely, and the Qilin Immortal lost control and crashed onto the earth, creating a deep pit of unknown depth.

The adversary didn’t even use any magic skills or Divine Abilities, relying only on bare-handed combat to suppress the Qilin Immortal, not giving any chance for the Survival Dao Fruit to come into play.

The Qilin Immortal opened his Storage Ring; various rare and precious immortal medicines were stuffed into his mouth like money was no object, and his injuries quickly recovered.

His horn glowed, with runes flickering. This was his innate Divine Ability: even at the Golden Core Stage, he could use his horn to shatter mountains. When contending with the Yingtian Immortal, even the Yingtian Immortal had to avoid its sharp edge.

He broke through the earth and charged at the mastermind behind the scenes. In a flash of lightning, the mastermind grasped the horn, reversed his grip, and slammed it to the ground, causing the Qilin Immortal to vomit blood heavily, with a muted sound from his body indicating numerous broken bones.

Suddenly, the wind and clouds underwent a drastic change, and the Exorcism Divine Thunder came crashing down fiercely, slamming hard into the mastermind.

An Immortal controlling the Heavenly Tribulation descended upon the world, eyes like torches, radiating a divine and majestic aura.

The Yingtian Immortal incarnation surveyed the area: the Qilin Immortal covered in wounds, Lu Yang desperately running, and that memorable silhouette shrouded in black mist. In an instant, he understood what had happened.

Couldn’t you find another way to call me here? Must you curse me!

This thought passed fleetingly, and then Yingtian Immortal’s attention immediately turned to the figure within the black mist. As always, it was difficult to deal with; even his Exorcism Divine Thunder couldn’t tear through the opponent’s black fog.

Damn, just with an incarnation, he couldn’t handle the mastermind behind the scenes.

"Ying Tian!" Upon seeing his old friend, the Qilin Immortal, lying in the pit, shouted excitedly. Among the Four Immortals, he had the best relationship with Yingtian Immortal.

"Quit the nonsense, let’s catch up later. Can you get up?"

"Yes!" The Qilin Immortal revitalized his spirit and reignited his momentum, flying up to the sky, ready to battle alongside Yingtian Immortal.

Chapter 1247: 1246: I Can Spare You

Lu Yang cursed at the Yingtian Immortal and quickly fled from beneath the Sixth Immortal, while in his Spiritual Space, the anxious urging voice of Fairy Eternity resounded.

"Is Miss Yun done?"

"Please be patient a little longer, esteemed Fairy. If we act rashly, the enemy might flee upon realizing the adverse situation. I will be ready soon."

...

Divine Thunder was clear and bright, and Zhongshan County had become an ocean of thunderbolts, with scorched earth, rolling thunder resounding through ten thousand miles of sky, and storms wreaking havoc, sending sand and stones flying. If one extended their Immortal Sense, it would be scattered in an instant, disrupting the Sixth Immortal’s perception.

Nectar descended, healing the injuries of the Qilin Immortal.

Yingtian Immortal stood amidst the ocean of thunderbolts, with three thousand Immortal Thunders above his head, and feet upon earth, fire, wind, and water; he was the undisputed master of the Heavenly Tribulation.

"Upper Pure Immortal Thunder!"

If the deployment of the Exorcism Divine Thunder when descending was an opportunistic move, then this Upper Pure Immortal Thunder was a buildup of power, incomparable in might to anything before!

The vast Immortal Thunder surged like a torrential flood, rushing toward the Sixth Immortal.

The black mist surrounding the Sixth Immortal seemed capable of blocking everything in the world, flowing upstream, targeting not the Yingtian Immortal but the Qilin Immortal.

His actions were urgent, fast, and precise; neither the Yingtian Immortal nor the Qilin Immortal could react in time.

With a thunderous palm, the Sixth Immortal pierced through the Qilin Immortal’s chest. From his chest, thunder burst forth, exploding with a bang, and the entire Immortal Body transformed into a thunderbolt.

The thunder scattered and reconvened into the likeness of the Qilin Immortal in the distance.

Having transformed into a body of lightning, the Qilin Immortal could freely traverse the ocean of thunderbolts.

Adaptation to the environment, survival of the fittest.

"A body of lightning?" The reason the Sixth Immortal did not confront the Yingtian Immortal first was that the Yingtian Immortal had descended in a body of lightning, which was difficult to deal with. He had not expected the Qilin Immortal to react so quickly and also adapt to the environment by transforming into a body of lightning.

But it was only a slight inconvenience.

"Taiqing Immortal Thunder!"

"Yuqing Immortal Thunder!"

Yingtian Immortal frantically spurred on the Tribulation Dao Fruit, forcing himself to create the Three Pure Immortal Thunders in the shortest time possible.

The Three Pure Immortal Thunders, used for slaying immortals and vanquishing demons, suppressed the world with such mighty power that once released, even the Yingtian Immortal himself could hardly control them. In Ancient Times, only Immortal Fairy had the skill to withstand this attack.

The Upper Pure Immortal Thunder transformed into a flood of thunderbolts, the Taiqing Immortal Thunder into an arsenal of ax, halberd, sword, and spear amidst thousands of Immortal Soldiers, and the Yuqing Immortal Thunder into a six-sided shield, forming a cube that sealed the Sixth Immortal, along with the Upper Pure and Taiqing Immortal Thunders, within.

The cube was dazzlingly bright, emitting full immortal light. Lu Yang merely looked up and blood streamed from his eyes, blinding him instantly. His eyes were ruined. Knowing that he was now equivalent to a Semi-Immortal, yet unable to even glance at it, one could hardly imagine the power of the Three Pure Immortal Thunders directly facing the Sixth Immortal.

Lu Yang healed his eyes using the Initial Form of Immortal Dao Fruit and distanced himself from the battlefield as much as possible.

He knew relying solely on the Three Pure Immortal Thunders would not be enough to defeat the Sixth Immortal. If this move had been effective, the Four Immortals and the hidden hand behind the scenes would have settled their score in Ancient Times.

Sure enough, with a loud bang, the Sixth Immortal performed some technique or perhaps simply relied on sheer power to blast open a gap in the cube formed by the Yuqing Immortal Thunder. The black mist twisted irregularly, a situation not seen before, clearly indicating that the Three Pure Immortal Thunders had affected him.

In the instant the Sixth Immortal blasted the gap, the Qilin Immortal accelerated with the power of lightning. Even the Speed Dao Fruit could hardly match such velocity. His entire body transformed into a being of lightning, except for the kirin horn, which remained as it was.

The kirin horn struck the black mist of the Sixth Immortal, sinking in like a stone into the sea, and making no progress whatsoever.

Yingtian Immortal wielded an ax and hacked at the back of the Sixth Immortal’s head!

This was an Immortal Artifact hastily crafted from the materials in the Qilin Immortal’s Storage Ring, its creation rushed by necessity, allowing for only one use.

One use was enough!

The Sixth Immortal caught the ax with his hand and crushed it with force!

"It did seem a bit more coordinated than in the Ancient Times."

Before the battle with the mastermind behind the scenes in the Ancient Times, the Four Immortals mainly sparred with each other and did not have the experience of fighting together. After establishing the Fire Dynasty, they worked hard to study their own characteristics and think about how to cooperate in battle to unleash the maximum combat power.

The Sixth Immortal exhaled several kinds of Immortal Fire, which exploded upon touching the thunder bodies of the other two Immortals, shattering their bodies to pieces.

The combination of thunder and fire is the most explosive in the world; naturally, it leads to a blast when they come into contact.

As the two attempted to reconstitute their thunder bodies, the Immortal Fire continued to hunt them down, exploding once more.

Though this level of explosion could not harm their essence, it did keep them occupied with reconstituting their thunder bodies, preventing them from accomplishing anything.

"If you’re willing to hand over the Reincarnation Dao Fruit Prototype and swear no longer to be my enemy, I can spare your lives," the Sixth Immortal said slowly, with a tone suggesting negotiation. "After all, I am not a bloodthirsty person. As long as you don’t stand in my way, what does your fate matter to me?"

The two burned with rage—The Sixth Immortal clearly did not regard them at all.

Even though they were obscured by the black fog, it was still apparent that the Sixth Immortal was shaking his head, seemingly unable to understand their actions: "Moreover, why should you sacrifice your lives for someone else?"

If the Four Immortals did not understand his strength and fought him during the Ancient Times, that would be somewhat understandable. However, having acknowledged the gap in strength after the ancient battle, they still dared to challenge him recklessly.

The Qilin Immortal roared in anger and abandoned the thunder body, reverting to his original form. These flames of Immortal Fire were of average power and only posed a threat to the thunder bodies; his Immortal Body could easily resist the flames.

The Yingtian Immortal used several pieces of Immortal Gold from the Qilin Immortal’s Storage Ring to temporarily craft some armor plating to shield against the Immortal Fire.

Neither of them answered the Sixth Immortal’s question. Their philosophies were entirely different from his. Even if they replied, what would it do? Could they possibly make the Sixth Immortal agree with their viewpoint?

"It is you who are seeking death."

The Sixth Immortal said in an indifferent tone, deciding their fate. He moved instantaneously in front of the Qilin Immortal, whose pupils contracted dramatically—The Sixth Immortal was even faster than before!

The Sixth Immortal grasped the Qilin Immortal’s neck and struck his face, causing the Qilin Immortal’s head to explode like a watermelon!

As the Qilin Immortal’s head slowly regrew, the Yingtian Immortal stimulated the Tribulation Dao Fruit to summon Nectar from heaven, which the Sixth Immortal used to smash the auspicious clouds formed from the Nectar.

Seeing the Sixth Immortal rushing towards him, the Yingtian Immortal hastily used the Thirty-three Layers Of Heavenly Tribulation to intercept, while the Sixth Immortal used the Qilin Immortal as a shield to block all Heavenly Tribulation, with his other hand seizing the neck of the Yingtian Immortal.

"You think I can’t touch you with your lightning body?"

Though within the lightning body, the Yingtian Immortal was choked with the suffocating sensation of a drowning mortal.

What kind of tactic was this?!

Suddenly, ice and snow spread for millions of miles, instantly covering the entirety of Zhongshan County in an instant. The extreme cold seemed as though it could freeze time itself, causing ice to form on Lu Yang’s body, requiring all his strength to break free!

Upon seeing this, the Yingtian Immortal immediately activated the Tribulation Dao Fruit, summoning Heavenly Tribulation once more. Rolling thunder, intermingled with endless Immortal Fire, combined harmoniously this time instead of exploding. The Heavenly Thunder induced flames from the earth like a primal cataclysm, with unparalleled might, even more terrifying than the Three Pure Ones Heavenly Tribulation. The black fog began to dissipate strand by strand, forcing the Sixth Immortal to temporarily release the Qilin Immortal and the Yingtian Immortal.

Accompanied by Immortal Fire and Profound Ice, the Immortal Jiuchong arrived, glaring at the Sixth Immortal with gritted teeth.

He had been discussing Dao with Mister Tiankui at the Five Elements Sect when he sensed a disturbance from the west, indicative of an Immortal Battle, so intense that it could be felt from such a distance.

Following the disturbance, he hurried towards the Wild State. Halfway through, the vibrations disappeared, suggesting the Immortal Battle had concluded. Out of curiosity, he continued towards the Wild State.

Approaching the Wild State, he encountered a much more intense wave of fluctuations, the intensity of the ongoing battle far surpassing that of the initial Immortal Battle!

Arriving at Zhongshan County, he saw the Sixth Immortal simultaneously restraining the Qilin Immortal and the Yingtian Immortal. Without time for further thought, he intervened, unleashing the Extreme Cold Domain to slow the flow of time and summoning Immortal Fire to attack the Sixth Immortal.

But Immortal Fire alone was not a threat to the Sixth Immortal.

The Yingtian Immortal immediately understood the Immortal Jiuchong’s intention and combined forces with Heavenly Thunder, leading to the current situation.

"Sixth Immortal..."

The Immortal Jiuchong’s roar filled with rage had not yet been fully voiced when he saw the Sixth Immortal perform a hand gesture, and ninety-nine mountains rose from the earth, flying towards the Jiuchong, hammering him into the side of a mountain. Mountain after mountain collapsed until the last one halted the impact.

Chapter 1248: 1247: Since You’re Here, Don’t Even Think About Leaving

Immortal Jiuchong tried to escape from the grip of the Sixth Immortal, but he had already witnessed the latter’s power in Ancient Times and knew that he could not compete.

In just an instant, his body exploded countless times under the attack of the Sixth Immortal.

"Switch!"

Immortal Jiuchong switched personalities, allowing his more aggressive personality to take control.

This aggressive personality didn’t mess around. Since he couldn’t confront the Sixth Immortal head-on, he changed tactics, immediately using the Earth Escape technique to burrow into the ground, escaping to the surface to regroup with Yingtian Immortal and Qilin Immortal.

...

"How is it outside, is the battle over?" Meng Jingzhou was restless in the Fengdu Space. He had glimpsed a moment earlier when Qilin Immortal was being suppressed in combat, overwhelmed by an unexpectedly powerful opponent.

And he had no idea what was happening to Old Lu outside.

"Let’s check again."

Last time Meng Jingzhou had half his body out; this time, he only stuck out his head.

Then he saw four Immortals furiously striking at each other, blood scattering across the sky, land splitting and crashing as if it were the end of the world.

How had the fight become even more fierce?

He shivered, hurrying to pull his head back in.

Before retreating, he caught a glimpse from the corner of his eye of Old Lu hopping around lively, not seeming to be in trouble.

...

Qilin Immortal raised his hooves and suddenly dealt a hoof blow to both Yingtian Immortal and Immortal Jiuchong. The two Immortals staggered back several steps and concurrently cried out, "I surrender!"

Qilin Immortal defeated Yingtian Immortal and Immortal Jiuchong, using the principle of "survival of the fittest" to further enhance his cultivation level. His physical body became incomparably strong; he was indeed the foremost of the Four Ancient Immortals now!

Yingtian Immortal and Immortal Jiuchong together cast the Infinite Heavenly Tribulation. The chaos, Yin and Yang, and Five Elements all intertwined, each alone was powerful enough to make any cultivator pale with fear. Now appearing like cost-free, these thunder tribulations, after being intensified by Immortal Jiuchong, exhibited unbelievable power.

If a Semi-Immortal considered this heavenly calamity as an Immortal Ascension Tribulation, not one would survive!

Heavenly Tribulations always followed the rule of "forty-nine with a slim chance of survival", but this time there was no chance at all, an unprecedented terrifying calamity since ancient times!

The Infinite Heavenly Tribulation spread far and wide, fiercely enough to scare any cultivator into losing the ability to think.

The Sixth Immortal shattered the ground with a stomp, the earth fissured, and the intensified Immortal Mountain turned into a solid Protective Shield, but it could not withstand the Infinite Heavenly Tribulation for even three breaths and crumbled.

Combining two types of Dao Fruit into one was not an easy feat without long-term integration.

"You’ve indeed worked hard to defeat me."

The Sixth Immortal maintained a calm tone, not sounding like the focus of an assault, but rather like a judge sitting high above.

At that moment, Yingtian Immortal took control of the battlefield. He used the world as a furnace, spiritual essence as kindling, and Infinite Heavenly Tribulation as fierce flames, aiming to refine the Sixth Immortal!

"So you are treating me as an Immortal Artifact to be refined. Not a bad technique."

Around the Sixth Immortal, the fog grew darker and murkier, and the Infinite Heavenly Tribulation did indeed affect his carefully refined black fog.

The Heavenly Tribulation seemed endless, and so did the black fog, leaving the fierce battlefield in a weird balance.

Suddenly, the roar from the King of Demons broke the balance, Qilin Immortal using the Infinite Heavenly Tribulation to conceal himself, waiting for the moment the Sixth Immortal became distracted to launch a surprise attack!

However, the Sixth Immortal still managed to divide his focus to deal with Qilin Immortal. He threw a punch toward Qilin Immortal, who seemed to pass through him like a phantom, his attack missing.

"A phantom? No, it’s Right-Erect Shadowless."

The Great Divine Skill Right-Erect Shadowless could temporarily turn one intangible. During intangibility, the enemy couldn’t hit you, and you couldn’t hit the enemy either.

Qilin Immortal moved behind the Sixth Immortal, exposing an open gate!

Now!

With his enhanced strength, Qilin Immortal gathered all his might, smashing his hooves fiercely onto the back of the Sixth Immortal!

The black fog on the back immediately thinned out!

Not enough!

Qilin Immortal opened his mouth, spitting out several Immortal Artifacts that Immortal Jiuchong had brought.

No matter what the original purpose of these Immortal Artifacts was, at this moment they had only one use—self-detonation!

Several Immortal Artifacts detonated at once, still glaring amid the dazzling Infinite Heavenly Tribulation, like the sun among fireflies!

The explosion of the Immortal Artifacts was massive, with the Qilin Immortal nearest to the blast. His physical body was nearly obliterated, many of his bones melted, yet he laughed without feeling any pain.

"Ha ha, Sixth Immortal, you had it coming!"

The dark fog surrounding the Sixth Immortal finally burst open, and his back was injured as well!

This was the first time the Qilin Immortal had spoken since the appearance of Immortal Jiuchong.

The three Immortals fought side by side, silent throughout, not even nodding or exchanging glances, yet their coordination was impeccably tacit, a routine they had rehearsed millions of times in their minds.

The dark fog on the Sixth Immortal’s back gathered again; he slowly turned around, preparing to strike at the Qilin Immortal.

The Qilin Immortal immediately transformed into a body of lightning and exploded, turning into countless streams of electricity that reconvened far away, hiding again within the Infinite Heavenly Tribulation.

"So annoying."

Just when the three Immortals thought their plan was successful and were ready to press their advantage, the Sixth Immortal suddenly disappeared!

The Infinite Heavenly Tribulation raged on, affecting not only the Sixth Immortal’s Immortal Sense but also that of the three Yingtian Immortals. They had to either withdraw the Infinite Heavenly Tribulation or use their eyes to search!

"He’s behind you!"

From the Qilin Immortal’s perspective, he could just see the position of the Sixth Immortal, who had used some technique to vanish and then suddenly reappeared behind Yingtian Immortal and Immortal Jiuchong!

Yingtian Immortal and Immortal Jiuchong both attempted to use Right-Erect Shadowless to evade the Sixth Immortal, but the Sixth Immortal used Right-Erect Shadowless as well.

Using Right-Erect Shadowless simultaneously meant that it was as if no one had used it, so the Sixth Immortal could touch them!

The Sixth Immortal stretched out his large hand and grabbed their skulls, the bones creaking alarmingly and visibly deforming!

He exerted force with both arms, smashing their skulls together, the dull sound terribly macabre. Despite the excruciating pain, neither Yingtian Immortal nor Immortal Jiuchong cried out in pain!

Dissatisfied with their reactions, the Sixth Immortal dropped seeds that sprouted into thorns.

The Sixth Immortal rapidly ascended, clutching the two, intending to smash them viciously onto the thorns!

However, just as he reached two-thirds of the way up, suddenly there was a loud bang, and he could rise no further!

He punched the barrier, but it merely rippled.

Through the spreading ripples, the barrier finally became visible!

Judging by the curvature of the barrier, the entire Zhongshan County was enclosed!

Someone had placed a seal while he was fighting!

He had been completely unaware of it beforehand!

"Who is it!"

He dropped Yingtian Immortal and Immortal Jiuchong, as these two were no longer important at this moment.

"Since you’re here, don’t think about leaving."

The spatial fluctuations rippled, and Yun Zhi emerged, her face expressionless.

Without exuding any aura, just standing there, she became the focus of the battlefield, providing unparalleled hope.

In fact, as early as during the battle between the Qilin Immortal and the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord, Lu Yang, seeing that the situation was dire and uncertain of the outcome, and with Dou Dou Fairy unable to act, had immediately called for Eldest Senior Sister.

But after the Qilin Immortal defeated the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord, there had been no need for Yun Zhi to show herself; she stood by, quietly listening to the Qilin Immortal recount the course of events.

Then, when a villain from behind the scenes appeared, attempting to rob the Reincarnation Dao Fruit Prototype, Yun Zhi took action.

In the battle at the East Sea, Yun Zhi fought Ashy Bean, who, unable to defeat her, used various escape techniques to flee.

Yun Zhi knew that the Sixth Immortal was an even more formidable presence than Ashy Bean. If he was determined to flee, even having both her and the Fairy here might not be enough to stop him.

For this reason, she had utilized the Power of National Fate of Great Xia Country to construct a barrier system within Zhongshan County to ensure that the Sixth Immortal could not escape.

Yingtian Immortal nearly laughed out loud, knowing that since Lu Yang was here, Yun Zhi would definitely appear.

They had finally made it to this moment!

Chapter 1249: 1248 Let me see who you really are!

Above Zhongshan County, the atmosphere was grim, and time seemed to freeze at that moment. No one made a move.

Lu Yang breathed a sigh of relief, he had managed to set up the barrier before the Sixth Immortal noticed.

Unnoticed, Mid-heaven Emperor Lord appeared beside Lu Yang.

While setting up the barrier, Yun Zhi had also captured the fleeing Mid-heaven Emperor Lord and entrusted Lu Yang with the important task of watching over him.

Although Lu Yang felt that Eldest Senior Sister was overestimating him,

"Emperor Lord, I advise you not to think about running away," Lu Yang slowly said, noticing that the Emperor Lord was contemplating escaping amidst the chaos.

"You once tried to secretly devour the Reincarnation Dao Fruit Prototype. The Sixth Immortal was nearby and heard everything. Do you think the Sixth Immortal would let you go?"

The face of Mid-heaven Emperor Lord changed again and again, forced to admit that what Lu Yang said was true. The power of the Sixth Immortal was beyond his imagination.

If he were to break the barrier, not only would he be escaping, but so would the Sixth Immortal.

By the time the Sixth Immortal came looking for him, it would be a dead end for him!

The Sixth Immortal looked at the opponent, and for the first time, a hint of seriousness appeared in his calm tone. The black mist contracted to its limit, forming something resembling armor, to prevent a sudden attack from the opponent.

Then, the Sixth Immortal chuckled, "Yun Zhi, with your current state, how many more times can you act?"

"If I am not mistaken, over time, you would gradually merge with the Dao. The more you act, the faster this process will occur. What then? Do you actually wish to speed up the merging process?"

The Yingtian Three Immortals were shocked upon hearing this. What did the Sixth Immortal mean by ’merging with the Dao’?

Lu Yang, who had been ready to relax, grew serious again. The Yingtian Three Immortals might not know the details, but he understood. Could it be that Eldest Senior Sister was becoming increasingly close to the state of merging with the Dao, and unlike the first time, she might not be able to exit from this state again?!

However, the Sixth Immortal’s words did not elicit any emotion from Yun Zhi. She calmly spoke in an undeniable tone, stating two facts.

"You are wrong about two things."

"Firstly, I still have many opportunities to act."

"Secondly, you are not my opponent."

The tone of Yun Zhi changed dramatically, filled with intense anger, which was vastly different from her usual demeanor.

"Your opponent is me!"

Everyone present recognized the origin of this voice and realized what had happened.

"Has Immortal Fairy borrowed Daoist Yun Zhi’s body?!" Yingtian Immortal was astonished. Could that even happen?

Since the Sixth Immortal and Fairy Eternity were sworn enemies, naturally, Yun Zhi wouldn’t overstep her bounds and participate in this Immortal Battle.

Fairy Eternity moved her body slightly; Miss Yun’s body was indeed quite useful, allowing her to utilize her full strength.

Boom—

Fairy Eternity didn’t give the Sixth Immortal any chance to react. Her eyes cold as knives, she transformed into blazing lightning racing across the sky, fiercely punching toward the Sixth Immortal!

The Sixth Immortal, fearing no one, chose to confront the attack head-on!

As the fists collided, the black mist clinging to the fist could not withstand the force of Fairy Eternity’s punch and immediately dispersed!

"This was something only the Yingtian Three Immortals could accomplish together!"

The black mist rapidly recondensed, and the Sixth Immortal’s eyes were equally icy, "It seems your new body is indeed very useful."

Fairy Eternity had borrowed Yun Zhi’s body, making it difficult for him to bypass her and seize the Reincarnation Dao Fruit Prototype without facing severe consequences.

It was better to look for another opportunity.

As the Sixth Immortal clashed with Fairy Eternity, her Immortal Fist Technique was overwhelmingly tyrannical. The Sixth Immortal kept retreating, moving back toward the edge of the barrier.

He seized the moment to punch the barrier, but contrary to his expectations, it did not shatter. Instead, it rippled, the waves spreading throughout and the force of his punch absorbed.

If he had time, such a mere barrier could naturally not stop him, but how could Fairy Eternity give him the chance?

"Think not of escaping!" Fairy Eternity charged forth, her presence immaculate and divine, like a Flying Immortal from Outer Space.

The two collided repeatedly, shattering the void, the aftershocks resonating, Dao Sounds echoing through the skies, and primitive runes interwove into a sea, dispersing into particles of light.

Even the Yingtian Three Immortals had never seen Fairy Eternity this furious, she was unstoppable, every entity in her path doomed. They wanted to intervene but could not act, as in such an intense battle, the rapid movements meant their attacks might hit Fairy Eternity instead of the Sixth Immortal.

The fury that Fairy Eternity felt toward the Sixth Immortal was not solely due to him killing her, but also because her death had plunged the Four Immortals into profound sadness, causing Little Lotus, Little Ling Lianyi, and the three others to remain heartbroken despite their amnesia!

And the arrival of the pitiable yet detestable Ashy Bean, the downfall of the Fire Dynasty, the Four Immortals hiding for three hundred thousand years.

Everything stemmed from her, she felt she had failed these people!

Her Immortal Fist Technique was powerful and majestic, supremely dominant, making any strong being pale in comparison.

Although it was the combined efforts of the Five Ancient Immortals that delayed the path to the Dao Fruit, in truth, Fairy Eternity alone was sufficient. In other words, in the Ancient Era, Semi-Immortals were as common as rain, all originating from Fairy Eternity!

Fairy Eternity’s fist technique was versatile and unpredictable, seemingly without pattern, yet all within the variations of her Immortal Fist Technique.

The Qilin Immortal, upon seeing Fairy Eternity executing her technique, felt a faint pain throughout his body, reminiscing about ancient times.

The Immortal Fist Technique was derived from herbs, each punch representing the effect of a different herb. Theoretically, one could adapt to the Immortal Fist Technique if struck enough times, but the issue was the new effects when different herbs were combined. The combinations were infinite and thus impossible to adapt to!

Despite the black mist continually condensing, the black mist on the Sixth Immortal’s body trembled irregularly, meaning he was struggling to control it precisely!

Fairy Eternity held back her frustration, the Sixth Immortal didn’t use the Dao Fruit, so she wouldn’t either; she wanted to defeat him fair and square!

Realizing his face was targeted, the Sixth Immortal shifted from attack to defense.

Although he momentarily held her back, he found her punches getting faster and heavier.

As the battle began, it was not Fairy Eternity’s true strength, for the cultivation systems differed and it was her first time using Yun Zhi’s body, necessitating a period of adjustment. Right now, she was gradually adapting to Yun Zhi’s body through battle.

Fairy Eternity’s eyes blazed, her fists glowing, and with a final thunderous boom, she broke through the Sixth Immortal’s arms and his facade!

Even if the Sixth Immortal tried to gather other parts of the black mist to his face, it was futile, the built-up strike unstoppable.

"Hiding in shadows, afraid to show your face, let me see who you really are!"

The black mist enveloping the Sixth Immortal was completely shattered, revealing a face all too familiar to both Fairy Eternity and the Yingtian Three Immortals!

"Yao Saint!"

The only one from ancient times who mastered the Great Divine Skill of Verbal Spell, Yao Saint.

Chapter 1250: 1249: Yao Saint

"Yao Saint, it’s you!"

Everyone was astonished; the Sixth Immortal turned out to be someone they believed had long since died, yet everyone had heard his name.

Speak and Follow Yao Saint.

"Who is Yao Saint?" Mid-heaven Emperor Lord frowned, for he had not heard the name before.

Lu Yang glanced at Mid-heaven Emperor Lord beside him, wondering how he could be unfamiliar with everyone. However, since Seek Dao Sect had a tradition of treating captives favorably, he kindly introduced, "He’s the cultivator from Ancient Times who mastered the divine ability ’Speak and Follow.’"

"Is that him?"

Mid-heaven Emperor Lord suddenly realized, aware that in Ancient Times there existed a genius who mastered the almost lost ’Speak and Follow,’ undefeated in his class and never once defeated in battle. However, he fell shortly after advancing to the Transcendance Tribulation Stage, causing the divine ability ’Speak and Follow’ to become lost to the ages.

Mid-heaven Emperor Lord had sought out traces of the ’Speak and Follow’ numerous times without success.

In the era where geniuses were innumerable and Tribulation Crossing Half Immortals could be found everywhere, Yao Saint didn’t stand out much, and even his name did not survive through time.

The reason lay in the fact he had perished too soon; before the Transcendance Tribulation Stage, he indeed enjoyed unmatched glory, with his legend spreading throughout an entire star system.

But which genius from Ancient Times did not have similar legends? Which was not one to have defeated countless rivals, claimed to be unbeatable in their own class, ascending to the pinnacle of a star system atop their heads?

What truly determined their strength, however, was the Transcendance Tribulation Stage.

Regrettably, Yao Saint fell too soon and did not have the chance to confront the other geniuses in battle. He died at the hands of Fairy Eternity, without even a single notable victory to his name.

Even immortals such as Yingtian Immortal had only heard the name of Yao Saint and had not crossed paths with him in combat. They had not expected Yao Saint to be such a formidable adversary.

Lu Yang remembered that, according to Fairy Eternity, she had injured Yao Saint, who had always protected himself too well and rarely took a hit. He cried out "It hurts to death," and consequently, he died.

His death was somewhat abrupt; talking about it, it sounded like suicide.

"In Ancient Times, you broke my Absolute Defense, and now you’ve pierced through my black mist. Immortal, you really are tough to deal with."

Yao Saint’s tone carried a hint of resentment, as if by some twist of fate, such events were constantly being replayed.

As a user of ’Speak and Follow,’ he had countless attempts on his life along the way. How could he not use ’Speak and Follow’ to increase his defenses—but to his surprise, the Immortal Fairy proved so powerful that she shattered the Absolute Defense he had cast with ’Speak and Follow.’

In preparation to battle the Time Immortal who had yet to ascend to immortality, his Absolute Defense was strong enough to withstand attacks of time itself. Besides that, it could also withstand all sorts of attacks, including spatial curses. However, despite his thorough preparations, Immortal Fairy broke through with pure strength.

It was absolutely unbelievable. Were all geniuses from other star systems this powerful?

He had long utilized ’Speak and Follow’ to set a life-saving measure for himself that could help him feign death and escape, anticipating such a situation.

From this encounter, he realized that there were always stronger beings, and he chose to bide his time, feigning death as if he never existed.

"Immortal, you took advantage of my unpreparedness to launch a surprise attack against me, an uncharitable victory, and yet you dare question why I would want to kill you?"

"It was you who first obliterated an innocent tribe!" Immortal Fairy exclaimed angrily, recalling the time when Yao Saint sought to destroy a small tribe, and she had no choice but to intervene urgently.

Moreover, she could only resort to a surprise attack, she and Yao Saint both being at the Transcendance Tribulation Stage; if they were to do battle, that tribe would surely suffer the consequences.

The tribe she belonged to had perished because of the clash of two mighty beings, leaving only her alive. The battle had even inadvertently affected them, and she was determined to prevent such a tragedy from occurring again.

"Mortals fail to recognize celestial authority, offend me, how could they expect anything other than death?" Yao Saint said coldly.

Born high above others, mortals were nothing but playthings to amuse him, even cultivators in his eyes were merely larger playthings. If these playthings did not obey him, killing them was of no concern.

"How did Yao Saint secretly become an immortal, using Verbal Spell to block my probing?" Yingtian Immortal murmured to himself, still puzzled.

His Tribulation Dao Fruit controlled the Heavenly Tribulation, and the Immortal Ascension Tribulation could not possibly be concealed from his Tribulation Dao Fruit.

This meant Yao Saint must have ascended to immortality before him.

But that’s not right, Verbal Spell is a divine ability, not a Dao Fruit Outline, and a divine ability could not possibly deceive the outline of his Tribulation Dao Fruit.

Unless...

Yingtian Immortal quickly realized, "His Dao Fruit is Verbal Spell!"

Only by elevating Verbal Spell to the level of a Speak and Follow Dao Fruit Outline, could he deceive his own Tribulation Dao Fruit Outline and ascend to immortality in secret!

The Speak and Follow Dao Fruit, this is an unfamiliar Dao Fruit to all, its true capabilities unknown to anyone!

"Quite lively here," a rather familiar voice suddenly rang out beside Lu Yang.

Lu Yang turned to see, it was Jiang Pingan, disguised as an old farmer, nondescript at all, so he quickly paid his respects, "So it’s the elder Jiang Pingan, what brings you here?"

Jiang Pingan looked at Lu Yang intently. Nonsense, such a commotion on my turf, almost piercing through the world, could I not come?

Do you really think you own the whole world just because you’re protected by Daoist Yun Zhi and Daoist Immortal?

And without my permission, how could Yun Zhi possibly use the Power of National Fate so freely? Do you think my Power of National Fate is for everyone to borrow?

To prevent Yao Saint from escaping, Jiang Pingan had Yun Zhi mobilize the entire Power of National Fate of Great Xia.

Lu Yang thought for a moment; it seemed he had never seen Jiang Pingan make a move. "Now that you’re here, why don’t you demonstrate some of your skills?"

Jiang Pingan gave Lu Yang an unimpressed look. This young man never had good intentions. He had arrived during the battle between Fairy Eternity and Yao Saint, a battle he certainly was not qualified to join.

"Let’s not. My old bones can’t stand such disturbance, and my Dao Fruit isn’t suited for battle."

"Daoist Yun Zhi asked me to come to your side, to keep an eye on the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord, to prevent him from causing trouble."

Yun Zhi knew it would be difficult for Lu Yang alone to watch over the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord, so she called for help.

Jiang Pingan, smiling, looked at the Mid-heaven Emperor Lord, "But it seems he’s quite docile."

"Daoist Mo Qingshui has established the Great Qian Dynasty atop the ruins, I’ve long heard of your name, and you should have heard of me. This is, after all, our first meeting."

"Scram!"

The Mid-heaven Emperor Lord cursed rudely, although there wasn’t any actual grievance between them. Yet, both being Founding Emperors, one captured and the other exalted, this contrast made him loathe the sight of Jiang Pingan.

Jiang Pingan wasn’t a petty man; after being cursed, he still spoke politely, "Daoist Mo, why be so hasty? I heard you’ve been seeking the Four Ancient Immortals, even threatening to dispose of them if they don’t cooperate, to replace them with your four Divine Monarchs. Today, three of the Four Ancient Immortals are gathered. Aren’t you going to meet them, or should I call them down for you?"

The Mid-heaven Emperor Lord went silent for a moment, then gave a bowed greeting, "Daoist Jiang Pingan, who swept across the land to establish Great Xia, forging extraordinary merits, I’ve long admired your name."
